  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图新视角合成需要鲁棒重建遮挡区域并保持高推理效率。现有结合前馈3DGS与扩散模型的混合解耦框架在大视角偏差下存在"容量竞争"：固定数量的高斯点被迫从可见区域转向新出现的遮挡区域，导致原场景保真度下降。

Method: Spackle采用三阶段流程：(1) 从给定视角预测基础3DGS属性；(2) 自动识别重建不佳的区域；(3) 仅针对这些区域学习残差3DGS。推理时结合基础与增强高斯点进行新视角合成。

Result: 大量实验表明，Spackle在大视角偏差场景下取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Spackle以轻量残差学习方式有效缓解容量竞争，在不牺牲效率的前提下提升大视角偏差的新视角合成质量。

Abstract: Single-image novel view synthesis (NVS) enables photorealistic rendering of un- observed viewpoints from a single input. Practical NVS systems require two key capabilities: robust reconstruction of occluded regions and high inference effi- ciency. While hybrid decoupled frameworks combining feedforward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and diffusion models show promise for large-view-deviation NVS, they suffer from capacity competition: a fixed number of Gaussians forces resource shifts from visible to newly disoccluded areas, degrading original scene fidelity when the target view deviates significantly from the input. To address this, we propose Spackle, a lightweight residual learning framework that mit- igates capacity competition without sacrificing efficiency. Spackle operates in three stages: predicting base 3DGS attributes from given views, automatically identifying poorly reconstructed regions, and learning a residual 3DGS optimized exclusively for these areas. At inference, we combine the baseline and aug- mented Gaussians for NVS. We conduct comprehensive experiments and show that Spackle achieves state-of-the-art performance on large-view-deviation cases.

</details>


### [37] [OneWorld: Learning Consistent Physics Across Actions in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30946)
*Ke He,Yichen Ding,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OneWorld，通过共享潜在物理机制联合建模多条动作条件下的反事实未来，提升跨干预物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型对同一初始场景独立生成的多条未来虽各自看似合理，却可能隐含互不兼容的物理属性（如摩擦、质量），导致跨干预预测矛盾，削弱规划与决策的可靠性。

Method: 提出共享机制反事实生成框架OneWorld：物理机制解释器先从每条动作-结果分支推断潜机制分布，再聚合为共享世界证据（兼顾信息量低分支的不确定性），用以约束流匹配训练并引导采样，实现机制一致同时保留不同动作的差异结果；并引入多干预评估协议（沿用ACWM-Phys交互设置）。

Result: 在受控环境中，OneWorld在保持有竞争力的单次rollout预测质量的同时，提升了跨干预的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 通过共享潜物理机制可有效缓解多未来间的物理不一致问题，为可靠的世界模型规划与决策提供新思路。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models aim to predict scene evolution under different actions, a capability that is essential for reliable planning, decision-making, and interaction in dynamic environments. However, futures generated independently from the same initial scene may each appear plausible while implying incompatible physical properties, such as friction or mass. This inconsistency can lead to contradictory predictions across interventions, making it difficult for the model to maintain a coherent understanding of the underlying world and limiting its reliability for planning and decision-making. To address these issues, we propose OneWorld, a shared-mechanism counterfactual generation framework that jointly models multiple action-conditioned futures under a common latent physical mechanism. A physical mechanism interpreter first infers a distribution over latent mechanisms from each action-outcome branch. These distributions are then aggregated into shared-world evidence, which captures whether the branches admit a common physical explanation while accounting for uncertainty in less informative branches. This evidence constrains flow training and guides sampling, encouraging consistency in the underlying physical mechanism while preserving the distinct outcomes induced by different actions. We further introduce a multi-intervention evaluation protocol in controlled environments, following the interaction settings of ACWM-Phys, to assess whether generated futures can be jointly explained by the same physical parameters, alongside standard measures of single-rollout prediction quality. Experiments in these environments show that OneWorld improves cross-intervention physical consistency while maintaining competitive single-rollout prediction quality.

</details>


### [38] [DAPEVO: Deep Adaptive Patch Frame-Event Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30947)
*Luca Gandolfi,Simone Nascivera,Roberto Pellerito,Rong Zou,Chiara Plizzari,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAPEVO是一种学习型视觉里程计系统，在共享patch位置独立估计图像与事件对应，融合两种模态的相关证据后进行运动细化，实现RGB退化时仍稳健的轨迹估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB视觉里程计在GPS拒止环境下易受运动模糊、光照挑战和掉帧影响；事件相机虽具高时间分辨率和动态范围，但其异步测量使可靠对应估计困难。本文旨在融合两者优势并提升鲁棒性。

Method: 每个被跟踪patch维护图像和事件描述子；对每个patch-frame边，用学习到的标量门融合模态特定的相关嵌入，再送入共享的循环细化与BA更新。系统支持纯事件观测以在RGB稀疏/缺失时继续跟踪，并采用模态感知的关键帧剔除以保留稀缺帧约束。

Result: UZH-FPV上仅保留1/6 RGB帧时，DAPEVO的ATE仅从1.00升到1.36m（+36%），而DPVO和RAMP-VO分别上升3.7倍和3.1倍。TartanEvent上3Hz RGB输入时DAPEVO ATE低于1m，DPVO与RAMP-VO超过9m；退化RGB下DAPEVO为0.60m，优于DPVO、RAMP-VO（>4m）及纯事件DEVO（0.87m）。

Conclusion: 通过融合图像与事件对应证据并支持纯事件跟踪，DAPEVO在RGB帧稀疏或退化条件下显著优于现有RGB和事件基线方法。

Abstract: Visual odometry is essential for autonomous navigation in GPS-denied environments, yet RGB-based methods remain vulnerable to motion blur, challenging illumination, and dropped frames. Event cameras complement conventional cameras with high temporal resolution and dynamic range, but their asynchronous measurements complicate reliable correspondence estimation. We present DAPEVO, a learned visual odometry system that estimates image and event correspondences independently at shared patch locations and fuses their correlation evidence before motion refinement. Each tracked patch maintains image and event descriptors, and a learned scalar gate combines modality-specific correlation embeddings for each patch--frame edge before a shared recurrent refinement and bundle-adjustment update. DAPEVO also supports event-only observations, enabling continued tracking when RGB frames are sparse or unavailable, while modality-aware keyframe culling preserves scarce frame constraints. On UZH-FPV, when retaining only one in six RGB frames, DAPEVO's mean absolute trajectory error (ATE) increases by only 36%, from 1.00 to 1.36m, whereas the ATE of DPVO and RAMP-VO rises by factors of $3.7\times$ and $3.1\times$, respectively. On TartanEvent, DAPEVO similarly remains below 1m ATE at 3Hz RGB input, while DPVO and RAMP-VO exceed 9m. Under degraded RGB input on TartanEvent, DAPEVO achieves an ATE of 0.60m, compared with more than 4m for both DPVO and RAMP-VO, while also outperforming event-only DEVO at 0.87m.

</details>


### [39] [MVVBench: Benchmarking 4D Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30952)
*Hyungjin Chung,Byeongjun Park,Joonseok Lee,Hojun Kim,Jaeho Choi,Byung-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVVBench多视角视频推理基准，要求跨视角、跨时间联合推理，并分析VLM失败模式及推理时与训练时的改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角视频理解需要整合多个非重叠相机流中的空间与时间证据，但现有模型缺乏对4D连续性的联合推理能力，缺少严格评测基准。

Method: 基于真实多相机数据集构建MVVBench，问题设计为"单视角歧义"且多数为"单时刻歧义"，仅跨视角跨时间联合可解；涵盖六类能力（隐式/显式属性识别、隐式/显式相对距离、相对相机位姿、组合计数），采用人工撰写QA并严格验证。此外研究推理时激发策略（任务特定CoT脚手架、结构化跨视角证据聚合）及可验证奖励的强化学习。

Result: VLM失败主要源于时间错定位、跨视角身份断裂和脆弱的多跳推理；推理时策略无需重训练即获显著提升，RLVR初步显示可激发基座模型的部分潜在多视角能力。

Conclusion: MVVBench为4D多视角推理提供严格评测，训练时方法是有前景的未来方向，为可靠的具身感知奠定基础。

Abstract: Multi-view video understanding requires integrating spatial and temporal evidence across multiple, often non-overlapping camera streams: tracking entities as they transition between viewpoints, aligning events across time, and reasoning about latent 4D continuity rather than any single visible frame. We introduce MVVBench, a benchmark for multi-view video reasoning built from real world multi camera datasets. Questions are curated to be monocular-ambiguous along both the view and the temporal axis: each question is unanswerable from any single view in the designated input set, and the majority are further unanswerable from any single moment. Each question becomes uniquely solvable only by jointly reasoning across views and across time. MVVBench spans diverse dynamic scenes and probes six capabilities: implicit/explicit attribute identification, implicit/explicit relative distance, relative camera pose, and compositional counting, with human-authored QA and rigorous verification. Beyond benchmarking, we provide an extensive analysis of when and why current vision language models succeed or fail, characterizing errors due to temporal mis-localization, cross-view identity breaks, and brittle multi-hop reasoning. We then study inference-time elicitation strategies that unlock latent multi-view competence---task-specific chain-of-thought scaffolds and structured cross-view evidence aggregation---yielding substantial gains without retraining. Finally, we present preliminary evidence that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can elicit some latent multi-view competence in the base model, pointing to training-time approaches as a promising direction for future work. Together, MVVBench offers a rigorous evaluation of 4D multi-view reasoning and a foundation for future progress toward reliable embodied perception.

</details>


### [40] [IDM-Net: A Lightweight Illumination-Decoupled Modulation Network for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30962)
*Cheng-Yen Hsiao,Jing-Ming Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IDM-Net，一种轻量级光照解耦调制网络，通过双编码器分别提取RGB结构特征和亮度(Y)通道光照先验，并用光照引导调制(IGM)将多尺度光照线索注入解码器，实现亮度恢复与颜色保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级LLIE模型难以同时优化光照恢复和颜色保真。现有色彩解耦方法虽分离亮度和色度，但仅将亮度作为增强目标优化，未充分利用其作为显式引导先验的潜力。

Method: IDM-Net采用双编码器：结构编码器从RGB提取多尺度外观特征，轻量光照编码器从解耦Y通道学习光照先验。引入IGM模块通过空间自适应仿射调制将多尺度光照线索注入解码器；并设计轻量特征细化块(FRB)逐步抑制退化伪影、恢复细节。

Result: 在多个标准低光图像增强基准上的大量实验表明，IDM-Net在轻量级LLIE方法中取得有竞争力性能，在恢复质量与计算效率间保持良好平衡。

Conclusion: IDM-Net有效挖掘亮度通道作为显式引导先验的潜力，通过光照引导调制实现亮度与颜色协同优化，为轻量级低光增强提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) remains challenging for lightweight models because illumination restoration and color fidelity are difficult to optimize simultaneously in the RGB color space. Although recent color-decoupled methods separate luminance and chrominance representations, they primarily optimize luminance as an enhancement target, leaving its potential as an explicit guidance prior largely unexplored during feature reconstruction. To address this limitation, we propose IDM-Net, a lightweight Illumination-Decoupled Modulation Network for low-light image enhancement. IDM-Net adopts a dual-encoder architecture consisting of a structure encoder that extracts multi-scale appearance features from the RGB image and a lightweight illumination encoder that learns illumination priors from the decoupled luminance (Y) channel. To effectively exploit these priors, we introduce an Illumination-Guided Modulation (IGM) module that injects multi-scale illumination cues into the decoder through spatially adaptive affine modulation, enabling accurate brightness restoration while preserving natural color consistency. Furthermore, we design a lightweight Feature Refinement Block (FRB) to progressively suppress degradation artifacts and recover fine-grained image details during reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple standard low-light image enhancement benchmarks demonstrate that IDM-Net achieves competitive performance among lightweight LLIE methods while maintaining an excellent balance between restoration quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [41] [Where and When to Force: Routed Forcing for Streaming Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30963)
*Zihan Su,Siwen Lu,Junhao Zhuang,Zeyue Xue,Haoyang Huang,Guanghao Li,Xiaofeng Tan,Chun Yuan,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Routed Forcing，通过按语义区域和噪声阶段路由蒸馏目标，缓解Self Forcing中DMD导致的动态与多样性坍缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动流式虚拟人生成需实时合成语音同步且动态多样的视频。Self Forcing用DMD将双向视频扩散模型蒸馏为因果少步生成器，但DMD最小化反向KL散度，本质是模式搜索的，会导致学生模型丢弃高动态模式、坍缩到静态输出，压缩了动态与多样性。

Method: 观察到坍缩具有区域异质性：姿态手势相关的人体区域多样性损失最大，音频驱动的嘴部区域损失小，背景几乎稳定。据此提出Routed Forcing：(1) 位置路由——对坍缩最严重的人体区域用Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD)以真实视频监督学生，对嘴部和背景保留DMD以维持唇同步和场景稳定；(2) 噪声阶段路由——在高噪声阶段激活DFD注入多样动态运动模式，低噪声阶段用DMD细化细节，避免真实视频与学生视频空间差异带来的模糊和伪影。

Result: 相比Self Forcing，动态性最高提升45%，多样性提升7-25%，同时保持视频质量和唇同步。

Conclusion: 按语义区域与噪声阶段路由蒸馏目标，可有效缓解DMD的模式坍缩，在保持视觉质量和唇同步的同时显著提升动态与多样性。

Abstract: Audio-driven streaming avatar generation requires real-time synthesis of speech-synchronized videos with dynamic and diverse motion. Self Forcing uses Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) to distill bidirectional video diffusion models into causal, few-step generators for real-time streaming. However, DMD minimizes a reverse KL divergence, which is inherently mode-seeking: it causes the student to discard high-dynamic modes and collapse onto static outputs, compressing both dynamics and diversity of generated videos. We find that this collapse is region-heterogeneous: person regions involving pose and gesture variations suffer the largest diversity loss, the audio-driven mouth region shows a small loss, and the background remains nearly stable. Based on this observation, we propose Routed Forcing, which routes the distillation objective by semantic region and noise stage to improve dynamics and diversity while preserving visual quality. Specifically, (1) Where to Force: Semantic-Region Routing applies Data-Forcing Distillation (DFD), which supervises the student with real videos, to the person region where diversity collapse is most severe, while retaining DMD for the mouth and background to preserve lip synchronization and scene stability. (2) When to Force: Noise-Stage Routing activates DFD at high noise stages, where real video serves as effective supervision to inject diverse and dynamic motion patterns. At low noise stages, DMD is used to refine details, avoiding blur and artifacts from spatial differences between real video and student-generated video. Experiments show that Routed Forcing improves dynamics by up to 45% and diversity by 7-25% over Self Forcing, while preserving video quality and lip synchronization.

</details>


### [42] [CCRV-Bench: Constraint-Based Evaluation of Causal Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30979)
*Linyuan Gao,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CCRV-Bench，一个约束驱动的单图物理场景视觉因果推理基准，通过正交框架与多种约束减少捷径线索，揭示多模态模型在因果推理上的真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉因果推理评测难以区分模型是基于视觉证据进行因果推理，还是依赖统计相关性走捷径，从而可能高估其真实能力，缺少可靠的评测手段。

Method: 构建正交框架，评估四个因果任务维度：因果关系发现、状态预测、因果诊断与干预；引入实体符号化、空间接地、事实对抗约束与极简输出约束，在保留物理常识的同时减少捷径线索；对 15 个多模态模型进行实验。

Result: 约束敏感性因任务和模型而异：干预任务的平均有效退化最大；空间接地约束平均破坏性最强；事实对抗约束提升了所有评估模型的 DCR。无约束性能无法决定受约束鲁棒性，单一聚合分数会掩盖因果识别、空间接地与约束合规表达等不同失败。

Conclusion: CCRV-Bench 为受控约束下的图像接地因果推理提供了标准化诊断框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in visual tasks, but their visual causal reasoning capabilities still lack reliable evaluation. Existing evaluations struggle to distinguish whether a model is performing causal reasoning based on visual evidence or relying on statistical correlations for shortcut learning, thereby potentially overestimating their actual capabilities. This paper proposes CCRV-Bench, a constraint-driven visual causal reasoning benchmark for single-image physical scenarios. We construct an orthogonal framework that evaluates four causal task dimensions: causal relation discovery, state prediction, causal diagnosis, and intervention. We further introduce entity symbolization, spatial grounding, the factual adversarial constraint, and minimalist output constraints to reduce shortcut cues while preserving the physical commonsense required by the task. Experiments across 15 multimodal models show that constraint sensitivity is task- and model-dependent: intervention has the largest average effective degradation among the four causal tasks, spatial grounding is the most damaging constraint on average, and the factual adversarial constraint improves DCR for all evaluated models. These results show that unconstrained performance does not determine constrained robustness and that a single aggregate score can obscure distinct failures in causal identification, spatial grounding, and constraint-compliant expression. CCRV-Bench provides a standardized framework for diagnosing image-grounded causal reasoning under controlled constraints. The code is available at https://github.com/0815linyuan/CCRV-Bench-Constraint-Based-Evaluation-of-Causal-Reasoning-in-Vision-Language-Models

</details>


### [43] [STORM-Bench: Evaluating Online Video QA under Evolving and Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30981)
*Siru Zhong,Shenghan Tan,Rihong Yan,Xiaohui Lv,Yuzheng Zhuang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Haohuan Fu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STORM-Bench基准，评估在线视频问答中的状态追踪与不确定性弃答能力；14个视频LLM在线准确率最高60.3%，但联合可靠性指标STORM-BR仅5.7%–35.6%（均值18.8%），暴露严重过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视频问答需在演化且不完整的证据下追踪状态转移，并在视觉证据不足时选择性弃答。现有基准多关注静态识别或长程检索，很少联合评估这两种能力。

Method: 构建STORM-Bench，含5,736个问题、630个变化密集的短片段，覆盖5个自我中心领域（STORM-Real）和2个受控仿真子集（STORM-Sim），1 FPS。问题按累积变化强度（低/中/高）与查询时可答性（Known/Uncertain）分层。提出STORM-BR调和指标衡量答案-状态联合正确性，及STORM-BR-ATTR做不确定性归因。

Result: 14个视频LLM在线准确率最高60.3%（均值51.7%），但STORM-BR仅5.7%–35.6%（均值18.8%），主要源于在不确定查询上的普遍过度自信。

Conclusion: STORM-Bench表明，任务准确率会掩盖认知可靠性与状态追踪方面的缺陷，需引入联合可靠性指标进行评测。

Abstract: Reliable online video question answering requires tracking state transitions while selectively abstaining when visual evidence is insufficient. Existing benchmarks focus on static recognition or long-range retrieval, rarely evaluating these coupled capabilities under evolving and incomplete evidence. We present STORM-Bench, comprising 5,736 questions across 630 compact, change-dense episodes spanning five egocentric domains (STORM-Real) and two controlled simulation subsets (STORM-Sim) at 1 FPS. Questions are stratified by a proxy for accumulated change intensity (Low, Medium, High) and query-time answerability (Known, Uncertain). To measure reliability, we introduce STORM-BR, a harmonic metric over joint answer-status correctness that exposes abstention failures masked by aggregate accuracy, alongside STORM-BR-ATTR for uncertainty attribution. Across 14 video LLMs, online accuracy peaks at 60.3\% (mean 51.7\%), whereas STORM-BR ranges from 5.7\% to 35.6\% (mean 18.8\%), driven by pervasive overconfidence on uncertain queries. STORM-Bench shows that task accuracy masks these gaps in epistemic reliability and state tracking. Benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/siruzhong/STORM-Bench.

</details>


### [44] [FARE: Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation for Catching Bait-and-Switch Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30982)
*Kai Yao,Marc Juarez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 FARE，用于部署阶段对生成式图像模型的完整性审计，判断生成图像是否来自已认证生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 AI 图像生成器常以不透明 API 形式部署，用户无法检查模型权重或结构。供应商可能在通过治理认证后暗中换成更廉价、低质量模型，损害公众信任甚至高风险领域安全。因此需要部署时的完整性审计。

Method: 提出 FARE（Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation）。对已认证生成器采样图像进行训练以完成注册。部署后，仅凭一张生成图像即可判断其是否与注册生成器一致。FARE 使用图像生成器特有伪影作为特征，并在训练中寻找困难样本以放大这些特征，收紧接受区域并提升对认证生成器细微变化的敏感性。

Result: 在多种生成器替换场景中，包括相似模型版本和模型变体替换，FARE 能有效检测替换；在严格操作点下持续优于现有基线，并在论文评估的 exact-model 与 decision-only 攻击下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: FARE 为不透明部署环境下的生成器完整性审计提供了可行方案，可检测认证后被替换的生成器，增强部署阶段的可信性与安全性。

Abstract: Modern AI image generators are increasingly deployed as opaque APIs, where customers can query the deployed service, but cannot inspect model weights or architecture. This creates a practical challenge: a provider may pass governance certification with one generator and later silently switch to a cheaper and lower-quality one for deployment, compromising public trust or even safety in high-stakes domains. We study integrity auditing at deployment time and propose FARE (Forensic Acceptance Region Estimation). A certified generator is enrolled by training FARE on images sampled from that generator. After deployment, FARE can determine whether a generated image is consistent with the enrolled generator---using only that image. FARE's features are based on image generator-specific artifacts that have been proposed for forensic applications. FARE amplifies these features during training by finding hard samples that tighten the acceptance region and increase sensitivity to subtle changes in the certified generator. Across generator swaps, including substitutions with similar model versions and model variants, FARE is effective at detecting swaps, consistently outperforming existing baselines at strict operating points, and remains effective under the exact-model and decision-only attacks evaluated in this work.

</details>


### [45] [Self-Supervised Perceptually Interpretable Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30987)
*Zain Ul Abidin,George Dimas,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督可感知解释的单目深度估计框架PIMDE，将图像分解为感知特征图并分别估计深度后融合，在KITTI上性能相当同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督单目深度估计虽精度提升，但基于RGB表征难以解释，无法分析各感知成分对深度的影响，限制失败案例分析及安全关键场景的信任。

Method: 不直接使用RGB输入，而是将图像分解为一组感知特征图(PFMs)，每个编码特定视觉线索；各深度估计分支独立处理PFM生成PIDE，再通过显式融合策略组合，从而直接考察每个感知线索对最终深度预测的贡献。

Result: 在KITTI基准数据集上，PIMDE性能与已有自监督MDE方法相当，同时提供关于不同感知线索如何影响深度估计的额外洞察。

Conclusion: 感知分解可在不牺牲深度估计精度的情况下支持可解释性。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation (MDE) enables depth prediction from monocular images without requiring ground-truth supervision, making it attractive for large-scale and real-world applications. Despite steady improvements in accuracy, most existing methods remain difficult to interpret, as depth is inferred from RGB representations that obscure the impact of individual perceptual image components. This lack of transparency limits systematic analysis of failure cases and reduces confidence in safety-critical settings. This paper presents a self-supervised framework for perceptually interpretable monocular depth estimation (PIMDE), designed to associate depth predictions with distinct perceptual components of the input image. Rather than operating directly on RGB inputs, the proposed method decomposes each image into a set of perceptual feature maps (PFMs), each encoding a specific visual cue. Distinct depth estimation branches process these PFMs independently to produce depth estimates (PIDEs), which are subsequently combined through an explicit fusion strategy. This formulation allows us to examine directly the contribution of each perceptual cue to the final depth prediction. Experiments conducted on the KITTI benchmark dataset demonstrate that PIMDE achieves performance comparable to established self-supervised MDE methods while providing additional insight into how different perceptual cues influence depth estimation. These results indicate that perceptual decomposition can support interpretability without sacrificing depth estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [46] [PhoenixSR: Generative Heterogeneous Distillation Unleashes Efficient Models for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30988)
*Xin Di,Mingyu Shi,Yuanfei Bao,Long Peng,Yue Zhao,Jiaming Guo,Renjing Pei,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhoenixSR通过生成式异构蒸馏框架，将扩散先验以分数分布匹配方式迁移到无扩散的前馈SR网络，训练后移除所有扩散组件，保持原学生网络架构与推理成本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散SR生成先验强但计算开销大，前馈CNN/Transformer高效却难恢复真实高频细节。作者想探究能否在不引入推理时扩散组件的前提下，把扩散先验迁移给现有前馈SR网络。

Method: 提出PhoenixSR：用预训练扩散模型作为分布级监督，通过基于分数的分布匹配蒸馏先验，同时保留成对SR监督以维持重建保真度。包含异构分布自适应（HDA，适配SR域目标分数、跟踪学生分布演化并以成对监督锚定训练）与方向可靠性加权（DRW，基于残差一致性重加权，减少不稳定分布指导）。训练后移除所有扩散相关组件。

Result: 在三个SR基准和六个前馈主干（含SwinIR、HAT、Real-ESRGAN、SeeMoRe）上，感知质量一致提升，同时重建保真度基本保持。

Conclusion: 无需推理期扩散组件即可有效迁移扩散先验至异构前馈SR网络，验证了生成式异构蒸馏在保真敏感SR任务中的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Real-world image super-resolution (SR) requires recovering perceptually realistic high-resolution images from complex low-resolution observations while preserving faithful content. Diffusion-based SR benefits from strong generative priors but incurs substantial computational overhead, whereas feed-forward CNN and Transformer SR models are efficient yet often struggle to recover realistic high-frequency details. This motivates a natural question: can diffusion priors be transferred to existing diffusion-free SR networks without introducing diffusion components at inference time? To this end, we propose PhoenixSR, a generative heterogeneous distillation framework that transfers diffusion priors to independently designed feed-forward SR networks through score-based distribution matching. Rather than aligning heterogeneous features or imitating sampled diffusion outputs, PhoenixSR uses the pretrained diffusion model as distribution-level supervision, while paired SR supervision preserves reconstruction fidelity. To make distribution matching effective for fidelity-sensitive SR, we introduce Heterogeneous Distribution Adaptation, which adapts the target score to the SR domain, improves tracking of the evolving student distribution, and anchors training with paired supervision. We further employ Directional Reliability Weighting, a lightweight residual-consistency-based reweighting strategy that reduces unstable distributional guidance. All diffusion-related components are removed after training, leaving the original student architecture and inference cost unchanged. Experiments on three SR benchmarks and six feed-forward backbones, including SwinIR, HAT, Real-ESRGAN, and SeeMoRe, show consistent perceptual improvements with largely preserved reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [47] [PICO: Projection-Informed Consistency Optimisation for 6DoF Surgical Tool Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30989)
*Lucy Fothergill,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones,Duygu Sarikaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PICO：端到端多任务模型，结合分割、深度预测与位姿回归，用投影损失和点到点损失提升外科工具6DoF位姿估计的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科工具6DoF位姿估计对自动化与安全交互至关重要；但基于运动学的方法因线驱机械臂误差累积而失效，基于视觉的方法多依赖外部标记或追踪器，且两阶段方法存在误差累积与计算开销，缺乏实时鲁棒性。

Method: 提出端到端可训练模型PICO，采用多任务学习架构，同时预测分割图、深度图以及平移与旋转参数；设计两个代理任务——投影损失与点到点损失，分别在2D和3D空间强制几何一致性。

Result: 在SurgRIPE数据集上以标准6DoF位姿指标对比SOTA。四个子集上表现稳定，旋转性能排名第二（含遮挡情况），平移性能具有竞争力，尤其在遮挡场景下表现良好。

Conclusion: PICO验证了多任务学习与几何感知代理任务在外科工具位姿估计中的有效性，在遮挡等挑战场景下鲁棒可靠，具备未来应用潜力。

Abstract: Purpose: Accurate 6 DoF pose estimation of surgical tools is critical for automa- tion, robotic proprioception, and safe interaction with the tissue operated on. Kinematics-based approaches suffer from accumulated errors due to the cable- driven nature of robotic arms, while vision-based methods often rely on external markers or trackers. Although more recent vision-based advances have been pro- posed, these two-stage pose estimation methods often lack real-time robustness due to accumulated errors and computational overhead. Methods: We propose a novel end-to-end trainable model, PICO. Our model employs a multi-task learning architecture to predict segmentation and depth maps, alongside regression of translation and rotation parameters. We define two proxy tasks that enforce geometric consistency in both 2D and 3D spaces, improving accuracy and robustness. For this, we propose a projection loss, and a point-to-point loss. Results: We evaluate our method on the SurgRIPE dataset, benchmarking its performance against state-of-the-art approaches using standard 6DoF pose esti- mation metrics. Our results demonstrate consistently strong performance across all four subsets, specifically in rotation, ranking second even under occlusion. It also demonstrates comparable translational performance, remaining competitive, especially in occluded cases. Conclusion: PICO demonstrates the effectiveness of multi-task learning and geometry-aware proxy tasks for robust and reliable surgical tool pose estimation, especially in occluded scenarios, highlighting potential for future applications.

</details>


### [48] [FLIP: Final Layer Inference-Time Probing for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30993)
*Drandreb Earl O. Juanico,Rowel O. Atienza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLIP，一种在开放权重视觉语言模型(VLM)上验证logit前干预位点是否支持任务相关结构化计算的后置探针，通过对最终归一化隐状态做逐元素下限截断进行扫描，识别出三类响应区域，并给出四准则协议以区分真实机制性效应与泛化扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部干预导致的行为变化在机制上含义模糊：可能源于对视觉证据的更好利用，也可能是输出不稳定或模型退化。需要一种严格方法来判断某个面向logit的干预位点是否承载结构化、任务相关的计算，而非泛化扰动。

Method: FLIP在计算logit前对最终归一化隐状态施加逐元素flooring，不改变参数、提示与解码。在受控检测/计数任务上扫描干预强度，并形式化提出四准则的探针-扫描协议：区域结构、grounding代理对齐、特征一致性依赖、以及在基于性能的负对照上无法复现同一正效应区域。

Result: 扫描显示三个区域：几乎无变化、有限内部区域(检测召回R50提升同时容错计数误差降低)、过度抑制。后归一化状态满足完整协议；原始解码层干预(包括最终归一化前的最后一层输出)与单例左右对照均不能复现该签名，而同点位算子与多个VLM可复现。

Conclusion: FLIP是干预式机制可解释性的验证步骤，而非一种引导(steering)方法，用于判别干预效果是否具有机制意义。

Abstract: We present FLIP, a final-layer inference-time probe for testing whether a logit-facing intervention site in an open-weight vision-language model (VLM) supports structured, task-linked computation rather than generic perturbation. Behavioral change under internal intervention is otherwise mechanistically ambiguous: it may reflect improved use of visual evidence, generic output instability, or outright degradation. FLIP applies elementwise flooring to the final normalized hidden state before logit computation, leaving parameters, prompts, and decoding unchanged. On a controlled detection/counting probe, sweeping intervention strength reveals three regions: negligible change, a bounded interior regime in which detection recall at IoU 0.50 ($R_{50}$) improves while tolerant counting error ($\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{count}}$) falls, and over-suppression. We formalize a four-criterion probe-and-sweep protocol for disciplining the interpretation of intervention effects: regime structure, grounding-proxy alignment, feature-coherence dependence, and failure to reproduce the same positive regime on a performance-based negative control. The post-normalization state passed to the output head is the logit-facing instantiation of this test; under a non-targeted flooring sweep it satisfies the full protocol. Raw decoder-layer interventions, including the last-block output before final normalization, and the singleton-pair left/right control fail to reproduce the Final-site signature, while same-site operators and multiple VLMs replicate it. FLIP is therefore a validation step for intervention-based mechanistic interpretability, not a steering method.

</details>


### [49] [TRACKGRAPH: Online Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs via Image-Space Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31005)
*Peder Borge Hellesylt,Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Kostas Alexis,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACKGRAPH 是一种在线开放词汇 3D 建图系统，先在图像流中维持短期 2D 掩码身份再融合到 3D，实现更快、更省显存的场景图构建与开放词汇检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通常对每帧图像分割、将检测与持久 3D 段关联，并频繁进行昂贵的视觉-语言推理，效率低。

Method: 在稀疏关键帧上用 FastSAM 掩码与 CLIP 特征，帧间用密集 DINOv3 特征高速传播掩码以维持 2D 身份；跟踪掩码融合进层次场景图中的类无关 3D 段层，3D 关联处理跟踪中断与重访；用紧凑多视图 CLIP 嵌入做开放词汇检索。

Result: 在 Replica、ScanNet++、HM3D 上开放词汇分割与检索与 SOTA 相当，Replica 同义词频率最高（0.50）；相比 ViT-H OVI-MAP 在 A100 上快 1.7x、显存少 3.3x；四足机器人上以 7.5Hz 在线建图与物体搜索，并用无人机数据测试空中视角。

Conclusion: 通过在 2D 图像流中先跟踪再融合到 3D，TRACKGRAPH 兼顾了开放词汇建图的性能与效率，并验证了真实机器人平台上的在线部署能力。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D maps enable robots to reason about previously unknown environments using natural language. However, existing systems typically segment every incoming image, associate detections with persistent 3D segments, and frequently perform costly Vision-Language (VL) inference. We present TRACKGRAPH, an online open-vocabulary system that maintains short-term 2D mask identity directly in the image stream before fusing segments into 3D. FastSAM masks and CLIP features are computed at sparse keyframes, while dense DINOv3 features are used to propagate masks at a high rate in between. The resulting tracked masks are fused into a class-agnostic 3D segment layer within a hierarchical scene graph, with 3D association handling tracking interruptions and long-term revisits. Compact multi-view CLIP embeddings enable open-vocabulary retrieval. Across Replica, ScanNet++, and HM3D, TRACKGRAPH achieves competitive open-vocabulary segmentation and retrieval against state-of-the-art mapping methods, including the highest synonym frequency on Replica (0.50). On the same NVIDIA A100, it is 1.7x faster and uses 3.3x less GPU memory than ViT-H OVI-MAP. Real-world quadruped deployments demonstrate onboard scene graph construction and object search at 7.5Hz, while recorded drone data is used to test the method under aerial viewpoints.

</details>


### [50] [Refining Cytology Predictions with Conditional Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31028)
*Manon Dausort,Tiffanie Godelaine,Karim El Khoury,Maxime Zanella,Christophe De Vleeschouwer,Benoît Macq*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CytoCRF，通过针对细胞学染色和染色质调整成对项并融合多骨干网络邻域，显著提升细胞学图像零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在组织学图像上零样本分类表现优异，但在细胞学图像上因染色和细胞形态差异大而表现不佳；现有CRF框架为组织病理学设计，无法迁移到跨多种染色方案的细胞学数据集。

Method: 提出CytoCRF，将CRF成对项针对染色质和细胞学特有染色进行调整，并通过融合多个骨干网络来丰富每个势项的邻域。

Result: 在十个细胞学数据集上，CytoCRF在每个标注预算下均优于现有CRF框架，仅用50个标注即超过最佳基线13.6个百分点，超过零样本33.7个百分点；融合多骨干网络带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 邻域拓扑结构比在其上计算的成对势项更重要，CytoCRF有效适应细胞学场景并显著提升分类性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong zero-shot (ZS) classification on histology images but do not perform as well on cytology, whose stains and cell morphology differ markedly compared to histology. Conditional random fields (CRFs) can refine noisy VLM predictions by propagating information across patches, but existing CRF frameworks were designed for histopathology and do not transfer to cytology datasets, released as independent patch pools spanning multiple staining protocols. We introduce CytoCRF, which adapts the pairwise terms to cytology by targeting chromatin and cytology-specific staining, and further enrich the neighborhood of each potential term by combining multiple backbones. Across ten cytology datasets, CytoCRF outperforms existing CRF frameworks at every annotation budget, reaching +13.6 percentage points over the best baseline and +33.7 over ZS with only 50 annotations. Combining information from multiple backbones brings further gains, showing that the neighborhood topology matters more than the pairwise potential computed over it.

</details>


### [51] [Exploiting Spatial Structure for Transductive Few-Shot Classification of Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31040)
*Tiffanie Godelaine,Manon Dausort,Karim El Khoury,Benoît Gérin,Benoît Macq,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SlideTIM，将转导方法LC-TIM适配到全切片图像（WSI），通过空间-潜在正则项和类别分布先验，提升病理WSI零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像分析在癌症诊断中价值高，但视觉语言模型的零样本预测在细粒度任务上噪声大，需要进一步精炼；现有转导方法大多未针对WSI设计。

Method: 将LC-TIM适配到WSI，引入结合的空间-潜在正则项（使空间和语义相近的patch获得相同预测）以及patch类别分布先验（校准预测类别比例），联合精炼所有预测。

Result: 在四个组织学数据集上，SlideTIM一致优于所有TIM变体；1 shot时macro-F1比最佳竞争基线提升+8.1pp，比零样本提升+19.4pp。

Conclusion: SlideTIM能有效应对WSI的复杂空间组织和强类别不平衡问题，显著提升分类性能。

Abstract: Automating the analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs), a key step in cancer diagnosis, has high clinical value, as it can reduce pathologist's workload while improving diagnosis accuracy. Recently, vision-language models have shown promising performance for patch-level classification without requiring any annotation, yet these zero-shot (ZS) predictions remain noisy on fine-grained tasks and must be further refined. A promising direction is to refine all predictions jointly, i.e., a transductive approach. However, most existing methods are not tailored to WSIs. We thus propose SlideTIM, an adaptation to WSIs of the recent transductive approach LC-TIM, which introduces a combined spatial--latent regularizer together with a prior on the patch class distribution. The former enforces spatially and semantically close patches to receive the same predictions, while the prior calibrates the predicted class proportions. Together, they address the complex spatial organization and the strong class imbalance of WSIs. Evaluated on four histology datasets, SlideTIM consistently outperforms all TIM variants, improving the macro-F1 by +8.1pp over the best competing baseline at 1 shot. Compared to the ZS, it raises the macro-F1 by +19.4pp at 1 shot. The code will be made available after submission.

</details>


### [52] [Where Compute Matters: Heterogeneous Attention for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31050)
*Olga Zatsarynna,Denis Korzhenkov,Juergen Gall,Amir Habibian,Mohsen Ghafoorian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HetA-DiT 提出一种异构注意力机制，根据 token 去噪难度自适应分配计算，仅约 20% token 走稠密全局注意力，在保持生成质量的同时提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成中自注意力对长时空 token 序列成本呈二次增长。现有高效注意力方法对所有 token 使用相同计算模式，但不同视频区域及生成过程的去噪难度差异很大。

Method: 引入轻量不确定性分支预测 token 级去噪难度，将不确定 token 路由到稠密全局注意力，可靠 token 用高效局部注意力；路由内容与时步自适应，提供单一参数控制质量-效率权衡。兼容少步分布匹配蒸馏，通过复用前一步不确定性估计避免额外 Transformer 评估。

Result: 在 DMD 蒸馏的 Wan2.2-5B 与 Wan2.1-1.3B 上评估，VBench、VBench-2.0 及人类偏好评估显示，仅约 20% token 走稠密注意力即可保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: 异构、自适应分配注意力计算能有效降低视频生成成本，同时维持高质量生成，且与蒸馏方法兼容、推理无额外开销。

Abstract: Efficient video generation requires reducing the quadratic cost of self-attention over long spatio-temporal token sequences. Existing efficient-attention methods typically apply the same computation pattern to every token, even though denoising difficulty varies substantially across video regions and evolves throughout the generation process. We introduce HetA-DiT, a heterogeneous attention mechanism that adaptively allocates computation according to token difficulty. A lightweight uncertainty branch predicts a token-wise estimate of denoising difficulty, which is used to route uncertain tokens through dense global attention while processing more reliable tokens with efficient local attention. The resulting routing is content- and timestep-adaptive, retains global context where it matters most, and provides a single parameter for controlling the quality-efficiency trade-off. HetA-DiT is compatible with few-step distribution-matching distillation and introduces no additional Transformer evaluation at inference time by reusing uncertainty estimates from the preceding denoising step. We evaluate the method on DMD-distilled Wan2.2-5B and Wan2.1-1.3B models. Across VBench, VBench-2.0, and human preference evaluation, HetA-DiT maintains competitive generation quality while routing only approximately 20% of tokens through dense attention.

</details>


### [53] [Band-Selection Stability and Semantic Segmentation Performance: A Study on Hyperspectral City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31074)
*Jiarong Li,Imad Ali Shah,Enda Ward,Martin Glavin,Edward Jones,Brian Deegan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估6种波段选择方法在10个独立采样ROI集上的稳定性与分割性能，发现稳定性与方法相关但不预测下游分割效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像受资源约束，需用波段选择方法；但其对采样数据的敏感性及与语义分割模型的关系尚不明确。

Method: 在Hyperspectral City V2(128波段)的10个类别均衡ROI集上评估6种波段选择方法，取前3个ROI集的Top-K(K=3~13)波段，用3种SSM对比128波段基线。

Result: 方法内稳定性依方法而异，Sim-LP最稳定，与JMIM+CSNR分割效果最佳；Top-K分割性能有竞争力(K=9时提升2.01 mIoU、1.72 mF1,推理快18-22倍),但性能不随K单调提升,稳定性与SSM性能无一致关联。

Conclusion: 方法内稳定性有参考价值但不能可靠预测下游分割性能，需在重复采样、不同子集大小和多种SSM下评估波段选择方法。

Abstract: Resource constraints make high-dimensional hyperspectral imaging challenging in autonomous perception, motivating the use of band selection methods. However, the sensitivity of band-selection methods to sampled data and their relationship to semantic segmentation models (SSMs) remain underexplored. This study evaluates six band selection methods on ten independently sampled, class-balanced region-of-interest (ROI) sets, yielding 60 top-25 band subsets from the Hyperspectral City V2 (128 bands: 450-950nm) dataset. Top-$K$ bands ($K\in\{3,5, ... 13\}$) from the first three ROI sets are evaluated with three SSMs against the corresponding 128-band baseline. Experiments show that intra-method stability is method-dependent: Sim-LP shows the highest stability (pairwise Jaccard similarity) and, together with JMIM+CSNR, yields the best segmentation results. Top-$K$ based SSMs remain competitive with baselines, with gains of up to 2.01 mIoU and 1.72 mF1 points, and 18-22x faster CPU inference for $K=9$. However, performance does not improve monotonically with $K$, and stability shows no consistent association with SSM performance. These findings suggest that intra-method stability is informative but an unreliable indicator of downstream segmentation performance, highlighting the need to evaluate band-selection methods across repeated samples, subset sizes, and SSMs.

</details>


### [54] [DepthEvidence: Unifying Metric Depth Prediction and Geometric Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31103)
*Jiangning Wei,Yuan Yao,Miaomiao Cui,Mingsheng Li,Humen Zhong,Shuai Bai,Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DepthEvidence模型（4B参数），利用自身预测的稠密度量深度作为物体锚定证据，实现空间度量推理与语言生成的结合，并构建Depth-VQA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理需将物体关联到几何测量并在语言推理中保留数值内容，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 相机条件解码器结合多尺度特征与高分辨率RGB预测全分辨率度量深度；稠密到语言接口将深度和特征转为物体对齐的连续几何token；几何监督保证度量信息在语言交互前后可恢复；指令微调支持物体测量与组合推理。

Result: 在九个数据集上取得最高平均稠密δ1，与专用估计器相当；在实例级度量深度估计及相对/度量推理轨道上领先；基本保持通用VQA性能并提升空间理解。

Conclusion: DepthEvidence有效统一稠密度量预测与语言推理，验证了以深度作为物体锚定证据的可行性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning with metric constraints requires linking objects to geometric measurements and preserving their numerical content during language reasoning. We present DepthEvidence, a 4B model that uses its own dense metric predictions as object-grounded evidence for language generation. A camera-conditioned decoder predicts full-resolution metric depth using multi-scale visual features and high-resolution RGB refinement. A dense-to-language interface converts predicted depths and decoder features into object-aligned continuous geometry tokens anchored to object identifiers. Geometric supervision encourages metric information to remain recoverable before and after language-context interaction, while instruction tuning supports object measurement and compositional reasoning. We introduce a Depth-VQA benchmark evaluating object-depth queries, relative comparisons, and decisions combining spatial and numerical constraints. Across nine datasets, DepthEvidence achieves the highest average dense $δ_1$ among evaluated methods, competitive with specialized estimators. It also leads the evaluated methods in instance-level metric depth estimation and overall accuracy on both relative and metric reasoning tracks, while broadly preserving general VQA performance and improving spatial understanding relative to the base model.

</details>


### [55] [Double-stream registration with pyramid fusion for HDR video with alternating exposures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31108)
*Onofre Martorell,Ivan Pereira-Sánchez,Antoni Fuentes,Antoni Buades*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于双流配准与精确金字塔融合的HDR视频重建框架，从交替曝光序列中恢复HDR，实验优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替曝光序列的HDR视频重建在极端亮度变化区域仍具挑战性。

Method: 输入三帧连续图像；用光流直接与中心帧配准，并引入互补的中点位移策略应对严重过曝；随后通过金字塔融合阶段将辐射图像与LDR图像融合为最终HDR输出。

Result: 实验结果一致优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 双流配准与精确金字塔融合有效提升了极端亮度变化下的HDR视频重建性能。

Abstract: High dynamic range (HDR) video reconstruction from al\-ter\-na\-ting-exposure sequences remains challenging, especially in regions with extreme luminance variation. We propose a novel HDR reconstruction framework based on dual-stream registration and accurate pyramid fusion. Given three consecutive frames, our method computes optical flow directly with the central frame, while introducing a complementary midpoint displacement strategy to handle cases with severe overexposition. A pyramid fusion stage then merges the resulting radiance and LDR images into a final HDR output. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [56] [Seeing Semantic Shift: Difference-Aware Sentence-Level Temporal Segmentation of Sign Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31148)
*Bowen Guo,Shiwei Gan,Yafeng Yin,Xiao Liu,Kuizhuang Liu,Zhiwei Jiang,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SignShift，一个差异感知的句子级手语分割框架，通过建模帧间特征变化来检测连续手语视频中的句子边界，无需字幕辅助，性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解方法在短视频上表现优异，但在长的连续手语视频上性能急剧下降。连续手语中句子转换往往平滑且视觉上模糊，缺乏明确停顿或姿态重置，导致静态帧表示难以捕捉指示边界的细微时序变化。

Method: 聚焦Visual-only Sentence-level Sign Language Segmentation（Vis-SSLS）任务，将连续手语视频划分为非重叠的句子级片段。设计Temporal Difference Module，融合全帧、面部和手部线索，通过帧间差分学习多尺度时序变化，捕捉细粒度局部运动学与全局语义转换。设计Segment Count Prediction模块，预测句子数量以引导边界选择，缓解过分割和欠分割问题。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SignShift显著优于现有方法，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SignShift通过显式建模帧间特征变化作为句子边界检测的语义线索，有效解决了连续手语视频的句子级分割难题，为下游识别与翻译任务提供了关键前提。

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language understanding have achieved impressive success on short, single-sentence videos, yet their performance drops sharply when applied to long, continuous sign language videos. To bridge this gap, we focus on a challenging and realistic setting: Visual-only Sentence-level Sign Language Segmentation (Vis-SSLS), which aims to partition continuous sign language videos into non-overlapping sentence-level segments without any caption assistance, serving as a crucial prerequisite for downstream recognition and translation tasks. However, sentence transitions in sign language are often smooth and visually ambiguous, lacking explicit pauses or posture resets. As a result, static frame representations may fail to capture the subtle temporal changes that indicate sentence boundaries. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{SignShift}, a difference-aware segmentation framework that explicitly models frame-to-frame feature variation as semantic cues for sentence boundary detection. First, to model the feature variation, we design a Temporal Difference Module, which incorporates full-frame, facial, and hand cues, and employs inter-frame differencing to learn multi-scale temporal variations that capture both fine-grained local kinematics and global semantic transitions. Second, to mitigate over- and under-segmentation issues, we design a Segment Count Prediction module, which predicts the number of sentences to guide boundary selection. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SignShift substantially outperforms existing methods, validating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [57] [FedHisto-PAST: Parameter-Efficient Stain-Aware Federated Learning for Cross-Site Lung Histopathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31150)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,M. M. Golam Hafiz,Saad Aloteibi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估 FedHisto-PAST v2 用于跨站点肺组织病理三分类（ACA/Normal/SCC），结合冻结 HIBOU-B 基础模型与参数高效适配、染色条件配对视图预测、可靠性感知原型学习和自适应联邦聚合。内部近乎饱和，外部 LungHist700 上 Macro-F1 0.7286、平衡准确率 0.7305，仅预测级一致性有明确独立贡献，更新参数仅 1.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨站点肺组织病理分类需应对染色差异、非IID客户端数据、类别缺失及大模型适配成本高的问题，现有联邦方法难以兼顾染色鲁棒性与参数效率。

Method: FedHisto-PAST v2：冻结 HIBOU-B 编码器 + 参数高效适配；染色条件配对视图预测与特征一致性；可靠性感知原型学习；自适应联邦聚合。实验为五客户端非IID、原始数据本地化模拟，含固定内部评估、客户端级分析、组件消融、通信核算及开发影响的探索性 LungHist700 队列。

Result: 所有主要方法在固定内部划分上接近天花板，判别受限；LungHist700 上 Macro-F1=0.728560，平衡准确率=0.730454；Normal/SCC 识别较高但 ACA 召回偏低，校准不完善；外部消融仅预测级一致性有明确独立贡献，特征一致性与原型正则无结论性整体增益；更新参数占 1.253841%。

Conclusion: 结果为染色感知、参数高效的联邦学习提供了探索性跨数据集证据；但未建立形式化隐私、患者级独立性、前瞻性部署或临床验证。

Abstract: Cross-site lung histopathology classification must account for stain variation, non-IID client data, missing classes, and the cost of adapting large pathology encoders. This study evaluates FedHisto-PAST v2 for three-way classification of adenocarcinoma (ACA), Normal, and squamous cell carcinoma (SCC). FedHisto-PAST v2 combines a frozen HIBOU-B foundation model with parameter-efficient adaptation, stain-conditioned paired-view prediction and feature consistency, reliability-aware prototype learning, and adaptive federated aggregation. Experiments used a five-client, non-IID, raw-data-local simulation with fixed internal evaluation, client-level analysis, component ablations, communication accounting, and a development-influenced exploratory LungHist700 cohort. All principal methods achieved near- ceiling internal performance, which limited discrimination on the fixed split. On LungHist700, FedHisto- PAST v2 achieved a Macro-F1 of 0.728560 and a balanced accuracy of 0.730454. Higher recognition of Normal and SCC was accompanied by lower ACA recall, and calibration remained imperfect. Prediction-level consistency was the only component with a clearly supported independent contribution in the external ablation analysis. Feature consistency and prototype regularization showed no conclusive independent overall gains in Macro-F1. The framework updated 1.253841% of the model parameters. The results provide exploratory cross-dataset evidence for stain-aware, parameter-efficient federation; they do not establish formal privacy, patient-level independence, prospective deployment, or clinical validation.

</details>


### [58] [HyperErase: Scale-Calibrated Hypernetwork for Multi-Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31154)
*Yi Sun,Xinhao Zhong,Zhiqi Zhang,Yimin Zhou,Junhao Li,Yuxia Qiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyperErase框架，用超网络驱动的提示条件参数合成实现概念擦除，替代静态权重范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法多采用静态权重范式，生成单一冻结适配器，难以适应多样提示变化，且扩展到多概念时存在参数干扰问题。

Method: 将概念擦除重新表述为提示条件参数摊销，训练超网络把文本描述映射为提示特定的LoRA更新；并引入解耦校正策略，将LoRA token分离为模式与尺度子空间，应用平方根变换抑制乘性过缩放，利用教师导出的规范先验进行推理时校正。

Result: 在多个主要概念类别上的实验表明，HyperErase在擦除有效性、图像质量和语义对齐的权衡上持续改进，性能可与黄金标准单概念基线相当；模型可在单次前向传播中为每个输入提示变体提供专用LoRA，无需推理时梯度更新。

Conclusion: 该框架为T2I模型中的概念擦除提供了原理性且灵活的新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have substantially improved visual synthesis, but have also raised increasing safety concerns due to their potential to generate harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods predominantly follow a static weight paradigm, producing a single frozen adapter that struggles to adapt to diverse prompt variations and suffers from parameter interference when scaling to multiple concepts. We propose \textbf{HyperErase}, a framework for concept erasure based on hypernetwork-driven prompt-conditioned parameter synthesis. Our approach first reframes concept erasure as prompt-conditioned parameter amortization and trains a hypernetwork to map textual descriptions to prompt-specific LoRA updates, eliminating the need for per-prompt gradient optimization or manual LoRA merging. To further improve the stability and precision of synthesized adapters, we develop a decoupled rectification strategy, which disentangles LoRA tokens into pattern and scale subspaces, applies a square-root transform to curb multiplicative over-scaling, and leverages teacher-derived canonical priors for inference-time correction. Extensive experiments across major concept categories demonstrate that HyperErase consistently improves the trade-off between erasure effectiveness, image quality, and semantic alignment, achieving performance comparable to gold-standard single-concept baselines. Furthermore, the resulting models can provide specialized LoRAs for each input prompt variation in a single forward pass without requiring gradient updates during inference. These principled and flexible framework offers a new paradigm for concept erasure in T2I models.

</details>


### [59] [ReG-SAM: Reference Graph-Driven SAM for 2D Foundational Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31160)
*Donghang Lyu,Zichen Zhang,Oleh Dzyubachyk,Marius Staring*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReG-SAM：基于SAM的2D血管分割框架，利用参考图集合增强血管表征，在19个数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像血管分割对诊断与治疗规划至关重要，但血管形态复杂、成像条件多样，现有深度学习方法难以构建跨解剖结构与模态的通用分割器。SAM虽有前景，但未充分利用血管形态，难以处理细粒度血管结构，性能欠佳。

Method: 提出ReG-SAM框架，利用参考图集合增强血管表征。引入两种模态感知表征：图提示嵌入（GPEs）编码图的全局空间特征，血管原型嵌入（VPEs）从多尺度特征图和血管掩码中捕捉细粒度模态特定血管特征。由于二者需推理时不可用的血管掩码，构建模态级血管数据库，并设计两种参考图引导的表征学习方案，从数据库样本而非真值掩码估计GPEs与VPEs。

Result: 在19个数据集上的大量实验表明，ReG-SAM持续优于现有基线，甚至超过使用人工提示的方法，尤其在困难细血管上表现突出。

Conclusion: ReG-SAM通过参考图引导的模态感知表征学习，实现了跨解剖与模态的通用血管分割，为SAM在细粒度血管分割中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vessel segmentation in medical images is essential for many clinical tasks, ranging from diagnosis to treatment planning. However, it remains challenging due to complex vascular morphology and diverse imaging conditions. Existing deep learning methods rarely aim at building a generalizable vessel segmentor across anatomies and modalities. While the Seg- ment Anything Model (SAM) has shown promise for med- ical image segmentation, its original design does not fully exploit vascular morphology and struggles with fine-grained vascular structures, leading to suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose ReG-SAM, a SAM-based framework tailored to 2D vessel segmentation that leverages reference graph set for enhancing vascular representations. Specifically, we introduce two modality-aware representations derived from the reference masks: graph prompt embeddings (GPEs) that encode global spatial features from graphs, and vascu- lar prototype embeddings (VPEs) that capture fine-grained modality-specific vessel characteristics from multi-scale fea- ture maps and vascular masks. Since both require vascular masks that are unavailable during inference and require robust modality-aware vascular feature representations, we construct a modality-wise vascular database and develop two reference graph-guided representation learning schemes for estimating GPEs and VPEs using samples from the database rather than ground-truth masks. Extensive experiments across 19 datasets demonstrate that ReG-SAM consistently outperforms existing baselines, even those using manual prompts, particularly on challenging thin vessels.

</details>


### [60] [TaskIR: Task-Driven Image Restoration via Degradation Adaptation and Task Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31170)
*Yanjie Tu,Qingsen Yan,Axi Niu,Wenxuan Cai,Tao Hu,Wei Dong,Haokui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TaskIR，一个两阶段任务驱动的统一图像复原框架，结合退化自适应复原与任务反馈细化，在多种退化和下游任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务驱动图像复原方法多聚焦单一退化类型，难以应对真实场景中的多样退化。不同退化对复原需求不同，复原不足会残留退化与伪影，损害物体边界和语义线索，从而降低下游任务性能。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一用退化表示模块(DRM)提取退化表示，使退化引导Transformer块(DGTB)动态调制特征变换实现自适应复原；阶段二用任务到复原反馈生成模块(TRFG)通过建模任务-表示差异生成复原反馈，再用选择性任务反馈细化模块(STFR)评估反馈相关性并选择性细化中间复原特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，TaskIR 在多种退化和任务上实现了具有竞争力的复原质量和下游任务性能。

Conclusion: TaskIR 通过退化自适应复原与任务反馈细化的统一框架，有效应对多样退化，兼顾图像质量与下游任务表现。

Abstract: Task-driven image restoration aims to improve both image quality and downstream task performance. However, existing methods predominantly focus on single degradation type and struggle to handle the diverse degradations encountered in real-world scenarios. Different degradations impose distinct restoration demands, and insufficient restoration may leave residual degradations and artifacts that impair object boundaries and semantic cues, thereby compromising downstream task performance. To address these challenges, we propose TaskIR, a two-stage task-driven unified image restoration framework that integrates degradation-adaptive restoration with task feedback refinement. In Stage I, a Degradation Representation Module (DRM) extracts degradation representations, enabling a Degradation-Guided Transformer Block (DGTB) to dynamically modulate feature transformations for adaptive restoration. In Stage II, a Task-to-Restoration Feedback Generation module (TRFG) transforms heterogeneous task features into restoration feedback by modeling task-representation discrepancies associated with the current restoration. Subsequently, a Selective Task Feedback Refinement module (STFR) assesses feedback relevance and selectively refines intermediate restoration features to mitigate interference with well-restored content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TaskIR achieves competitive restoration quality and downstream task performance across diverse degradations and tasks.

</details>


### [61] [Who Says What: Symbolic Trimodal Binding Mechanisms in Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31193)
*Jihoo Jung,Youngjoon Jang,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AVLLM在多说话人对话视频中的三模态绑定机制，发现其依赖符号化绑定，并针对音视频错位瓶颈提出无需训练的ASD提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音视频大模型在处理含多说话人对话的视频时推理困难，核心在于需解决"谁说了什么"，这依赖文本-音频-视觉三模态绑定，但该绑定如何实现尚不明确。

Method: 系统分析AVLLM中涌现的符号化三模态绑定机制：将听觉成分编码为时间话语序列符号变量、视觉成分编码为空间实体坐标符号变量。针对绑定失败主要源于音视频错位，提出利用现成ASD模型在活跃说话人上叠加边界框的音视频提示方法。

Result: 该免训练方法在四个对话类基准上立即带来性能提升；在ASD提示视频上进行不到300步的轻量微调后，提升还扩展到三个通用音视频基准。

Conclusion: 音视频错位是三模态绑定失败的主要瓶颈，基于ASD的提示方法可有效缓解，且具泛化性，为增强AVLLM对话推理提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Current Audio-Visual LLMs (AVLLMs) struggle with reasoning over videos featuring multi-speaker dialogues. In such videos, resolving "who says what" is crucial, which necessitates trimodal (text-audio-visual) binding. Motivated by these challenges, we systematically investigate how this trimodal binding is achieved in AVLLMs. Specifically, we identify emergent symbolic trimodal binding mechanisms in AVLLMs that utilize modality-specific symbolic variables. By encoding auditory and visual components into symbolic variables-capturing temporal utterance sequences and spatial entity coordinates, respectively-the model establishes cross-modal linking within this abstract space. Crucially, we reveal that when trimodal binding fails, the breakdown predominantly stems from misaligned audio-visual connections. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce an audio-visual prompting method utilizing an off-the-shelf Active Speaker Detection (ASD) model. By simply overlaying visual bounding boxes on active speakers, this training-free approach yields immediate performance gains across four conversation-centric benchmarks. Moreover, lightweight fine-tuning of fewer than 300 steps on these ASD-prompted-videos extends these gains to three general AV benchmarks, suggesting the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [62] [Light Field Primitive for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31198)
*Liang Chen,Jiahui Ning,Xun Jiang,Xing Xu,Jimmy Ren,Fenglei Fan,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出光场基元（LFP），用4D光线空间中紧凑的可微基元替代稠密光线数据库，实现实时新视角合成并统一支持多种光学效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统新视角合成依赖稠密光线数据库，缺乏结构灵活性，难以统一处理抗锯齿、去模糊、鱼眼、透明折射等光学与外观效应。

Method: 在经典双平面参数化下，将成组光线压缩为可学习基元，查询响应由光线归属度决定；渲染即合成所有响应，可直接从带位姿图像优化并用光线实时渲染。

Result: 在标准基准上性能有竞争力，且以极小渲染器改动即实现多尺度抗锯齿、散焦去模糊与重聚焦、鱼眼相机渲染及透明物体折射重建，媲美专门框架。

Conclusion: LFP的核心优势在于结构性：基元直接位于4D光线空间中，使各种光学与外观效应成为单一共享渲染器的行为，实现高效统一的新视角合成。

Abstract: We present Light Field Primitives (LFP), a formulation for novel view synthesis that replaces the dense ray database with a compact set of differentiable primitives in the classical two-plane parameterization. Each primitive condenses a group of rays into one learned record, and its response to a query is governed by how closely that query belongs to the group. Rendering a camera ray then reduces to compositing all responses it elicits, and a scene can be optimized directly from posed images and rendered in real time with rays. Beyond its competitive performance on standard benchmarks, the main advantage of LFP is structural: its primitives reside directly in the 4D ray space, so optical and appearance effects that are already operations on the light field become behaviors of a single shared renderer. With minimal changes to that renderer, LFP supports multi-scale anti-aliasing, defocus deblurring with refocusing, rendering for fisheye cameras, and even transparent object reconstruction with ray refraction, matching specialized frameworks that devote substantial machinery to these effects.

</details>


### [63] [Preserve-and-Compose Training for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31202)
*Sehyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACT方法，通过结合源图像视觉证据与目标caption监督，改进零样本组合图像检索（CIR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索（CIR）需检索满足用户修改且保留参考图像相关内容的图像，但收集目标图像成本高。零样本CIR用目标caption作为监督，但目标caption可能遗漏了应保留的源图像细节。

Method: 提出Preserve-and-Compose Training (PACT)，利用image-text-text (ITT)三元组进行训练，无需目标图像或更新gallery，既将组合查询与目标caption对齐，又通过视觉监督保留源证据。此外引入Chord评分，在冻结图像空间中结合目标相似度与源相对方向一致性。

Result: 在四个ZS-CIR基准上，结合目标caption监督与源图像证据在多个数据集、骨干网络规模和外部gallery上均取得强检索性能。

Conclusion: 结合目标caption监督与源图像证据能有效提升跨数据集、跨骨干规模和外部gallery的检索性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Composed image retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve images that satisfy a user-specified modification while preserving relevant visual content from a reference image. Collecting target images for this purpose is costly, motivating zero-shot CIR methods that instead use target captions as supervision. However, target captions may omit source details that should be preserved. We therefore propose, Preserve-and-Compose Training, which complements target-caption supervision with visual evidence from the source image. PACT learns from image--text--text (ITT) triplets without target images or gallery updates, aligning composed queries with target captions while preserving source evidence through visual supervision. We further introduce Chord scoring, which combines target similarity with source-relative directional agreement in the frozen image space. Results across four ZS-CIR benchmarks show that combining target-caption supervision with source-image evidence leads to strong retrieval performance across datasets, backbone scales, and external galleries. The code is available on https://github.com/sehyunkwon/PACT.

</details>


### [64] [WeaveAgent: A Two-Stage Tool-Routing Agent for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31234)
*Zhongyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeaveAgent 是一个两阶段工具路由智能体，将路由与视觉感知解耦，通过 SFT+GRPO 训练实现可靠工具调用，并给出诊断性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分遥感中视觉 token 昂贵，且预训练模型零样本不产生工具调用，工具调用格式必须可靠。

Method: 两阶段：Stage A 路由优先，训练而非诱导工具发出；Stage B 条件执行，内在查询走视觉问答（缩略图/证据板压缩），外在查询在原始全分辨率图像上执行工具调用并进行观察掩码的二轮回答。训练采用对齐 SFT 后接 GRPO，奖励为 R_WA2。

Result: 对齐 SFT 将外在路由从 0% 提升至 80.75%（323/400）；GRPO 抑制 9 个内在误发且工具选择不变。2B 系统整体未超零样本 8B（0.263 vs 0.250）。Oracle 归因：加载观察使外在答案准确率从 0.025 升至 0.425，两轮 SFT 再加 +9.3 至 0.518。消融显示抑制是视觉依据的；改写查询使训练检查点损失 2-11 分。

Conclusion: 方法揭示工具调用可靠性与视觉感知解耦的可行路径，并提供诊断性归因；训练与评估基于 VagueUHR 语料，代码数据将发布。

Abstract: Problem. Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing with vague user intents has two bottlenecks: visual tokens are expensive, and tool calling must be format-reliable (pretrained models emit zero tool calls zero-shot). Method. WeaveAgent, a two-stage tool-routing agent, decouples routing from visual perception. Stage A is routing-first: emission is trained, not elicited. Stage B executes conditionally: intrinsic queries enter visual answering (full-scene thumbnail; a WeaveEarth-style evidence board as an optional fixed-budget, approx. 5k-token compression interface); extrinsic queries execute tool call on original full-resolution imagery, answering from tool observations in a second, observation-masked round. Training: alignment SFT, then GRPO under reward R_WA2. Results. Alignment SFT lifts extrinsic routing from 0% to 80.75% (323/400); GRPO suppresses 9 intrinsic mis-emissions while tool selection is unchanged. The trained 2B system does not beat the zero-shot 8B baseline overall (0.263 vs. 0.250), a diagnostic contribution. Oracle attribution separates two repair ingredients: loading the observation into context lifts extrinsic answer accuracy from 0.025 to 0.425 under marker-free cross-mode returns, and the two-turn SFT stage adds a further +9.3 points to 0.518 at a small routing cost. A +/- image ablation shows emission suppression is visually grounded, and a query-register matrix shows LLM-rewritten queries cost trained checkpoints 2-11 points. Scope. All training and evaluation use the 5,000 / 3,273 / 1,000-record VagueUHR corpus (600 intrinsic + 400 tool-requiring; the base seeds synthesis and is not used for optimization). Single-pass evidence construction runs at 7.31 s per image on an RTX 4090. Code, data, and evaluation protocols will be released.

</details>


### [65] [Gauss What You Need: Compact Gaussian Splatting Across Scene Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31248)
*Afif Boudaoud,Jiayi Liu,Alexandru Calotoiu,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TangoGS 将捕获规模先验与训练反馈结合，自适应决定 3DGS 高斯数量，跨场景尺度实现质量-体积最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 中高斯原语数量影响重建质量、存储与渲染成本，但如何跨不同捕获规模自动选择数量仍未解决：标准基准上的有效配置在更大捕获上导致高斯过少、细节不足。

Method: 观察到待表示表面（由捕获范围与分辨率决定）在训练前已知，而内容复杂度在训练中通过训练视图重建质量体现。TangoGS 据此结合捕获推导的模型规模与训练自适应：训练前根据捕获总像素（扣除重复观测同一场景点的视图）推导学习允许量以确定模型增长规模；训练中由重建质量指导高斯增删。

Result: 在 13 个标准基准场景上，TangoGS 以少 48% 的高斯达到最优基线 LeGS 的平均 PSNR；在 8 个大捕获上，同一配置自动扩展模型，取得最高平均 PSNR，比第二名高 0.54 dB 且高斯数为其 2.3 倍。

Conclusion: 捕获推导的学习允许量与训练质量引导的密度控制相结合，无需重新调参即可在跨场景尺度上实现当前最优的质量-体积折衷。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene as a collection of Gaussian primitives from a set of posed photographs called the capture. The number of primitives used to represent the scene affects reconstruction quality, storage, and rendering cost. How to select this number automatically across capture scales remains unresolved: configurations effective on standard benchmarks can leave larger captures with too few Gaussians to reconstruct fine details. We observe that the surface to represent, given by the capture's extent and resolution, is known before training, whereas its content complexity becomes apparent during training, through the reconstruction quality on the training views. We introduce TangoGS, which combines capture-derived model sizing with training-based adaptation: the capture determines the scale of the model, and training feedback determines its final size within that scale. Before training, TangoGS derives a learning allowance for model growth from the capture's total pixels after discounting views that re-observe the same scene points. During training, reconstruction quality guides how many Gaussians to add and remove. On 13 standard benchmark scenes, TangoGS matches the mean PSNR of the best-performing evaluated baseline, LeGS, with $48\%$ fewer Gaussians. On eight large captures, the same configuration automatically scales to larger models when necessary, achieving the highest mean PSNR among evaluated methods: $0.54$ dB above the runner-up with $2.3\times$ as many Gaussians. Together, capture-derived learning allowances and training-quality guided density control enable a state-of-the-art quality--size compromise across scene scales without retuning.

</details>


### [66] [MoTop: Motion-Topological Model For Micro AU Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31285)
*Huai-Qian Khor,Mengting Wei,Yante Li,Chu Kiong Loo,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoTop模型，通过可学习运动上下文与面部关键点拓扑建模，提升微表情AU检测精度，在CD6ME上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情AU检测是面部分析的关键上游任务，但AU激活区域受限导致AU歧义，且微表情动作细微，难以精确建模。

Method: MoTop结合可学习运动上下文生成区域软引导，用面部关键点捕捉微AU的细尺度拓扑变化；通过线性外推放大关键点过渡以增强低强度动态；并设计解剖学面部聚类增强层次化几何多尺度建模。

Result: 在微AU检测任务的CD6ME协议上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 运动-拓扑建模结合关键点增强与解剖聚类可有效缓解AU歧义，提升微AU检测效果。

Abstract: Facial micro-expressions are spontaneous, brief, and subtle facial movements that reveal suppressed emotions in high-stakes environments. In contrast to classic expression analysis, detecting action unit (AU) yields a finer representation of facial movements, serving as a preliminary step before defining expression classes and other downstream tasks. Therefore, it represents a crucial upstream task in facial analysis, and improving an AU detection module increases the precision of facial analysis. Despite that, detecting AU is challenging because of the constrictive nature of the AU activation regions, leading to confusion among different AUs known as AU ambiguity. To model the fine-scale changes, we propose \textbf{MoTop}, a motion-topological model that is augmented with a learnable motion context, yielding regional soft guidance for facial activity, followed by facial landmarks that capture the fine-scale topological changes of micro AUs. To increase the micro facial landmark representations, we amplify the encoded facial landmark transitions via linear extrapolation, thereby increasing the spatial proximity of landmarks and enhancing the low-intensity landmark dynamics. In addition, we design anatomical facial clusters that enhance the hierarchical representation, facilitating multi-scale modelling of facial geometry and improving micro-topological representations. With these contributions, we have achieved state-of-the-art performance on the CD6ME protocol for the micro AU detection task.

</details>


### [67] [UniAR: A Unified Framework for Autism Recognition Enhanced by Multi-View Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31298)
*Lei Xin,Zeheng Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Shihong Huang,Fanhu Zeng,Changjiang Jiang,Dengbo He,Yutao Yue,Zhenglun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAR：面向ASD识别的统一多模态框架，通过多粒度提示学习生成诊断文本，并用专家混合多尺度对齐模块对齐视觉与语义信息，在MRI和表情基准上领先SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD早期准确诊断很重要，但现有方法受制于诊断文本数据稀缺，只能依赖视觉分析，难以建模临床语义推理，因此需要解决语义稀缺下的鲁棒识别问题。

Method: 利用大型多模态模型生成词、短语、句子级分层诊断描述以补充配对临床报告；设计基于Mixture-of-Experts的多尺度对齐模块，动态匹配向量量化视觉原型与对应粒度语义表示。

Result: 在脑MRI和面部表情四个基准上持续超越SOTA：MRI平均准确率75.9%，面部91.6%，分别较基线提升1.5和1.2个百分点。

Conclusion: UniAR为语义稀缺条件下的ASD筛查提供了鲁棒且可解释的统一框架。

Abstract: Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a complex neurodevelopmental disorder for which early and accurate diagnosis is critical to improving long-term developmental outcomes. However, existing ASD recognition methods are often constrained by the scarcity of diagnostic text data, forcing them to rely mainly on visual analysis and limiting their ability to model clinically meaningful semantic reasoning. To address this challenge, we propose UniAR, a unified framework enhanced by multi-granularity prompt learning for robust ASD recognition under heterogeneous data variations. Specifically, UniAR leverages a large multimodal model to generate hierarchical diagnostic descriptions at the word, phrase, and sentence levels, compensating for the lack of paired clinical reports. To align the generated semantics with visual evidence, we further design a Mixture-of-Experts-based Multi-Scale Alignment Module, which dynamically matches vector-quantized visual prototypes with semantic representations at corresponding granularities. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks covering brain MRI and facial expression scenarios show that UniAR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, achieving average accuracies of 75.9\% on MRI benchmarks and 91.6\% on facial benchmarks, while improving average Accuracy on MRI benchmarks by 1.5 percentage points and average Accuracy on facial benchmarks by 1.2 percentage points over baselines. These results demonstrate that UniAR offers a robust and interpretable framework for ASD screening under semantic scarcity.

</details>


### [68] [CytoSPM: Open-Vocabulary Cytopathology Detection with Structured Prompt Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31314)
*Wenjie Li,Zishan Xu,Jinyang Huang,Zhengxin Nie,Shichao Kan,Yixiong Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多域开放词汇细胞病理检测基准PentaCyto及高效检测器CytoSPM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞病理类别细粒度、长尾且跨器官持续演化，需要开放词汇识别；但现有检测器多为单域闭集，且缺乏统一开放词汇评测基准。

Method: PentaCyto覆盖宫颈、泌尿、呼吸、浆液、甲状腺五域，含24基础类与9新类，每类配有结构化细胞形态学提示。CytoSPM采用解耦两阶段设计：先提取可复用的类无关视觉表征，再用类名与形态学提示进行类感知的结构化提示匹配。

Result: 在PentaCyto上新类检测与开放词汇检测均优于现有方法，同时保持高效推理。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放词汇细胞病理检测提供统一基准与高效方案。

Abstract: Cytopathology detection requires open-vocabulary recognition because cellular categories are fine-grained, long-tailed, and continuously evolving across different organ systems. However, existing cytology detectors are mostly single-domain and closed-set, and there is still no unified benchmark for evaluating open-vocabulary cytopathology detection. We present PentaCyto, a multi-domain benchmark covering cervical, urinary, respiratory, serous fluid, and thyroid cytology, with 24 base categories and 9 held-out novel categories. Each category is associated with structured cytomorphology prompts that describe diagnostic morphological attributes and provide clinically grounded textual knowledge. We further propose CytoSPM, an efficient detector based on a decoupled two-stage design. It first extracts reusable class-agnostic visual representations, and then performs class-aware structural prompt matching with class names and cytomorphology prompts. On PentaCyto, CytoSPM outperforms existing methods in novel-category detection and open-vocabulary detection while maintaining efficient inference.

</details>


### [69] [CG-HAF: An Interpretable Global-Local Lesion-Burden Fusion Framework for Ordinal Acne Severity Grading in Agentic Skincare Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31326)
*Muhammad Muhtasim Shahriar,Md. Naimur Asif Borno,Saad Aloteibi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CG-HAF通过显式融合全局严重度概率与检测器提取的病灶负担描述符，用轻量可解释分类器做序数痤疮分级，在同数据集上显著优于仅用全局证据的基线，但跨数据集迁移受分级标准不一致制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序数痤疮分级需同时区分视觉相近的相邻等级，并兼顾整体面部外观与局部病灶负担；现有方法多将其压缩为单一不透明表征，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出全局-局部融合框架CG-HAF：将多个独立训练分类器的整体严重度概率取平均，与目标检测器提供的结构化病灶负担描述符（病灶数量、检测置信度、病灶面积）组合成紧凑表征，再由轻量可解释分类器输出最终等级。

Result: 在常用基准上，融合方法较仅用全局证据的基线取得统计显著的提升，最严重病例上增益最大；在采用不同分级标准的独立数据集上表现明显下降，诊断分析显示差距主要源于分级标准不匹配，而非仅检测失败。

Conclusion: 可解释的全局-局部融合是序数痤疮分级的有效策略，而分级标准对齐是跨数据集可迁移性的关键；所得严重度信号还可支持护肤应用中透明、非诊断性的决策。

Abstract: Ordinal acne severity grading requires distinguishing visually similar neighboring grades while jointly weighing holistic facial appearance and localized lesion burden - evidence that most existing approaches collapse into a single opaque representation. We introduce CG-HAF, a global-local fusion framework that instead keeps this evidence explicit: averaged holistic severity probabilities from independently trained classifiers are combined with structured lesion-burden descriptors from an object detector (lesion count, detection confidence, lesion area) into a compact representation, from which a lightweight, interpretable classifier produces the final grade. On a widely used benchmark, this fusion yields a clear, statistically supported improvement over global-evidence-only baselines, with the largest gains on the most severe cases. Testing on an independent dataset with a different grading standard shows that strong within-dataset performance does not transfer automatically, and a follow-up diagnostic attributes much of this gap to mismatched grading criteria rather than detection failure alone. These findings support interpretable global-local fusion as an effective strategy for ordinal acne grading while highlighting criterion alignment as key to cross-dataset portability, with a further illustration of how the resulting severity signal can support transparent, non-diagnostic decision-making in skincare applications.

</details>


### [70] [DyMD: Preserving Interaction Dynamics through Distribution Matching Distillation in Few-Step Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31349)
*Haojun Xu,Jie Huang,Xin Lu,Mingchen Zhong,Zihao Fan,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyMD：面向具身视频的分布匹配蒸馏框架，自适应调整教师监督与critic拟合，将14B教师蒸馏为4步1.3B学生模型，提升运动保真度与下游规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视频扩散模型采样步数多、成本高，难以用于交互式下游任务。DMD可实现少步生成，但会抑制机器人与物体的运动。作者发现其根源在于：弱重加噪使教师后验集中在运动匮乏的rollout附近，限制运动恢复指导；而运动较强的rollout往往带来更大的fake-score拟合误差，阻碍生成器学习交互动态。

Method: 提出DyMD，随学生演化自适应调整教师监督与critic拟合。1）时间亲和度条件重加噪采样：按每个rollout当前交互保真度混合基础调度与教师先验，自适应调整时间步分布，平衡运动恢复与外观精修。2）动力学引导fake-score跟踪：用噪声条件预测器从潜变量时序动态估计噪声相对拟合难度，在critic损失中上调预测困难rollout的权重。

Result: 将14B教师蒸馏为4步1.3B学生，推理无需辅助模块。具身视频基准上，R-Bench任务遵循度较Base DMD提升9.6个百分点，PAI-Bench-G域分数提升5.1分，视觉质量相当。作为下游动作规划骨干，在两个WorldArena任务上平均成功率34%，Base DMD为16%。

Conclusion: DyMD通过自适应教师监督与critic拟合，解决了DMD在具身视频中运动抑制的问题，在保持视觉质量的同时显著提升运动保真度与下游规划成功率。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models offer expressive priors for embodied prediction and learning, yet their many-step sampling remains costly for interactive downstream use. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) enables few-step video generation, but can suppress robot--object motion while preserving visual quality. Examining DMD's teacher and fake-score signals, we find that weak re-noising keeps the teacher posterior concentrated near motion-deficient rollouts, limiting motion-restoring guidance. Meanwhile, stronger-motion rollouts tend to incur larger fake-score fitting errors, which can hinder the generator's learning of interaction dynamics. We propose DyMD, a DMD framework that adapts both teacher supervision and critic fitting to the evolving student. Temporal affinity--conditioned re-noise sampling adapts the timestep distribution to each rollout's current interaction fidelity by mixing the base schedule with a teacher prior motivated by local posterior variation, thereby balancing motion recovery and appearance refinement. To better track stronger-motion rollouts, dynamics-guided fake-score tracking uses a noise-conditioned predictor to estimate noise-relative fitting difficulty from latent temporal dynamics, then upweights predicted-hard rollouts in the critic loss. Using DyMD, we distill a 14B teacher into a four-step 1.3B student with no auxiliary modules at inference. On embodied-video benchmarks, the student improves R-Bench task adherence by $9.6$ percentage points and PAI-Bench-G Domain score by $5.1$ points over Base DMD while maintaining comparable visual quality. As a backbone for downstream action planning, our student achieves 34% mean success across two WorldArena tasks, compared with 16% for Base DMD.

</details>


### [71] [Open Vocabulary Domain Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31356)
*Sumanth Udupa,Mehrtash Harandi,Yadan Luo,Mahsa Baktashmotlagh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"开放词汇域遗忘"(OVDU)新协议，要求域遗忘须能泛化到未见类别，并提出手术式参数编辑框架解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似域遗忘(ADU)方法基于错误的闭词汇假设：仅在遗忘微调时见过的类别上评估，因此只是过拟合已见"类-域"对，域对未见类别仍易被识别，造成"遗忘"的假象。真正的域擦除应是类别无关的。

Method: 提出手术式参数编辑框架：1) Fisher信息掩码隔离域敏感权重，从数学上保护基础零样本泛化能力；2) 目标流形散射(TMS)目标，通过基于偏好的挖掘在局部打散遗忘域的风格几何结构。

Result: 在PACS、OfficeHome、DomainNet上评估，开放词汇泛化能力大幅优于现有基线；样本效率极高，仅用4 shots即超过基线峰值8-shot结果。

Conclusion: 形式化OVDU协议并验证类别无关域遗忘的可行性与优越性，实现高效且泛化良好的域遗忘。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit remarkable zero-shot generalization, yet they often encode unwanted or hazardous stylistic domains such as idealized textbook diagrams in medical AI or cartoon vehicles in autonomous driving. Approximate Domain Unlearning (ADU) aims to selectively erase a model's recognition of a target visual domain while preserving accuracy on the remaining domains. However, existing ADU methods operate under a flawed closed-vocabulary assumption: they evaluate unlearning solely on the specific object classes seen during the unlearning fine-tuning phase. Consequently, these methods do not unlearn the domain itself; they merely overfit to seen class-domain pairs, leaving the domain easily recognizable for unseen classes and providing a false sense of removal. We argue that true domain erasure must be class-agnostic. To address this, we formalize Open-Vocabulary Domain Unlearning (OVDU), a rigorous protocol that mandates domain forgetting must transfer to held-out classes. To solve the OVDU challenge, we propose a surgical parameter-editing framework. First, a Fisher Information mask isolates domain-sensitive weights, mathematically protecting foundational zero-shot generalization. Second, our Targeted Manifold Scattering (TMS) objective uses preference-based mining to locally scatter the forget domain's stylistic geometry. Evaluated across PACS, OfficeHome, and DomainNet, our method vastly improves open-vocabulary generalization over existing baselines. Crucially, it delivers exceptional sample efficiency, outperforming peak 8-shot baseline results with only 4 shots.

</details>


### [72] [OpenVAM: Open-World Visual Attention Modeling with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31364)
*Kiana Hooshanfar,Amirhossein Kazerouni,Alireza Hosseini,Michael Brudno,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenVAM是一个统一的可解释视觉注意力建模框架，结合密集显著性定位与VLM语义解释，跨自然场景、商业图像和UI/web布局实现域鲁棒性。


<details>
