Our results reveal a clear gap between cultural awareness in Haitian Creole and higher-resource French, with Haitian performance being more uneven across domains and more affected by French linguistic interference. Story generation further reveals recurring portrayals of Haitian characters through hardship and resilience, showing that even positive characterizations can encode stereotypical narratives. Our code, benchmark, and evaluation framework are publicly available.

</details>


### [146] [Muslim: A Deployed Arabic Voice AI Platform for Grounded Islamic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31511)
*Yahya Mohamed Elnawasany*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Muslim是一个生产级阿拉伯语语音AI平台，为真实用户提供有据可查的伊斯兰知识，包含实时语音流水线、多源检索层，并发布了微调模型、账户计量层与三层可观测性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究原型通常缺乏可运营产品所需的要素（模型发布、滥用防护、面向特定故障模式的可观测性），本文旨在填补这一空白，构建服务于真实用户的伊斯兰知识语音AI产品。

Method: 构建实时语音流水线（NeMo阿拉伯语ASR、OpenAI兼容LLM端点、自托管TTS）与跨六个MCP服务器路由的确定性多源检索层；发布微调模型Muslim-6B-PRO（5.94B，工具路由）与Fasih-TTS-V1（MSA TTS）；设计账户计量层（免费配额、容量感知拒绝、延迟邮箱验证）与三层可观测性栈（存活、错误报告、产品分析）。

Result: 念诵验证准确率98.4%（124例）；端到端语音延迟0.9–1.7秒；Fasih-TTS-V1在阿拉伯语TTS Arena（MSA）中综合排名第17位中的第5，开源权重系统第11位中的第2。

Conclusion: 论文报告了真实测量的延迟与准确率数据，并讨论了在生产环境中运行伊斯兰知识语音产品的具体工程权衡与局限。

Abstract: We present Muslim, a production Arabic voice AI platform serving grounded, sourced Islamic knowledge to real users. Beyond a real-time voice pipeline (NeMo Arabic ASR, an OpenAI-compatible LLM endpoint, self-hosted TTS) and a deterministic multi-source retrieval layer routed across six Model Context Protocol servers, we report three things a research prototype typically lacks. First, a released family of fine-tuned Arabic Islamic model artifacts: an efficient tool-routing LLM (Muslim-6B-PRO, 5.94B parameters) and a Modern Standard Arabic TTS model (Fasih-TTS-V1) that ranks 5th of 17 overall and 2nd of 11 open-weight systems on the community-voted Arabic TTS Arena for MSA. Second, an account and metering layer - a free per-account turn allowance, capacity-aware refusal, and email verification deferred to the point it actually matters - that turns an open demo into an operable, abuse-resistant product. Third, a three-layer observability stack (liveness, error reporting, product analytics) built specifically around the system's characteristic failure mode: a GPU-bound agent host going silent while the web tier keeps serving normally. We report real, measured latency and accuracy figures (98.4% recitation-validation accuracy on 124 cases; end-to-end voice latency of 0.9-1.7s) and discuss the concrete engineering trade-offs and limitations of running an Islamic-knowledge voice product in production.

</details>


### [147] [MexHat: A Dataset for Hate Speech Detection in Mexican Spanish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31553)
*Itzel Tlelo-Coyotecatl,Hugo Jair Escalante*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MexHat，一个面向墨西哥西班牙语语境的视频仇恨言论检测数据集，包含约1千个视频片段，涵盖三类分类与细粒度子类标注任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测需要捕捉语境线索以准确理解内容意图，但非英语资源稀缺，尤其欠缺对多模态内容中微妙、依赖语境且与文化相关特征的建模能力。

Method: 构建MexHat视频数据集，采集约1k视频片段，进行两层标注：三分类任务（无负面内容、冒犯性内容、仇恨言论内容），以及细粒度子类别任务（三个仇恨言论子类别）。

Result: 提供数据集统计信息与基线实验结果，凸显该任务的内在挑战性。

Conclusion: MexHat填补了墨西哥西班牙语多模态仇恨言论检测资源的空白，为后续研究提供基准与挑战。

Abstract: Ensuring online safety through content monitoring had raised Hate Speech Detection as a crucial task to be addressed. By essence the task demands the capture of contextual cues, which are essential for a precise understanding of the content's intent. Although automated detection approaches for the task have advanced significantly, the scarcity of non-English resources persists, limiting the ability of models to adapt to the subtle, context-dependent, and culturally related nature of multimodal content. In this paper, we introduce MexHat, a video dataset designed to capture the linguistic and cultural cues for the hate-speech detection task in a Mexican Spanish context. Our dataset comprises around 1k video clips annotated across two tasks: a three-way class evaluation (no negative content, offensive content and hate-speech content), and a fine-grained class evaluation including three hate-speech sub-categories. The dataset statistics and the baseline results highlight the inherent challenges associated with the task. Disclaimer: This paper contains sensitive content that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [148] [Strategically Diverse Sampling for Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31571)
*Alexander Gurung,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自训练数据的战略多样性（strategic diversity），提出 GROOT（构建方法层次树并采样不同路径）和 Verbalized Sampling 两种采样方法。在竞技编程和下一章预测任务中，策略多样化数据训练的模型在困难任务上优于 IID 训练，且为 RL 和测试时扩展提供强初始化；甚至用 Qwen3-4B 的战略多样但错误的轨迹自训练，超过了 235B 教师的 IID 蒸馏，说明方法多样性可能比正确性或教师规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 训练与推理方法（RL、测试时扩展）依赖重复采样，但只有响应有实质差异时才获益。自训练同样面临此问题：训练数据多由 IID 采样并按正确性过滤，导致过度代表模型已偏好的策略，缺乏战略多样性。

Method: 以"战略多样性"（解决问题的实质性方法差异）作为构建自训练数据的原则。提出两种采样方法：GROOT——构建方法的层次树并采样不同路径；Verbalized Sampling（VS）——适配生成无结构的方法集合。在竞技编程和下一章预测两个领域进行实验。

Result: 在困难任务上，策略采样数据训练的模型优于 IID 训练模型，并为 RL 和测试时扩展提供强初始化。最显著的是，用 Qwen3-4B 的战略多样但错误的轨迹进行自训练，效果超过从 235B 教师的 IID 蒸馏。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了关于"什么是有效自训练数据"的主流假设，表明方法的多样性可能比正确性或教师规模更重要。

Abstract: Many LLM training and inference methods, including RL and test-time scaling, depend on repeated sampling, but benefit only when the responses meaningfully differ. Self-training faces the same challenge: training data is typically constructed by sampling IID responses and filtering primarily for correctness, thereby overrepresenting strategies a model already favours. We investigate strategic diversity, or substantive variation among approaches to a problem, as an alternative principle for constructing self-training data. We generate strategically diverse data with two sampling methods: GROOT, a new method which constructs a hierarchical tree of approaches and samples distinct paths, and Verbalized Sampling (VS), adapted to produce an unstructured set of approaches. Across competitive programming and Next-Chapter Prediction domains, models trained on strategically sampled data outperform IID-trained counterparts on difficult tasks and provide strong initializations for RL and test-time scaling. Most strikingly, self-training on strategically diverse but incorrect traces from Qwen3-4B outperforms IID distillation from a 235B teacher. These results challenge prevailing assumptions about what makes useful self-training data and show that diversity of approaches can matter more than correctness or teacher scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Silent Success: A Release Gate That Passed on Checks It Never Ran, and Eight More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30307)
*Dong Hyeon Jeon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出质量门禁把"未执行"当作"通过"，引入pass/violate/unable to determine第三值修复；收集九例同类"未观测即通过"缺陷，证明补救措施收敛且可证伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产发布中的阻塞门禁因缺失数据被计为"无违规"而误报PASS，导致两周绿色构建实际几乎未做检查，暴露出"未观测"被静默当作"通过"这一失效模式。

Method: 将二值（pass/violate）扩展为三值（pass、violate、unable to determine），使未执行门禁变为失败并计入退出状态；随后在相同规范上开展独立检测，并收集跨场景的同类实例进行归纳验证。

Result: 三值门禁将静默通过转为失败；独立检测器在0.000177个百分点裕度触发、触发底线稳定到小数第六位；共发现九个同类实例（同项目五个、开源两个、缓存TTL未生效网关、释放返回空却计入空闲槽的推理服务器，以及作者自犯一例）。

Conclusion: 论文价值不在类别新颖性，而在补救措施的收敛：九个不同遗漏问题对应同一种干预，其中七个可由单次查询、命令或比较解决；该收敛主张可被证伪，作者据此请求评判。

Abstract: A blocking quality gate in a production release pipeline reported PASS on a run in which one subgate had executed neither of its two checks and another had run six of its eight. Both keys that decided the run asked whether a violation had been observed, and both computed that from a population already stripped of the cases that failed to run, so absent data answered "no" and a run that checked almost nothing scored perfectly, for two weeks of green builds. Introducing a third value, pass, violate, and unable to determine, turned those silent passes into failures and put the shortfall into the exit status; separate work on the same specifications then found a detector firing with a margin of 0.000177 percentage points, its firing floor holding at the sixth decimal place. Eight further instances of the same form followed: five more from the same engagement, two in open-source projects, a gateway where a configured cache TTL was declared but never applied on the write path, and an inference server whose count of free cache slots was credited on every step for releases that returned nothing, and one committed by the author while writing this paper, using tooling built to prevent exactly it. What the nine cases are offered for is not the novelty of the category but the convergence of the remedy, nine different missed questions, one kind of act, seven of the nine answerable by a single query, command, or comparison, and that convergence is falsifiable, which is what this paper asks to be judged on.

</details>


### [150] [Empty Intersection: Provenance Coverage Rose to 98% and Neither Verification Decision Moved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30308)
*Dong Hyeon Jeon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文测量两种被开方处方(逐行分级与单一写入入口)能否改变生产部署中的验证结论,结果两者均无法触及两个验证决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前companion paper诊断该部署的验证程序用系统自己写入的值决定结果;本文检验常被提出的两种结构防御是否真能改变verdict,因为此前无人测量。

Method: 在冻结的194,620行快照及快照支持的两个验证决策上,对两种处方分别测量:按grade过滤查询、扩展grade词表、单一入口强制grade。

Result: 过滤使两决策从pass转为undetermined;扩展词表使classified coverage从36.1%升至98.4%;单一入口拒绝3,070次写入。三者均未给决策提供可采信输入;修复的行与决策读取的32行不相交,某干预移动的121,296行全在查询窗口之外。

Conclusion: 处方在自身规格上并不失败,但均针对总体而非具体决策。本文报告决策能获得可采信输入的条件,并指出两决策受阻原因不同。

Abstract: Two structural defenses for provenance, a grade on every row, so that a verification routine cannot mistake the system's own output for an observation, and a single write ingress, so that the grade is enforced rather than merely conventional, were measured against the production deployment that motivated them, over a frozen snapshot of 194,620 rows and the two verification decisions the snapshot supports. Neither reaches either decision. Both were prescribed by a companion paper, which diagnosed that deployment: its verification routines decided outcomes using values the system itself had written. Neither prescription is new: both are established practice in fields that do not cite one another, and no prior work measuring whether either changes a verdict was found, so what is offered here is the measurement and not the prescriptions. Filtering the verification queries by grade turns both decisions from pass to undetermined; widening the grade vocabulary raises classified coverage from 36.1% to 98.4%; a single ingress requiring a grade refuses 3,070 writes. None of the three gives either decision admissible input. The prescriptions do not fail at what they specify. Each is stated over the population and makes no reference to any decision, so neither says which rows a decision will read, and the rows each intervention repairs and the 32 rows the decisions read do not intersect. The intervention that changed the most rows shows the reach most plainly: all 121,296 rows it moved from unnameable to named fall outside both query windows. This paper reports the conditions, measured rather than designed, under which the decisions would have admissible input at all, and notes that the two decisions are blocked for different reasons.

</details>


### [151] [HyQDB: LLM-Assisted Debugging for Hybrid Quantum Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30313)
*Charlie Campbell,Hao Mark Chen,Shuang Liang,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HyQDB，一种分层智能体，通过注入确定性硬件、物理与优化证据来修复混合量子程序中的静默故障，并构建了 QFaultBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子程序的故障常静默发生，现有工具支持有限且评测数据低估了该类故障。作者洞察到故障可分为需确定性分析的机械故障与需重建程序意图的概念故障，需不同策略。

Method: HyQDB 采用分层智能体：将确定性硬件、物理与优化证据注入 LLM 修复过程；当未检测到证据时，将"静默"作为信号升级到第二层意图重建，推断程序行为并与实现进行调和。同时构建基于专家故障分类法的 QFaultBench 基准。

Result: 在留出的人工编写程序集上，HyQDB 将修复成功率从标准 LLM 的 45% 提升至 75%；升级门控关键，使机械故障修复准确率提升 20%。

Conclusion: 分层策略与证据注入有效应对混合量子程序的静默故障，升级门控对机械故障修复至关重要。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum program failures frequently occur silently, yet existing debugging tools provide limited support for detecting and repairing them. These faults dominate the failures reported by domain experts, yet existing tools evaluate on public data that under-represents this failure mode. Our key insight is that faults divide into two classes that require different strategies: mechanical faults, which allow deterministic analysis, and conceptual faults, which require reconstructing the program's intent. To address this challenge, we present HyQDB, a tiered agent that injects deterministic hardware, physics and optimization evidence into the LLM repair process. When no evidence is detected, the agent treats this silence as a signal to escalate to a second intent-reconstruction tier, that infers the program's behaviour and reconciles it with the implementation. To evaluate HyQDB, we introduce QFaultBench, a benchmark built from an expert-derived fault taxonomy that represents the true failure modes of hybrid programs. On a held-out set of human-authored programs, HyQDB raises repair success over a standard LLM from 45% to 75%. We show that the escalation gate is crucial, giving a 20% improvement in mechanical fault repair accuracy.

</details>


### [152] [Proceedings Tenth Symposium on Working Formal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30324)
*Mircea Marin,Adrian Crăciun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是 FROM 2026 研讨会论文集的前言，介绍会议举办信息与收录论文数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无（该内容为会议论文集前言，非研究性论文）。

Method: 无。

Result: FROM 2026 于 2026 年 9 月 15-17 日在罗马尼亚蒂米什瓦拉举行，与 SYNASC 2026 同期举办，收录 14 篇经程序委员会接收的论文。

Conclusion: 该卷为 FROM 2026 会议论文集。

Abstract: The 10th Working Formal Methods Symposium (FROM 2026) was held in Timişoara, Romania on September 15-17, 2026. It was organized jointly by the Faculty of Informatics of West University of Timişoara, the e-Austria institute, and the Institute for Logic and Data Science (ILDS). This event was co-located with the 28th International Symposium on Symbolic and Numeric Algorithms for Scientific Computing (SYNASC 2026). This volume contains the texts of  the 14 papers accepted by the Program Committee for presentation.

</details>


### [153] [What Will Remain Human in Software Architecture? A Focus Group Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30334)
*Uwe van Heesch,Olaf Zimmermann,Christian Kohls*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过在EuroPLoP 2026举办焦点小组（22名产学研参与者），探讨AI开发代理对软件架构任务的影响，提出"harness engineering"与"认知债务"等核心概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发代理日益用于支持甚至部分自动化软件架构任务，需了解从业者如何看待这一转变：哪些发生变化、哪些保持不变、以及出现了哪些新责任。

Method: 在EuroPLoP 2026组织焦点小组，22名来自工业界与学术界的参与者围绕当前实践、信任与验证策略、AI自主性边界、治理挑战及教育影响展开讨论。

Result: 形成广泛共识：架构决策、问责制与架构护栏的编写本质上仍是人类任务；提出"harness engineering"（构建治理AI辅助系统创建的系统，含验证机制、知识层与企业标准）；确认"关键性"（不确定性与变更成本之和）是校准人类监督的通用标准；指出"认知债务"（缺乏深入理解而接受AI决策导致的人对系统理解逐步侵蚀）的担忧。

Conclusion: 报告呈现焦点小组发现，并提出未来研究方向。

Abstract: AI development agents are increasingly used to support and partially automate software architecture tasks. To explore how practitioners perceive this shift, specifically what changes, what remains, and what new responsibilities emerge, we conducted a focus group at the 31st European Conference on Pattern Languages of Programs, People, and Practices (EuroPLoP 2026). Twenty-two participants from industry and academia discussed current practices, trust and validation strategies, the boundaries of AI autonomy, governance challenges, and implications for education. Among others, we found broad consensus that architectural decision-making, accountability, and the authoring of architectural guardrails remain fundamentally human tasks. A central emergent concept was harness engineering: the discipline of building the system that governs AI-assisted system creation, comprising validation mechanisms, knowledge lay- ers, and company-specific standards. The participants agreed that criticality, understood as the combination of uncertainty and cost of change, serves as the universal criterion for calibrating human oversight. A further concern was cognitive debt: the progressive erosion of human understanding of the system when AI-assisted decisions are accepted without full intellectual engagement. In this report, we present the findings of the focus group and describe directions for future work.

</details>


### [154] [Untangling the Spaghetti Code in Game Development: A Review of Challenges and Academic Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30349)
*Esdras Caleb Oliveira Silva,Lyrene Fernandes da Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述（34篇工作）研究游戏开发中代码质量问题的成因、解决方案、研究者与开发者的协作情况，以及现有方案的充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏领域代码质量被认为结构较差、更复杂，导致可维护性低、缺陷频率高，需系统梳理问题现状与对策。

Method: 系统性文献综述，围绕四个研究问题对34项相关工作进行映射与分析。

Result: 游戏领域受短期思维、需求持续变更、代码复用减少、几乎无自动化测试影响；已有检测代码异味的工具/模型、测试实践与适配游戏的产品线等方案，但实践者采用度低。

Conclusion: 现有解决方案尚未被广泛采用，未来需研究其整合障碍，并加强研究者与开发者协作。

Abstract: The code quality in the game domain is perceived as less structured and more convoluted than in other domains, which results in lower maintainability and higher bug frequency. In this work, we conduct a systematic literature review to address four main research questions: (1) how the incidence of code smells and technical debt differs in game development compared to other software domains; (2) what academic solutions are proposed to mitigate these code quality issues; (3) whether researchers collaborate effectively with developers to implement these solutions; and (4) if the proposed solutions are sufficient to tackle the diagnosed problems. Thirty-four works were mapped and analyzed. We found that the game domain is affected by a short-term mentality, continuous requirements changes, reduced code reuse, and minimal to no automated testing. As potential solutions, we identified tools and models to detect code smells, along with testing practices and product lines adapted for game development. Although these solutions exist, they are not widely adopted by practitioners, highlighting the need to investigate barriers to their integration.

</details>


### [155] [Evaluating Code Recommender Systems: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30351)
*Daniel Borst,Stefan Sobernig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统文献综述(92篇)揭示代码推荐系统评估以系统中心的离线评估为主,以用户为中心的评估严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码推荐系统(CRS)日益重要,但其评估中"以人为本"的实践状况尚不明确。

Method: 对2017-2024年评估CRS的92篇文献进行系统文献综述与内容分析。

Result: 离线评估最普遍,在线评估与用户研究罕见;评估集中于软件构建阶段;多数报告效度威胁,以材料的外部威胁最常见。

Conclusion: CRS评估聚焦单一或少数系统质量,系统中心与用户中心评估失衡,多类型组合评估极少。

Abstract: Context: Code recommender systems (CRSs) are specialized software systems operating on source artifacts to provide automatically generated recommendations to software developers in all phases of development. The goal is to improve software quality while enhancing developers' efficiency, effectiveness, and experience. Problem: Despite the growing importance, little is known about the state of evaluating these systems in a human-centric manner. Research Approach: We conducted a systematic literature review to identify and to synthesize primary studies evaluating CRSs (2017-2024). Ninety-two publications were included and subjected to a systematic content analysis. Results: Our study confirms that Offline Evaluations are the most common, system-centric evaluation type, whereas user-centric evaluations (Online Evaluations, User Studies) are rarely reported. Evaluations are concentrated on the Software Construction phase. Most studies explicitly report threats to validity, with External threats to study Materials being the most common. Conclusion: The state of evaluations on CRSs is narrowly focused on single or a few system qualities. There is an imbalance between a majority of system-centric evaluations and a minority of user-centric evaluations. Combined, multi-type evaluations are rarely reported.

</details>


### [156] [Closing the Loop: Continuous Measurement-Driven Refinement of Offloading Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30429)
*Falk Dettinger,Matthias Weiß,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种运行时闭环校准机制，通过在线增量更新轻量级多头神经网络，持续修正车联网边缘计算中RTT、处理时间和利用率的绝对性能预测，在动态环境下减少预测漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆将计算密集型感知与决策任务卸载到后端服务器，需要准确预测RTT等绝对性能指标。但强烈的时序变化、异构后端硬件和多模态延迟模式导致离线训练的预测器发生漂移，形成对延迟敏感功能而言的可靠性缺口。

Method: 采用面向运营的、测量驱动的闭环系统：在运行时将真实执行测量与预测值对齐，对轻量级多头神经网络进行增量在线更新并保持模型稳定性；模型隐式学习输入指标的非高斯广分布，评估中用基于sigma的误差分析刻画动态条件下的残差变异性。

Result: 在两个Kubernetes集群上的实验表明，持续测量驱动的精化降低了预测漂移，提高了RTT、处理时间和利用率的预测精度，并在异构延迟模式间稳定了预测行为；但输入指标的广而多模态分布对绝对预测设置了根本限制，残差误差常超过设定阈值。

Conclusion: 在线校准对动态车联网边缘环境中的鲁棒计算卸载是可行且必要的，同时表明未来需要解决极端延迟模式和高方差运行条件的机制。

Abstract: Modern vehicles increasingly offload computation- ally intensive perception and decision functions to backend servers, requiring accurate predictions of absolute performance metrics such as Round-Trip Time (RTT), processing time, and utilization. In practice, strong temporal variability, heterogeneous backend hardware, and multimodal latency regimes cause offline- trained predictors to drift, creating a reliability gap for latency- sensitive functions. We address this gap with an operational, measurement-driven closed loop that continuously recalibrates absolute-value predictors during runtime. The system aligns real execution measurements with predicted values and performs incremental online updates of a lightweight multi-head neural network while preserving model stability. The model implicitly learns the broad, non-Gaussian spread of input metrics, and a sigma-based error analysis in our evaluation characterizes resid- ual variability under dynamic conditions. Experiments across two Kubernetes clusters show that continuous measurement- driven refinement reduces prediction drift, improves accuracy for RTT, processing time, and utilization, and stabilizes prediction behavior across heterogeneous latency regimes. However, the broad and multimodal distribution of input metrics imposes fundamental limits on absolute-value prediction, with residual errors frequently exceeding configured thresholds. Overall, online calibration proves feasible and necessary for robust computation offloading in dynamic vehicular edge environments, while high- lighting the need for future mechanisms that address extreme latency regimes and high-variance operating conditions.

</details>


### [157] [EA-Ops: Git-Native Architecture as Code for Continuous Enterprise Architecture Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30593)
*Vahid Tavakkoli,Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出EA-Ops，一种Git原生的"架构即代码"框架，将架构事实用YAML表示，基于ArchiMate 3.2校验、治理规则和图形化影响分析，并生成报告与静态门户。经240次故障注入试验全部与真值一致（P/R/F1=1.000）；5万对象/10万关系时验证中位52.6s、影响遍历627ms、内存919MB，验证了Git原生持续治理在数万对象规模的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业架构（EA）仓库常将架构模型与工程变更工作流分离，导致模型与实现脱节、缺乏持续治理与可复现的变更影响分析，难以支撑大规模架构的自动化治理。

Method: EA-Ops为开源Git原生框架：架构事实以YAML表示；对ArchiMate 3.2档案进行类型化关系校验；执行组织特定治理规则；基于图做变更影响分析；从同一受审源生成面向人的报告和静态交互门户。评测用可复现的GitHub Actions测试台，注入8类结构性/语义/治理故障、每类30次试验（共240次），并做30次重复的可扩展性实验及10场景Metroville数字许可参考架构对比独立的广度优先搜索预言机。

Result: 240次试验全部与真值精确匹配，精确率、召回率和F1均为1.000；扩展至5万对象和10万关系时验证中位52.582s、影响遍历中位627ms、峰值RSS 919.2MB；10场景参考架构在验证结果与影响集上均与独立BFS预言机完全一致。配置的10万对象端到端基准成功生成模型，但在性能阶段超过180分钟CI预算，未外推任何计时结果；在5万对象时，Markdown报告生成而非语义验证成为主要扩展瓶颈。

Conclusion: 结果表明Git原生持续治理在数万对象规模上是实用的EA运营模式，同时也明确了更大规模仓库的限制与优化目标（如报告生成瓶颈与10万对象的CI时长问题）。

Abstract: Enterprise architecture (EA) repositories frequently separate architecture models from the engineering workflow used to change software and infrastructure. This article presents EA-Ops, an open-source Git-native Enterprise Architecture-as-Code framework that represents architecture facts as YAML, validates typed relationships against an ArchiMate 3.2 profile, enforces organization-specific governance rules, performs graph-based change-impact analysis, and publishes human-facing reports and a static interactive portal from the same reviewed source. We evaluate EA-Ops with a reproducible GitHub Actions harness. Eight independently injected structural, semantic, and governance fault classes were executed across 30 trials each; all 240 trials matched ground truth exactly, with precision, recall, and $F_1$ of 1.000. Scalability experiments with 30 measured repetitions reached 50,000 objects and 100,000 relationships: median validation time was 52.582~s, median impact traversal was 627.000~ms, and peak resident-set size was 919.2~MB. A ten-scenario Metroville digital-permit reference architecture produced exact validation outcomes and exact impact-set agreement with an independent breadth-first-search oracle in every scenario. The configured 100,000-object end-to-end benchmark generated its model successfully but exceeded the 180-minute CI budget during the performance stage; no timing result is extrapolated. At 50,000 objects, Markdown report generation rather than semantic validation is the dominant scaling bottleneck. The results support Git-native continuous governance as a practical EA operating model at tens-of-thousands-of-object scale while defining clear limits and optimization targets for larger repositories.

</details>


### [158] [A Framework for Identifying, Categorizing, and Explaining Bias in AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30642)
*Manaal Basha,Aimee M. Ribeiro,Gema Rodriguez-Perez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究构建了带偏见过滤标签的Python代码数据集，评估LLM自动检测和解释代码偏见的能力，发现Gemini和Qwen3-coder表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益融入软件开发流程，AI生成代码中的无意识偏见引发担忧，但缺乏系统性识别、分类和解释这些偏见的研究。

Method: 扩展已有偏见Python代码数据集，人工标注偏见类别和理由建立真值数据集；用ICL评估专有和开源LLM作为偏见检测与解释系统；通过结构化解释和代码识别指标分析LLM解释与人工理由的相似度。

Result: Gemini分类准确率80.14%，精确率84.0%，召回率95.7%；开源最佳Qwen3-coder准确率82.45%，精确率68.64%，召回率80.22%。两者解释相似度分别为80.4%和80.14%，代码识别相似度为86.0%和87.82%。

Conclusion: LLM能有效检测生成Python代码中的偏见逻辑，并产生与专家解读高度一致的解释。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become integrated into software development workflows, concerns regarding unintentional biases in AI-generated code. Although evidence suggests these biases exist, limited research has systematically identified, categorized, and explained them. This study investigates bias in AI-generated code and evaluates whether LLMs can reliably identify and explain it through a taxonomy-driven framework. We extended an existing dataset of biased AI-generated Python code and manually annotated snippets with bias categories and human-authored justifications to establish a ground-truth dataset. Using this dataset, we evaluated proprietary and open-source LLMs as automated bias detection and justification systems through ICL. Finally, we analyzed similarity between LLM-generated explanations and human-authored justifications using structured justification and code identification metrics.
  Our findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively support code bias identification and explanation. Gemini achieved 80.14% classification accuracy, with 84.0% precision and 95.7% recall, while the best open-source alternative, Qwen3-coder, achieved 82.45% accuracy, 68.64% precision, and 80.22% recall. Additionally, the models achieved justification similarity scores of 80.4% and 80.14%, respectively, relative to human-authored reasoning, and code identification similarity scores of 86.0% and 87.82%. These results suggest that LLMs can detect biased logic in generated Python code and produce explanations that substantially align with expert interpretations.

</details>


### [159] [Developing a Roadmap to an AI-first Organization: A Case Study in Embedded Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30863)
*Viktor Kjellberg,Srijita Basu,Simin Sun,Farnaz Fotrousi,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究，探讨一家大型嵌入式系统公司向"AI优先"组织转型的过程，分析40名参与者对自主AI代理影响的预期，并提出转型路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理从"助手"向具备规划、执行、评估能力的高自主系统演进，将重塑软件工程。嵌入式软件组织因对质量、可追溯性、验证和长期可维护性的严格要求，其转型尤为关键，但相关实践研究仍不足。

Method: 采用混合方法的案例研究：通过一场有40名参与者（scrum master、架构师、管理层、产品负责人）的半结构化工作坊收集数据并进行分析。

Result: 参与者预期自主AI将影响团队结构、所需能力、组织策略以及开发者角色。基于此，论文讨论了联邦式AI团队组建、人在环实践，以及嵌入式软件工程中AI代理的可持续采纳。

Conclusion: 论文提出了该组织迈向"AI优先"组织的具体路线图，并为嵌入式软件工程中AI代理的落地提供实践启示。

Abstract: The emergence of AI agents is expected to reshape software engineering by moving beyond AI as assistants towards systems capable of planning, executing, and evaluating development tasks with increasing autonomy. This transition is particularly significant for embedded software organizations, where strict requirements for quality, traceability, verification, and long-term maintainability often apply. This paper presents a case study of a large embedded systems company and its transition toward becoming an AI-first organization. Through a mixed method, we analyzed data collected from a semi-structured workshop with 40 participants, including scrum masters, architects, management, and product owners. The findings show that the participants expect agentic AI to affect team structure, required competencies, organizational strategies, and developers' roles within the organization. Based on these findings, the paper discusses implications for federated AI team formation, human-in-the-loop practices in such an organization, and the sustainable adoption of AI agents in embedded software engineering. We also present a concrete roadmap for the organization towards becoming an AI-first organization.

</details>


### [160] [CoCoRerank: Towards Conventional Commit Message Generation by Component and Candidate Consistency Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30953)
*Shaopeng Jia,Yali Du,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究完整CCS格式的规范提交信息生成，构建86,688条高质量基准数据，并提出基于二维一致性的重排序框架CoCoRerank，提升结构组件预测与主题生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动提交信息生成通常将消息视为非结构化文本序列，难以支持期望遵循Conventional Commits Specification（type (scope): subject）的标准化开发工作流。

Method: 构建包含86,688条开源GitHub提交的新基准，经结构规范化与语义质量过滤，将消息归一化为type、scope、subject。提出CoCoRerank重排序框架，利用代码变更、type、scope、subject之间的横向一致性以及多候选生成之间的纵向共识。

Result: 与代表性CMG基线、LLM生成器、重排序策略及消融变体实验表明，CoCoRerank提升了结构组件预测和subject生成质量。

Conclusion: 完整CCS监督与多维一致性建模为准确、规范的提交信息生成提供了有效基础。工件已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Commit messages are essential for understanding software changes, yet automatic commit message generation typically treats a message as an unstructured text sequence. This limits its ability to support standardized development workflows, where commit messages are often expected to follow the Conventional Commits Specification (CCS) in the form type (scope): subject. In this paper, we study conventional commit message generation under the complete CCS format. We construct a new benchmark of 86,688 high-quality commits collected from open-source GitHub repositories, with each message normalized into type, scope, and subject through structural normalization and semantic quality filtering. Based on this benchmark, we propose a two-dimensional consistency-based reranking framework named CoCoRerank for LLM-based generation. CoCoRerank exploits horizontal consistency among the code change, type, scope, and subject, as well as vertical consensus across multiple generated candidates. Experiments with representative CMG baselines, LLM generators, reranking strategies, and ablation variants show that CoCoRerank improves both structural component prediction and subject generation quality. The results demonstrate that complete CCS supervision and multidimensional consistency modeling provide an effective foundation for accurate and standardized commit message generation. The artifact is publicly released at https://github.com/bluewhalebug/CoCoRerank.

</details>


### [161] [Rethinking Data Quality for AI-Driven Systems: Evidence from Practitioner Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31191)
*Hariharan Gopinath,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 访谈16名从业者，结合反身性主题分析，提出AI系统中数据质量的六大主题及生命周期保障框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据质量研究将数据视为存储、处理和验证的输入，但在AI驱动的软件密集型系统中，数据还塑造模型行为、评估与合法使用。关于从业者如何在AI条件下定义、评估和管理数据质量的实证证据仍然有限。

Method: 访谈9个组织的16名从业者，对访谈文本进行反身性主题分析，归纳出六个主题，并将五种反复出现的条件作为解释这些主题与信任下降关系的线索，进而提出"生命周期保障"的概念框架。

Result: 可追溯性从模块化调试转向模型行为归因；用模型做质量评估引入循环性；Agent上下文与记忆成为数据对象；合成与伪标注数据使真实性成为质量关切；基础模型开发中合法性成为训练数据门槛，代表性通过系统需安全运行的场景覆盖度来评判。

Conclusion: 以往ML研究多单独考察这些问题，本研究提供从业者视角，说明它们如何作为工程与组织问题共同出现，并用生命周期保障框架整合：重点在于产出证据，证明在数据影响嵌入模型行为、模型判断或Agent行动时，数据能支撑特定的AI主张。

Abstract: Data quality research has usually treated data as an input that is stored, processed, and validated. In AI-driven software-intensive systems, data also shapes model behavior, evaluation, and lawful use. Empirical evidence remains limited on how practitioners define, assess, and manage quality under these conditions. We interviewed 16 practitioners from nine organizations and analyzed the transcripts using reflexive thematic analysis and developed six themes from participants' accounts. In AI systems, traceability shifted from modular debugging to attributing model behavior, while using models as quality assessors introduced circularity. Agent context and memory became data objects, and synthetic and pseudo-labeled data made authenticity a quality concern. In foundation-model development, lawfulness became a gate for training data, while representativeness was judged through coverage of situations in which the system must behave safely. Prior ML research examines many of these problems separately. Our study provides a practitioner-grounded account of how they are encountered together as an engineering and organizational concern. We also interpret five recurring conditions as helping explain how the themes relate to reduced trust in data and AI outcomes. We synthesize these findings through lifecycle assurance: a conceptual framing focused on producing evidence that data can support a specific AI claim when its influence may be embedded in model behavior, model-based judgments, or agent actions.

</details>


### [162] [Joule-Profiler: Profiling the Energy Consumption of Build Automation Tools Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31228)
*Jérémy Woirhaye,François Gibier,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Joule-Profiler：开源Linux命令行工具，通过Intel RAPL与NVML测量硬件能耗，并按用户自定义阶段归因，用于Maven构建管线的分阶段能耗剖析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建管线的能耗对开发者基本不可见；现有CI能耗工具要么因云环境硬件限制只能做模型估算，要么仅报告总量而无法分解到具体阶段。

Method: 测量CPU（Intel RAPL）与GPU（NVML）硬件能耗，通过监控标准输出，将能耗归因到用户定义的程序阶段；以Google Gson为案例，用基于token模式匹配的Maven插件调用分析最近5个Gson版本的构建，区分冷构建（本地仓库为空）与热构建（依赖已缓存）。

Result: 得到各Gson版本构建的分阶段能耗画像，并可对比冷、热构建的能耗差异。

Conclusion: Joule-Profiler可实现细粒度、基于实测的构建管线能耗剖析，为CI能耗可见性提供工具支持。

Abstract: Build pipelines are integral to modern software development, yet their energy footprint remains largely invisible to practitioners. Existing CI energy tools either rely on model-based estimation (due to hardware access restrictions in cloud runners) or report only total pipeline energy without decomposing it into meaningful phases. Joule-Profiler is an open-source command-line tool for Linux that measures hardware energy consumption via Intel RAPL (CPU), NVML (NVIDIA GPU), and attributes it to user-defined program phases by monitoring standard output. In this tool paper, we demonstrate Joule-Profiler in the context of Maven build pipelines using Google Gson as a case study. By applying a token pattern-matching Maven plugin invocations, we analyze builds across the 5 most recent Gson releases into per-phase energy profiles, comparing cold builds (empty local repository) and warm builds (cached dependencies).

</details>


### [163] [Semi-Automatic Quantification of Bayesian Networks for Software Decision Support: Comparing WSA and RNM in a Software R&D Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31275)
*Mirko Perkusich,João Nunes,Emilia Mendes,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar Sousa,Danyllo Albuquerque,Kyller C. Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过嵌入式案例研究，比较WSA与RNM两种半自动贝叶斯网络CPT量化方法在软件决策中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家驱动的贝叶斯网络可用于数据有限时的软件决策，但量化CPT需大量概率判断；半自动方法可减少直接启发，但缺乏来自实际决策过程的对比证据。

Method: 在单一软件研发组织的两个场景（IoT项目特征选择、UI设计选择）中，对WSA和RNM作为完整启发-量化流水线进行嵌入式案例比较，共享图结构、根先验和决策证据；用15个专家定义的模型走查场景及决策会议中替代方案的回顾性重建进行评估。

Result: WSA在两个场景分别匹配7/7和6/8的走查预期，RNM为4/7和4/8；两者均将所选方案排在回顾排名靠前位置，但完整分布不同：特征模式中位总变差距离0.304，设计模式为0.340，某一设计模式达0.890。

Conclusion: 相似入围行为不代表不确定性表示等价；选择与验证此类模型时应考虑节点语义、专家可提供的判断、校准要求、完整输出分布及概率的下游用途。

Abstract: Expert-driven Bayesian networks can support recurring software decisions when historical data are limited, but quantifying their conditional probability tables (CPTs) requires many probability judgments. Semi-automatic methods reduce direct elicitation, yet practitioners have little comparative evidence from operational decision processes. We report an embedded case study comparing the Weighted Sum Algorithm (WSA) and Ranked Nodes Method (RNM) as complete elicitation-and-quantification pipelines in two contexts within a single software research and development (R&D) organization: feature selection for Internet of Things projects and user interface design selection. Within each context, the pipelines shared the graph, root priors, and decision evidence. We evaluated them through 15 expert-defined model-walkthrough scenarios and retrospective reconstructions of alternatives recorded in decision meetings. WSA matched 7/7 and 6/8 walkthrough expectations, whereas RNM matched 4/7 and 4/8. Both pipelines placed the selected alternatives near the top of the retrospective rankings. Their complete distributions nevertheless differed: the median total variation distance was 0.304 across four distinct feature patterns and 0.340 across seven distinct design patterns, rising to 0.890 for one design pattern. These results concern two ordinal value-estimation models in one organization. They show that similar shortlist behavior does not imply equivalent representations of uncertainty. For comparable software decision-support models, method selection and validation should consider node semantics, the judgments experts can provide, calibration requirements, complete output distributions, and the intended downstream use of the probabilities.

</details>


### [164] [When the Model Retires: An Empirical Study of LLM Migration in Open-Source Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31288)
*Hyungjin Lukas Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过挖掘 GitHub 上迁移提交，量化商业 LLM API 模型退役对应用的实际影响，发现约 82% 的迁移发生在模型关停之后，且通知期长短显著影响迁移及时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业 LLM API 依赖的模型版本由提供商按自身计划退役，通知期从一年到两周不等。作者想探究模型退役时应用实际发生什么，以评估弃用政策、产品依赖风险及工具需求。

Method: 挖掘 GitHub 上迁移离开 OpenAI、Anthropic、Google 官方弃用模型/端点的提交，与官方公告和关停日期匹配。从 17,703 个非 fork 仓库的 22,555 个提交中匹配 5,139 个官方事件；两名独立编码者验证 300 个分层样本（kappa=0.89-0.95），并按标签重新加权估计。

Result: 估计 82%（95% CI 79-84）的迁移在关停日期之后才提交，与仓库热度、过往退役经验或抽象层无关。该比例随通知政策变化：Anthropic 60-114 天通知为 89%，OpenAI 一年通知的 Assistants API 仅 13%；通知长度每增加一个 e 倍，关停后迁移几率降低约四分之三。94% 应用硬编码模型标识，迁移工作量从中位数 6 行（仅提示）到近 700 行（微调），仅 8% 切换提供商。

Conclusion: 模型退役后应用普遍延迟迁移，通知期是降低关停后迁移的关键因素。研究发布数据集与流程，并对弃用政策、LLM 产品依赖风险评估和工具设计提出启示。

Abstract: Applications built on commercial large language model (LLM) APIs depend on model versions that providers retire on their own schedule, with notice periods ranging from one year to two weeks. We ask what actually happens to applications when a model is retired. We mine GitHub for commits that migrate away from officially deprecated models and endpoints of OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google, matching each commit to the provider's published announcement and shutdown dates. From 22,555 commits in 17,703 non-fork repositories (2024-2026), 5,139 are matched to an official event; two independent coders validated a stratified sample of 300 (kappa = 0.89-0.95), and we reweight all estimates by their labels. We find that an estimated 82% (95% CI 79-84) of migrations away from retired models were committed after the shutdown date - after the application had started failing - regardless of repository popularity, prior retirement experience, or the presence of a provider-abstraction layer. The share tracks the provider's notice policy: 89% for Anthropic's 60-114-day notices versus 13% for OpenAI's one-year Assistants API notice, and each e-fold increase in notice length reduces the odds of post-shutdown migration by about three quarters. Model identifiers are hard-coded in 94% of migrating applications, migration effort scales from a median of 6 added lines for prompt-only applications to nearly 700 for fine-tuned ones, and only 8% of migrations switch provider. We release the dataset and pipeline and discuss implications for deprecation policy, dependency-risk assessment of LLM products, and tooling.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond Approved Actions: Runtime Validation of Persistent Outcomes in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31301)
*Haoran Zhang,Hengtong Zhang,Zhiyu Liang,Yu Yan,Decheng Zuo,Hongzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 EffectMatch 运行时，在执行边界内收集持久化变更并与应用审批内容比对，阻止未经批准的持久化结果被当作成功并传播到后续步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 越来越多直接操作软件系统，会修改数据库和在线服务。但已批准的数据库更新可能成功执行，却产生未被批准的额外持久化副作用（如发出通知）。现有防护只能审批动作或记录后果，无法在继续执行前检查持久化结果，导致未批准结果被当作成功并向下游传播。

Method: EffectMatch 是一个运行时系统：在受控执行边界内收集所有持久化变更，将其与应用针对当前状态和执行所批准的内容进行比对，并以比对结果决定是否提交及是否执行相关依赖步骤。

Result: 在 206 个公开业务任务上的对比评估中，EffectMatch 保留了全部干净执行，并阻止了所有测试到的错误提交。六个各运行 20 次的消融实验暴露了移除各机制导致的失败；80 个任务拓扑案例保持了真实交接并阻止了无效续执行。

Conclusion: EffectMatch 能阻止与应用审批不一致的持久化结果被静默接受及向下游传播。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act on software systems, no longer merely generating text but also changing databases and online services. However, an approved database update may succeed yet leave an unapproved notification because execution can produce persistent effects beyond the requested change. Current safeguards can approve an action or record its aftermath, but without checking the persistent result before continuation, an unapproved outcome can be accepted as success and propagated to later steps. We present EffectMatch, a runtime that collects persistent changes within a controlled execution boundary and compares them with what the application approved for the current state and execution. The comparison governs commit and dependent execution. In comparative evaluation on 206 public business tasks, EffectMatch preserved all clean executions and prevented all tested incorrect commits. Six 20-run ablations exposed the failure caused by each removed mechanism, while 80 task-topology cases preserved truthful handoffs and blocked invalid continuation. Together, these results show that EffectMatch blocks the silent acceptance and downstream propagation of persistent outcomes inconsistent with application approval.

</details>


### [166] [Compact Documentation for Coding Agents: A Benchmark, an Optimizer, and Why It Does Not Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31587)
*Md Shohel Arman,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究自然语言文档是否帮助编码智能体解决软件问题，提出往返基准与优化器，但发现当源码存在时文档无实质帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"更好的文档能帮助智能体解决真实仓库问题"这一假设是否成立。

Method: 构建往返基准：根据描述重新生成代码并跑原始测试来打分；以基准作为优化信号，搜索描述撰写提示，并测试文档对智能体解决真实issue的影响。

Result: 完整性而非长度决定描述保真度；优化后的提示可达满分保真并泛化到未见文件；但在两个模型家族、十个仓库上，文档未能超越仅给issue，阳性对照验证了评估能检测真实改进。

Conclusion: 报告这一负面结果，并刻画文档真正起作用的边界条件。

Abstract: We investigate whether natural-language documentation helps coding agents resolve software issues, and we build the tools to construct and evaluate it. We introduce a roundtrip benchmark that scores code descriptions by whether code regenerated from them passes the original tests, and show that completeness, not length, drives a description's fidelity. Using the benchmark as an optimization signal, we discover a description-writing prompt that reaches full fidelity and generalizes to unseen files. We then test the hypothesis that motivated the work: that better documentation helps an agent resolve real repository issues. Across two model families and ten repositories, and against a positive control confirming that our evaluation can detect a genuine improvement, we find that it does not. When the source is present, neither static compact documentation nor retrieved context beats the issue alone. We report this negative result together with the benchmark and the optimizer, and we characterize the boundary at which documentation helps.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Too Late to Slash: Coordinating a Risk-Free Equivocation Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30509)
*Hao Chung,Chen-Da Liu-Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文挑战"罚没(slashing)能保障PoS经济安全"的传统观点，构造了理性验证者在算法罚没下可无风险协调发起分叉攻击的策略，证明其为事后纳什均衡，即使收益ε任意小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对slashing安全性的常见论证依赖直觉而非形式化博弈论保证：认为没有slashing时验证者可无损失地尝试equovocation攻击，slashing使此行为有成本，从而保障与质押价值相当的应用安全。作者希望检验该论证是否成立。

Method: 构建一个针对理性验证者的无风险协调协议，在算法slashing规则下分析验证者"招募他人一起双签/分叉"的策略。证明该策略组合构成事后纳什均衡(ex post Nash equilibrium)，且无需验证者事先知道有多少人会参与。

Result: 该均衡对任意小的成功双签收益ε>0都成立，无论其相对质押量多小。这表明仅靠slashing无法提供与质押价值成比例的经济安全保证，slashing存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 算法slashing依赖的正是攻击者可控制的共识过程，不同于现实中可借助法院等外部执行的抵押安排。呼吁对slashing安全性进行形式化分析，避免简单套用具有独立执行机制的金融系统的直觉论证。

Abstract: Slashing is commonly argued to secure proof-of-stake blockchains by confiscating the stake of misbehaving validators. The usual justification is that, without slashing, validators can solicit an equivocation attack by signing conflicting blocks: if enough others join, the attack succeeds; otherwise, the attempt incurs no loss. Slashing is intended to make such attempts costly and thereby guarantee the security of applications whose economic value is comparable to the bonded stake. This rationale, however, rests on heuristic arguments rather than a formal game-theoretic guarantee.
  We challenge this rationale by constructing a risk-free coordination protocol for rational validators under algorithmic slashing. We show that the prescribed strategy profile, in which rational validators solicit other validators to equivocate, constitutes an ex post Nash equilibrium, even when validators do not know in advance how many others will participate. The equilibrium holds for any gain $ε>0$ from successful equivocation, however small relative to the bonded stake. Thus, slashing alone does not guarantee economic security proportional to the value of bonded stake.
  Our results expose a fundamental limitation of algorithmic slashing. Whereas conventional collateral arrangements in many real-world scenarios can rely on external enforcement, for example through courts, algorithmic slashing depends on the same consensus process that the attackers control. These findings call for a formal analysis of slashing's security, rather than overly simplistic arguments drawn from financial systems with independent enforcement.

</details>


### [168] [Proportional Representation in Temporal Voting with Ranked Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30555)
*Noam Hazon,Leora Schmerler,Nicholas Teh*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究序数偏好下时序投票的比例代表制，将选民每轮的top候选视为赞成票，但允许截断点在不同选民和轮次间灵活变化。建立JR/PJR/EJR/PSC的时序公理层级，刻画可保证性及所需的未来信息量，并分析公理验证的计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注赞成选票，而现实场景中选民提供的是序数偏好且偏好随时间变化。将序数偏好转化为赞成票时，合适的截断点因人、因轮而异，因此需要研究在不同截断点解释下比例代表制公理能否被保证，以及需要多少未来信息。

Method: 将每轮选一人的时序投票与JR、PJR、EJR、PSC的时序版本结合，考虑三类截断点设定（固定且共同、共同但逐轮变化、每个选民每轮单独设定），构建公理层级；分析各类公理在不同未来知识条件下的可保证性、所需规则类别及多项式时间算法；并研究公理验证的计算复杂性。

Result: 与赞成选票不同，任何版本的EJR都无法保证；固定共同截断点下JR、PJR、PSC可保证但需预知全部偏好；截断点逐轮变化时，对仅部分轮次一致的群体，即使预知全部偏好也无法保证JR或PSC；对每轮都一致的群体，仅知轮数即可在多项式时间内保证PJR，PSC甚至无需未来信息；个体截断点下JR/PJR均不可保证，但串行独裁可达到加性损失意义下最优的PJR近似；自然偏好限制可恢复精确保证。公理检查常为coNP完全，但更强的公理有时更易检查。

Conclusion: 序数时序投票中的比例代表制比赞成选票情形更受限：EJR完全不可保证，截断点的灵活性会削弱多项公理的可保证性，且未来信息与公理强度之间存在权衡。自然偏好限制和较弱公理或更强的特定公理可改善可保证性与可检查性。

Abstract: We study proportional representation in temporal voting, where one candidate is selected in each round. While prior work has focused on approval ballots, we consider ranked preferences, which may change over time. A natural approach treats each voter's top candidates as approved, but the right cutoff may differ across voters and rounds. We therefore require proportionality to hold for every admissible choice of cutoffs, whether fixed and common, common but varying across rounds, or set individually for each voter in each round. Combining these interpretations with temporal versions of justified representation (JR), proportional JR (PJR), extended JR (EJR), and proportionality for solid coalitions (PSC) gives us a hierarchy of axioms. We ask which of these axioms can be guaranteed, and with how much knowledge of the future. Unlike with approval ballots, no version of EJR can be guaranteed, and for the other axioms, flexibility in the cutoffs comes at a price. With a fixed common cutoff, JR, PJR, and PSC can be guaranteed, but only by rules that see all preferences in advance. Once the cutoff may vary across rounds, even such rules cannot guarantee JR or PSC for groups that agree in only some rounds. For groups that agree in every round, however, knowing only the number of rounds suffices for PJR in polynomial time, and PSC needs no knowledge of the future at all. Under individual cutoffs, no version of JR or PJR can be guaranteed, yet a rule as simple as serial dictatorship achieves PJR up to an additive loss that no rule can improve on, however much it knows. Natural preference restrictions restore exact guarantees. Finally, we show that checking our axioms is often coNP-complete; but perhaps surprisingly, a stronger axiom can be easier to check.

</details>


### [169] [From Bilateral Trade to Matching Markets: Sharp Gains from Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30702)
*Zhengyang Liu,Ying Qin,Zihe Wang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究匹配市场中的贸易收益，识别Bayesian机制设计下second-best与first-best收益的最坏情形比值，在多种先验与类型结构下给出了精确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双边贸易之外，匹配市场中可实现的贸易收益损失究竟有多大尚不清楚；本文希望在独立私人价值、Bayesian激励相容、临时个体理性及无预期预算赤字约束下，量化second-best相对于first-best的最坏情形效率损失。

Method: 将有限双边贸易的second-best保证推广到具有独立Borel先验、任意向下封闭可行性约束且first-best期望收益有限的匹配市场；对有界买方（单调风险率）和任意有界卖方，精确求解second-best与first-best的最坏情形比值；对至多m种类型的二元买卖双方，逐一确定精确比值。

Result: second-best保证可无损推广；有界买方且单调风险率情形下，最坏情形比值约为0.72490721；二元买卖双方m种类型时给出所有m的精确比值，m=2为8/9，m增大时极限为4/5；两类界在双边贸易中即已紧。

Conclusion: 匹配市场中预算平衡和激励约束导致的贸易收益损失可由仅依赖双边贸易的紧界刻画，说明双边贸易是这些效率损失的关键来源。

Abstract: We study gains from trade in matching markets with independent private values and costs, Bayesian incentive compatibility, interim individual rationality, and no expected budget deficit. A second-best
  guarantee for finite bilateral trade extends without loss to matching markets with independent Borel priors, arbitrary downward-closed feasibility, and finite expected first-best gains. For bounded buyers with monotone hazard rates and arbitrary bounded sellers, we determine the exact worst-case ratio of second-best to first-best gains, approximately $0.72490721$. For binary buyers and sellers with at most $m$ types, we determine the exact ratio for every $m$, including $8/9$ when $m=2$ and a limit of $4/5$ as $m$ grows. Both families of bounds are tight already in bilateral trade.

</details>


### [170] [How Much Must a Private Mempool Hide? Exact Leakage Thresholds for Sandwich Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31379)
*Tingyi Lin,Jiazhuo Li,Ruoran Lai*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 论文研究了在恒定乘积AMM（Uniswap v2）中，即使内存池隐藏交易，泄露的交易规模区间信息如何导致三明治攻击获利，并给出精确的获利条件与闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有/加密内存池用于隐藏待处理交易以阻止三明治攻击和MEV，但交易对、方向与粗略规模区间仍会泄露。作者要回答：多少泄露量足以让三明治攻击变得有利可图。

Method: 针对无手续费恒定乘积AMM建模：交易者观察包含受害者交易规模的区间，通过第一价格拍卖竞标三明治权利；获胜的前置交易必须保证受害者在区间内任意规模下仍可执行。

Result: 结果显示关键取决于区间内最小可行规模：它单独决定可行前置交易集合，最大可行前置交易对逐点、期望与最坏情况利润均最优，保证利润有闭式解。存在执行成本时，隐私层只需隐藏低于某明确阈值的下界，区间上端无关。两个或以上对称交易者时，所有纯策略完美贝叶斯均衡都将全部期望净租金交给拍卖者。若方向也被隐藏，则无任何非条件式前置交易能同时应对两个方向，但完美隐藏前置交易后套利仍可能存活。

Conclusion: 即使只泄露交易规模的粗略区间，也足以使三明治攻击有利可图；隐私设计的关键在于隐藏规模下界。当存在多个对称竞争者时，MEV租金被完全竞争掉归拍卖者，且方向隐藏可有效阻止前置三明治但无法阻止交易后套利。

Abstract: Private and encrypted mempools hide pending transactions to stop sandwich attacks and other forms of maximal extractable value (MEV), but what they hide is rarely everything: a transaction's pair, direction, and a coarse range for its size can still leak. How much leakage makes sandwiching pay? We answer exactly for a fee-free constant-product automated market maker, the pricing rule behind Uniswap v2. Traders observe an interval containing the victim's size and bid in a first-price auction for the right to sandwich it, and the winning front-run must keep the victim's trade executable at every size in the interval. The answer turns on the smallest size consistent with the leak. It alone determines the feasible front-runs, the largest feasible front-run is optimal for pointwise, expected, and worst-case profit alike, and the guaranteed profit has a closed form. When execution is costly, a privacy layer that wants to rule out sandwiches profitable at every consistent size may therefore reveal anything about the size except a lower bound above an explicit threshold; the upper end of the range is irrelevant. With two or more symmetric traders, every pure-strategy perfect Bayesian equilibrium of the auction hands the entire expected net rent to the auctioneer. If the direction is hidden too, no non-contingent first leg front-runs both possible directions, while post-trade arbitrage can survive even perfect pre-trade hiding.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [HybridInfer: Thermal-Aware Reinforcement-Learning Tier Routing for On-Device, Edge, and Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30270)
*Simran Koul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HybridInfer，一个热感知强化学习路由器，根据手机热余量和查询复杂度在三层（端侧/边缘/云）LLM 之间调度，在真实 Android 设备上缓解端侧推理的运行时不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧小模型推理有隐私、离线和零成本优势，但受热约束影响严重。作者发现该约束比"降速"更严峻：在旗舰 Snapdragon 设备上，持续端侧生成会导致 GPU 推理运行时崩溃或静默卡死，根源在 OpenCL 内核编译和移动 GPU 的长提示 prefill。现有跨层路由器不感知温度，且多在仿真或非移动硬件上评估。

Method: HybridInfer 面向三层架构：端侧 Llama 3.2 3B、带检索的边缘 Llama 3.1 8B、云端 GPT-4o。以手机热余量和查询复杂度估计为状态，用离线训练的 Q-learning 策略选择层。奖励权衡质量、延迟、成本和热惩罚，并加入本地执行奖励项。作者指出该本地奖励是热感知路由的前提，否则最优策略会把所有查询卸载。

Result: 在真实 Android 的 210 条提示基准上，学习路由器的质量显著高于两个手工调优启发式（配对 Wilcoxon，p<0.02），且在所有自适应条件中成本最低。全端侧条件在可服务查询上质量相当，但慢 3–6 倍，并在长查询上失败。因此路由在延迟、可靠性和覆盖度上胜出，而非质量。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实硬件上利用端侧热余量在能力不同的 LLM 推理层间进行选择的方案。路由相较纯端侧主要优势在于延迟、可靠性和覆盖度；本地奖励项对避免全卸载至关重要。

Abstract: On-device inference with small language models keeps user data local, works offline, and incurs no per-query cost, so the on-device tier is preferred when it is adequate. It is thermally constrained, however, and I find the constraint is sharper than a slowdown: on a flagship Snapdragon device, sustained on-device generation destabilizes the GPU inference runtime, which crashes or silently wedges after a few consecutive queries. The failure lies in the current toolchain (OpenCL kernel compilation and long-prompt prefill on the mobile GPU), recurs even when the device is cool, and is worst for long generations. Multi-tier routers across on-device, edge, and cloud models can relieve this pressure, but existing routers are thermal-blind and typically evaluated in simulation or on non-mobile hardware. I present HybridInfer, a thermal-aware reinforcement-learning router for a three-tier hierarchy (on-device Llama 3.2 3B, edge Llama 3.1 8B with retrieval, cloud GPT-4o) that uses the phone's thermal headroom and a query-complexity estimate as state and selects a tier by an offline-trained Q-learning policy. Its reward trades quality against latency, cost, and a thermal penalty, plus a locality bonus crediting on-device execution. I show this bonus is a precondition for thermal-aware routing: without it the optimal policy offloads every query. On a real Android benchmark of 210 prompts, the learned router attains significantly higher quality than two hand-tuned heuristics (paired Wilcoxon, p < 0.02) at the lowest cost of any adaptive condition. Always-on-device conditions match per-query quality on servable queries but are three to six times slower and fail on long queries, so routing wins on latency, reliability, and coverage rather than quality. To my knowledge this is the first use of on-device thermal headroom to select among LLM inference tiers of differing capability on real hardware.

</details>


### [172] [When the Preconditioning Exponent Turns Negative: Learning-Rate Coupling and Cross-Environment Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30271)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过配对四环境分类任务的受控实验，研究自适应优化器中二阶矩估计指数p与全局学习率η的交互作用，发现最大化跨环境准确率的p随log10(η)近似线性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分自适应方法研究介于动量式更新与Adam平方根之间的指数，但该指数与全局学习率的交互作用尚不明确，尤其在跨环境鲁棒性方面缺乏理解。

Method: 设计含稳定稀疏特征、环境相关虚假稀疏特征、密集特征和高维噪声的四环境分类问题；进行21个预条件指数p∈[-0.5,0.5]与5个学习率η∈[10^-4,10^-2]的配对跨环境训练。

Result: 最大化跨环境准确率的p随log10(η)近似线性下降，斜率-0.270至-0.300，R²为0.972–0.996；η=10^-2时源验证偏好正指数，而跨环境与最差环境准则偏好负指数；检查点分解显示较低p降低虚假-稳定与噪声-稳定权重比。

Conclusion: 负p并非普适最优，而是由学习率与预条件联合作用产生的高步长配置；研究揭示了源域验证与跨环境鲁棒性之间的模型选择冲突，且结论为单种子有限预算的机制研究而非广泛基准。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers are commonly parameterized by a fixed power of the second-moment estimate. Existing partially adaptive methods study exponents between momentum-like updates and the standard Adam square root, while the interaction between this exponent and the global learning rate is less understood. We perform a controlled cross-environment study using a paired four-environment classification problem with stable sparse features, environment-dependent spurious sparse features, dense features, and high-dimensional noise. Across \NumRuns{} source-training runs covering 21 preconditioning exponents $p\in[-0.5,0.5]$ and five learning rates $η\in[10^{-4},10^{-2}]$, we find that the exponent maximizing cross-environment accuracy decreases almost linearly with $\log_{10}η$. The fitted slopes range from $-0.270$ to $-0.300$, with $R^2$ between $0.972$ and $0.996$. At $η=10^{-2}$, source-validation selection still prefers positive exponents in all four environments, whereas cross-environment and worst-environment criteria prefer negative exponents. Checkpoint decomposition shows that lower $p$ reduces the learned spurious-to-stable and noise-to-stable weight ratios; under reversed correlation, it also reduces the magnitude of the harmful spurious margin. Negative $p$ is therefore not a universally optimal setting. It is a high-step-size allocation regime produced by the joint action of learning rate and preconditioning. The study also exposes a model-selection conflict: source-domain validation systematically selects a different preconditioning regime from the one that maximizes robustness to environmental change. The results are a single-seed, finite-budget mechanism study rather than a broad benchmark claim.

</details>


### [173] [ENAS: An Efficient Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search Framework for TinyML on Resource-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30272)
*Mohd Moin Khan,Naman Srivastava,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ENAS是一个无需GPU、面向TinyML的硬件感知NAS框架，通过静态可行性检查、基于cell的搜索空间和三阶段混合搜索策略，在微控制器上实现更快搜索和更低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现有NAS框架依赖GPU加速，难以适用于资源受限的开发环境；同时微控制器部署中峰值激活RAM是核心约束，需要硬件感知的高效搜索方法。

Method: 结合静态可行性检查、支持标准/深度可分离/瓶颈块及可选跳跃连接的cell级搜索空间，以及三阶段混合搜索策略（随机→top-K→变异）并配持久化跨运行缓存；无需GPU即可运行。

Result: 在Visual Wake Words和Melanoma Cancer两个TinyML基准上，跨8种微控制器（20KB–1MB SRAM）和9种分辨率评估；平均搜索加速2.41×和1.70×，测试精度与NanoNAS相当；峰值激活RAM显著更低；STM32H743上达79.4%测试精度，比CPU-only贪心基线高2.6个百分点。

Conclusion: ENAS证明了无需GPU的硬件感知NAS可在微控制器上高效运行，在保持竞争力的同时降低搜索时间和内存占用，已开源。

Abstract: We present \textbf{ENAS}, a hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS) framework that combines a static feasibility check, a cell-based search space supporting standard, depthwise-separable, and bottleneck blocks with optional skip connections, and a three-stage hybrid search strategy (random $\rightarrow$ top-$K$ $\rightarrow$ mutation) with persistent cross-run caching. Unlike many existing NAS frameworks that rely on GPU acceleration, ENAS is designed to operate efficiently without requiring GPUs, making it suitable for resource-constrained development environments. We evaluate ENAS on two TinyML benchmarks, Visual Wake Words and Melanoma Cancer, across eight microcontrollers with memory footprints ranging from 20\,KB to 1\,MB SRAM and nine input image resolutions. Our experimental results show that ENAS achieves mean search-time speedups of $2.41{\times}$ and $1.70{\times}$ on the Visual Wake Words and Melanoma Cancer datasets, respectively, while maintaining competitive test accuracy compared with the recent NanoNAS framework. A measured resource analysis further shows that ENAS-selected models use substantially lower peak activation RAM, the binding constraint for microcontroller deployment at matched accuracy. Additionally, ENAS achieves $79.4\%$ test accuracy on an STM32H743-based microcontroller, outperforming the greedy CPU-only baseline by $2.6$ percentage points. We release the ENAS framework as open-source at: https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/ENAS

</details>


### [174] [Common-Mode Collapse and Recovery in Direct Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31589)
*Varun Reddy,Bernardo L. Sabatini,Houman Safaai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DFA训练的隐藏层在tanh单元与独立sigmoid输出下会因误差共模分量导致激活崩塌与学习停滞；通过均值-协方差分解可定位该现象并给出缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFA用输出误差的固定随机投影训练隐藏层，但普通SGD有时会停滞在接近常数预测器的损失水平，作者希望找出停滞根源。

Method: 将误差做精确的均值-协方差分解，分离出由均值教学信号和均值突触前活动构成的秩一更新；用无拟合参数的简化模型分析该主导分量如何驱动tanh饱和，并在48种设置、MNIST及更深/卷积网络和CIFAR-10上验证。

Result: 秩一分量驱动tanh单元饱和；初始化时随机反馈平均上无法系统性纠正共享误差。简化模型能预测48种设置下激活敏感性的集中。MNIST上类别可解码性在崩塌后仍部分保留，但固定学习率下读出学习慢；Adam虽崩塌更深却学得更快；把基线读出校准到类别先验可抑制崩塌并加速学习；用误差符号会维持崩塌，减去批次均值可防止持续崩塌并改善学习。

Conclusion: DFA的停滞源于误差共模与秩一更新导致的激活崩塌；校准读出或减去批次均值可缓解，且该效应在更深、卷积网络和CIFAR-10上普遍存在，严重程度依赖读出、优化器和输入统计。

Abstract: Direct feedback alignment (DFA) trains hidden layers through fixed random projections of output error. With tanh hidden units and independent sigmoid outputs, plain stochastic gradient descent can stall near the loss of a constant predictor of class frequencies. We trace this stall to the error's common mode, the component shared across inputs. An exact mean-covariance decomposition separates a rank-one update formed by the mean teaching signal and mean presynaptic activity. Its leading component drives tanh units toward saturation. At initialization, random feedback provides no systematic correction of the shared error on average; readout learning limits its duration. A reduced model initialized from the network, without fitted parameters, predicts the concentration of activation sensitivity across 48 settings. On MNIST, class decodability largely survives collapse, but readout learning remains slow at a fixed learning rate. Adam learns faster despite deeper collapse. Calibrating the baseline readout to the class prior suppresses collapse and speeds learning; weaker feedback trades less collapse for slower learning. Replacing errors by their signs sustains collapse; subtracting the signal's batch mean prevents sustained collapse and improves learning in the tested setting. Related effects occur in deeper and convolutional networks and on CIFAR-10, with severity and cost depending on the readout, optimizer and input statistics.

</details>


### [175] [Offline Policy Evaluation as a decision support tool for designing Adaptive Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30273)
*João Victor Ferreira Alves,Eduardo Rocha Laurentino,Gustavo de Oliveira Kanno,Thiago Costa Rizuti da Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 结合离线策略评估(OPE)与受控热启动仿真，利用历史A/B测试数据判断并选择优于固定分配的上下文赌博机自适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史A/B测试数据是静态分配采集的，如何用其判断哪些自适应策略（若有）能优于原始设计、以及适用条件，尚缺实用方法。目标是帮助决定何时值得部署自适应实验。

Method: 先用双稳健估计器和估计的冗余成分做离线策略评估，对一组预定义的自适应/非自适应策略排序；在可获得真实值时，用同一奖励概率的数据生成过程构建仿真，将离线训练的策略热启动部署，形成锚定真实值的环境研究离线到在线的过渡。使用带已知异质性的合成RCT与oracle策略，并在Hillstrom、Criteo Uplift、LaLonde基准上以策略价值和regret视角复现。

Result: 当存在有意义的异质性时，上下文感知的自适应策略优于固定分配；无显著异质性时收益甚微。基准数据集上的结论一致。

Conclusion: 提出了一套实用方法，用以判断自适应实验是否值得部署，并基于既有A/B测试数据在竞争性自适应策略中做出选择。

Abstract: We investigate how historical data from fixed randomized experiments (A/B tests) can be used to inform the deployment of adaptive experiments based on contextual bandits. Given data collected under a static allocation, our goal is to assess which adaptive policies, if any, would have outperformed the original design and under what conditions. To this end, we combine off-policy evaluation (OPE) with a controlled warm-start simulation. From logged A/B test data exhibiting heterogeneous treatment effects, we estimate nuisance components and use doubly robust estimators to rank a portfolio of pre-specified adaptive and non-adaptive policies. When ground truth is available, we then deploy the same offline-trained policies in a simulator that reuses the exact data-generating reward probabilities, providing a safe, ground-truth-anchored environment to study the offline-to-online transition under warm starting. Using synthetic randomized controlled trials with known heterogeneity structures and an oracle policy, our results indicate that adaptive, context-aware policies improve upon fixed allocations when meaningful heterogeneity is present, while providing little benefit in its absence. We reinforce our findings on standard open benchmarks (Hillstrom, Criteo Uplift, and LaLonde), reinterpreted through a policy-value and regret perspective. Overall, our results provide a practical methodology for deciding when adaptive experimentation is worth deploying and how to select among competing adaptive policies using existing A/B test data.

</details>


### [176] [Cosine Similarity Is Not Evidence: Measuring the Noise Floor of Interpretability Transfer Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30275)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文主张：在AI安全可解释性中，尺度不变统计量（如余弦相似度）若不报告噪声底线则不能作为压缩下“保持”证据；需给出n与κ并对比比特位内零假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性产物在全精度权重上校准，却在量化权重上部署，并用不报告噪声底线的尺度不变统计量证明其“存活”。缺少可解释的样本量n与类别分离度ρ，使得高余弦值无法判断是保持还是噪声。

Method: 以difference-in-means方向估计器为例，引入无量纲量κ=nρ²/d，使用经典闭式E[cos]≈(1+4/κ)^-1；在真实激活上测量类别分离度ρ（Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct），用split-half零假设作为底线；在已知n处，对低比特余弦与同一量化模型内的split-half零假设比较，而非用全精度零假设。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上ρ=33–61（跨深度），两次独立估计仅因采样就可达到0.978–0.994一致；已发表的0.996全精度-量化拒绝方向余弦因未报告n而不可解读。INT4下方向发生旋转，且偏差超过估计器自身噪声；INT8未检测到移动，但不构成等价性结论。尺度不变统计量无法区分平移与衰减。

Conclusion: 尺度不变统计量必须报告噪声底线与n，低比特评估应在对应量化模型内测split-half零假设；仅凭高余弦不能宣称压缩后保持。作者给出只需一次前向传播的报告建议，并发布代码、数据与一键复现。

Abstract: A statistic reported without the quantity needed to interpret it is not evidence. We develop that thesis for a concrete practice in AI safety. Interpretability artifacts are calibrated on full-precision weights, deployed on quantized ones, and certified as surviving the change by scale-invariant statistics (cosine similarity, correlation, AUROC) that are reported without their noise floor. For the difference-in-means direction estimator, the split-half floor is governed by one dimensionless number, $κ= nρ^2/d$. The closed form $\mathbb{E}[\cos] \approx (1+4/κ)^{-1}$ is classical; the missing input is the class separation $ρ$, which we measure on real activations; no compression-transfer study we know of reports it. On Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, $ρ= 33$--$61$ across depth, so two independent runs of the estimator agree to $0.978$--$0.994$ by sampling alone. A published cosine of $0.996$ between full-precision and quantized refusal directions therefore cannot be read as preservation without the $n$ it was computed at, which is not reported. Where $n$ is known, we judge each low-bit cosine against the split-half null measured within that quantized model, because a full-precision null assumes the low-bit estimator has the same variance. That assumption is exactly what a null exists to test. The result is plain: at INT4 the direction rotated, and the deficit exceeds the estimator's own noise. At INT8 we detect no movement, which is not an equivalence claim. We also show that a scale-invariant statistic cannot distinguish translation from attenuation of a transferred decision variable, although the two call for opposite remedies. We close with reporting recommendations that cost one forward pass. Code, data, and a one-cell reproduction are released at https://github.com/pvarshh/quantinterp

</details>


### [177] [Why Clipping Matters in AdaGrad? Toward a High-Probability Theory under Generalized Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30276)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Ayaan Mohd,Harshit Rawat,Arnab Roy,Mayank Baranwal,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分析广义光滑与重尾噪声下原始AdaGrad，证明裁剪是稳定自适应几何结构的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在广义光滑性和仅有有界二阶矩的重尾噪声下，原始同步坐标AdaGrad的行为，揭示未裁剪时的失效机制。

Method: 分析未裁剪AdaGrad在重尾噪声下自适应分母学习噪声冲击几何而非局部曲率导致的各向异性失准，并证明裁剪的修复作用。

Result: 给出原始非滞后AdaGrad的高概率有限时域保证，梯度平方平均为O(d(√logT+log(1/δ))/√T)，复杂度为Õ(ε^{-2})。

Conclusion: 在重尾噪声下，裁剪是AdaGrad自适应几何的结构性稳定器，而非仅鲁棒性启发式。

Abstract: We analyze the original same-step coordinate-wise AdaGrad under generalized smoothness and heavy-tailed noise with bounded variance. In this setting, local curvature may grow sub-quadratically with the gradient norm, and stochastic gradients are assumed to have only bounded conditional second moments. We show that unclipped AdaGrad can become \emph{anisotropically miscalibrated}: under heavy-tailed noise, the adaptive denominator can learn the geometry of rare noise shocks rather than the local curvature of the objective, leading to a persistent directional distortion that blocks finite-horizon Euclidean progress. We then prove that clipping repairs this failure mode. Our main result is a finite-horizon high-probability guarantee for the original non-lagged AdaGrad update, yielding $\frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla f(x_t)\|^2=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{d\big(\sqrt{\log T} + \log \frac{1}δ\big)}{\sqrt{T}}\right),$ and hence $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ complexity. This shows that, for AdaGrad under heavy-tailed noise, clipping is a structural stabilizer of the adaptive geometry rather than merely a robustness heuristic.

</details>


### [178] [Fixed Points Without Fixed Diffusion: Implicit Neural Sheaves for Convergent Test-Time Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30277)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SheafDEQ，一种带自适应神经层（sheaf）传播的子齐次深度均衡架构，在保证唯一均衡与收敛性的同时提升隐式 GNN 的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式 GNN（IGNN）依赖对循环更新的约束来保证均衡唯一且可达到，限制了均衡处的可用变换，表达能力受限。核心问题：能否在保留均衡范式优势的同时，通过更丰富的边依赖变换提升表达力？

Method: SheafDEQ 采用子齐次深度均衡框架，结合可学习的矩阵值 sheaf 限制映射，可对齐、混合或反转邻居表示。在温和正则条件下证明其存在唯一均衡，且从任意正初始化经不动点迭代全局收敛；收缩性进一步保证在有界通信滞后下的收敛。

Result: 在需重复非局部聚合的分布式推理任务上，SheafDEQ 在 Sums、MNIST Terrain、Coordinates 上优于固定传播的隐式基线；在连接逐渐异配的社区检测任务上也更优。持续迭代诊断显示 100 次迭代后残差下降，初始化尺度 0.001–10 下预测敏感度低；延迟更新实验显示对有界通信滞后敏感度低。

Conclusion: SheafDEQ 通过自适应神经 sheaf 传播，在保持均衡隐式 GNN 唯一性与收敛性保证的同时提升了表达能力，并在分布式推理与异配社区检测任务上取得改进，且对初始化和通信滞后具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Implicit Graph Neural Networks (IGNNs) define node representations as fixed points of message-passing operators, enabling effectively infinite-depth propagation, iteration-independent parameterization, and flexible test-time computation. Yet these benefits depend on the equilibrium being unique and attainable by fixed-point iteration. Existing constructions often impose constraints on recurrent updates to obtain these guarantees, limiting the transformations available at equilibrium. This raises a central question: can IGNNs gain expressiveness through richer, edge-dependent transformations while retaining the inherent strengths of their equilibrium formulation? We introduce SheafDEQ, a subhomogeneous deep-equilibrium architecture with adaptive neural-sheaf propagation. Its learned, matrix-valued sheaf restriction maps can align, mix, or reverse neighbouring representations. Under mild regularity conditions, we prove that SheafDEQ admits a unique equilibrium reached globally by fixed-point iteration from any positive initialization. Contractivity further guarantees convergence under bounded communication staleness. We evaluate SheafDEQ on distributed-inference tasks requiring repeated nonlocal aggregation and on community detection whose rewiring increasingly favours cross-community interactions. SheafDEQ improves over fixed-propagation implicit baselines on Sums, MNIST Terrain, and Coordinates, and on community detection as connectivity becomes increasingly heterophilic. Continued-iteration diagnostics show decreasing residuals and low prediction sensitivity after 100 iterations for initialization scales from $0.001$ to $10$, while delayed-update experiments show low sensitivity to bounded communication staleness.

</details>


### [179] [Neural Ideals and Neural Codes: An Algebraic Framework for Neural Network Classification and Feature Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30279)
*Venkata Subbaiah Yerrapati,Rahul Dixit,Ajay Kumar Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个代数框架，通过"神经理想"研究分类神经网络隐藏层捕获的特征，并给出计算方法、稳定化定理及可视化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络隐藏层所捕获的特征是机器学习的根本挑战，尽管神经网络在各类分类任务中取得广泛成功。

Method: 提出代数框架，建立神经网络与神经理想之间的对应关系，设计神经理想的计算算法，并证明使其可近似计算的稳定化定理；基于该框架提出识别与解释各隐藏层神经元所捕获特征的算法。

Result: 在MNIST手写数字数据集上验证了方法的实际效果，结果表明神经理想作为数学与计算工具在分析神经网络特征方面具有关键作用；并开发了交互式软件可视化每个神经元捕获的特征。

Conclusion: 神经理想为理解和解释神经网络隐藏层特征提供了有效的数学与计算工具，相关可视化工具已开源。

Abstract: Understanding the features captured by the hidden layers of neural networks is a fundamental challenge in machine learning, despite their widespread success across various classification problems. In this work, we propose an algebraic framework for examining neural networks that model classification problems. Certain results, such as the correspondence between the neural network and neural ideals, algorithms for computing the neural ideals, and a stabilization theorem that enables approximation of the neural ideals, are first established. As an application to the framework, we present algorithms to identify and interpret the features captured by each hidden-layer neuron. Along with these theoretical developments, the practical performance has been demonstrated on the MNIST digit dataset, and the results highlight the pivotal role of neural ideals as a mathematical and computational tool for analyzing the features captured by neural networks. Further, we develop an interactive software that builds on the presented framework to visualize the features captured by each neuron. This tool is available at https://github.com/yvs1967/neural-network-representation-explorer

</details>


### [180] [Seasonal and Quantum-inspired Models for Neutron Monitor Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30281)
*Krishna Bhatia,Shalini Devendrababu,Srinjoy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在LMKS中子监测器时间序列上的多步预测复现研究表明，量子启发式KAN变体QiKAN取得最低预测误差，而简单Seasonal Naive基线也极具竞争力，说明强季节性先验对周期性强科学信号预测可能优于复杂模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高度周期性科学监测时间序列，复杂深度模型是否真正优于简单季节性基线尚不清晰，需要可复现的实证评估来厘清季节性与低维函数先验的作用。

Method: 在LMKS中子监测器序列上开展多步预测，覆盖Seasonal Naive、LSTM、TCN、N-BEATS、KAN及量子启发变体QiLSTM、QiKAN；给出数据特征、诊断分析、预处理与训练流程，并以MAE和RMSE聚合评估所有模型。

Result: 快速运行结果显示，量子启发式KAN变体QiKAN的聚合预测误差最低，简单Seasonal Naive基线表现也相当接近，与多种复杂序列模型相当。

Conclusion: 对高度周期性的科学监测信号，具有强季节性先验或低维函数先验的模型可匹配甚至超越复杂序列架构，未来值得探索简约、可分解的函数逼近器。

Abstract: We present a focused and reproducible study of multi-horizon forecasting on the Lomnicky Stit neutron monitor (LMKS) time series. Our evaluation suite covers simple seasonal baselines, modern deep sequence models, and functional and quantum-inspired architectures, including Seasonal Naive, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN), N-BEATS, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), and two quantum-inspired variants, QiLSTM and QiKAN. We describe the dataset characteristics, diagnostic analysis, preprocessing pipeline, and training procedures, and report aggregate point-forecast performance using mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) for all evaluated models. Our quick-run results indicate that the quantum-inspired KAN variant, QiKAN, achieves the lowest aggregate forecasting error among the evaluated configurations, while the simple Seasonal Naive baseline remains remarkably competitive. These results suggest that, for highly periodic scientific monitoring time series, models incorporating strong seasonal or low-dimensional functional priors can match or outperform substantially more complex sequence architectures. The findings motivate further investigation of parsimonious and decomposable function approximators for forecasting periodic scientific signals.

</details>


### [181] [When Does Advection-Aware Graph Nowcasting Help? A Controlled Study of Distributed Solar Ramp Forecasting with a Self-Supervised Cloud-Motion Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30286)
*Phillip Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在合成数据集上验证：显式平流图对光伏功率爬坡预测无明显优势；准确运动矢量作为输入特征比图结构更重要；提出的自监督云运动估计器角度误差2-4°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式光伏/辐照度传感器网络上云致功率爬坡的短期预测是电网运营痛点，一个自然的想法是构建"平流感知"图神经网络。

Method: 使用已知风场的受控合成测试台；对比显式平流图与静态/学习邻接时空GNN；提出位置感知编码器，仅用多滞后光流重建目标（退火核）自监督训练云运动估计器，并将其冻结后向量输入预测器。

Result: (i) 真实互相关CMV下，显式平流图不优于普通GNN；(ii) 完美CMV约一半收益仅来自将运动矢量作为输入特征；(iii) 仅当v*H位移落在传感器网络内时平流才有帮助。自监督估计器中位角误差2-4°，比互相关法好2-4倍；中等风速下缩小约60%的oracle-CMV RMSE差距（降低8-15%）。空间相干概率头为负结果。

Conclusion: 所有结论基于单一合成仿真器，真实网络验证是必要的下一步，并给出验证方案。

Abstract: Short-term forecasting of cloud-induced power ramps across a network of distributed photovoltaic (PV) or irradiance sensors is a recognised pain point for grid operators. A natural idea is to make the graph neural network (GNN) advection-aware: connect each site to the sites upwind of it, with edge time-lags set by the cloud-motion vector (CMV), so that a ramp is propagated forward before it physically arrives. Using a controlled synthetic testbed with a known wind field, we show that (i) with a realistic cross-correlation CMV estimate, an explicit advection graph does not beat a plain static or learned-adjacency spatiotemporal GNN; (ii) roughly half of the benefit available from a perfect CMV comes simply from providing an accurate motion vector as an input feature, not from graph structure; and (iii) advection helps only when the advective displacement over the forecast horizon, v*H, fits inside the sensor network. Motivated by (ii), we introduce a small self-supervised cloud-motion estimator -- a position-aware encoder trained only on a multi-lag optical-flow reconstruction objective with an annealed kernel -- that recovers the true wind vector to 2-4 degrees median angular error, 2-4x better than the classical cross-correlation method across every wind regime. Freezing this estimator and feeding its vector to the forecaster closes about 60% of the oracle-CMV RMSE gap at moderate wind (8-15% RMSE reduction over no advection), with no external wind data. We also report a negative result for a spatially-coherent probabilistic head. All claims are established on a single synthetic simulator; we discuss why real-network validation is the necessary next step and outline it.

</details>


### [182] [NeuralCert: certified computational discovery of extremal mathematical constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30296)
*Mark Patrick Roeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"发现-认证"框架：用可分离紧凑表示学习高维变分试验函数，经谱诊断与剪枝后通过多模评估进行精确认证，可在普通PC上运行，并在三类极值问题中展示神经网络优化对严格数学的三种贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络求解数学问题日益流行，但随机模型本身无法提供数学上的精确性，缺乏严格性与可验证性。

Method: 发现-认证框架：以紧凑可分离表示学习高维变分试验函数，进行谱诊断与剪枝，再借助多模评估实现精确认证，使数值证明完全显式且可独立验证。

Result: 在三个极值问题上，神经优化以三种方式助力严格数学：发现改进构造、揭示导向证明的经验不变量、暴露其几何激发新解析或数值表示的优化壁垒。

Conclusion: 结果表明可通向AI辅助数学的路径：灵活的计算发现与精确认证成为单一严格工作流中互补的两部分。

Abstract: Neural networks are becoming popular in solving mathematical problems, but stochastic models do not provide mathematical exactness by themselves.
