Once the developer initiates a distributed learning job by toggling DP, SL, HA, and KD in the admin dashboard, the system dispatches the job to a set of Android clients and Dockerized helper aggregators. In the demonstration, these mechanisms run live across configurations. Then, the clients train the model locally and return their updates. The trained model is served to a consumer-side Android application that performs on-device WuW detection on a live microphone stream. In particular, the conference attendees will be invited to speak the trigger phrase and monitor in real time the per-class confidence and inference latency. Finally, we release the source code and short video walkthroughs of these configurations.

</details>


### [256] [SAGE: A sampling-aware global evaluation benchmark for species distribution modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31082)
*Emilia Arens,Nina van Tiel,Robin Zbinden,Damien Robert,Lukas Drees,Chiara Vanalli,Benjamin Kellenberger,Niklaus E. Zimmermann,Loïc Pellissier,Devis Tuia,Jan Dirk Wegner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE基准，用GBIF训练、sPlotOpen评估5771种植物的SDM，按采样努力和相对流行度分组物种，揭示深度学习模型对稀有/低频物种的优势及偏差校正的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物种分布模型(SDM)准确性随数据和模型变化，需知道哪些物种的模型可信。DeepSDM虽能联合建模数千物种，但平均性能掩盖物种级差异，尤其稀有物种，且记录偏差使出现计数误导。评估多物种SDM需解决这些因素。

Method: 构建Sampling-Aware Global Evaluation (SAGE)基准：GBIF记录用于训练，sPlotOpen植被样方提供存在-缺失评估，覆盖5771种植物。提出按采样努力(范围采样密度)和相对流行度(记录频率)两属性分组物种的评估框架，对比单物种SDM与多物种DeepSDM。

Result: 随机森林和DeepSDM整体表现最佳，但互不占优：DeepSDM在不常记录物种上优于单物种SDM，对采样充分物种无明显优势。该优势仅在将空间稀疏化和重加权等偏差校正实践迁移到深度学习时才出现。

Conclusion: SAGE能识别特定方法有益的物种和数据条件，支持开发更透明、生态可信的SDM。

Abstract: Knowing where species occur is fundamental for biodiversity research and conservation. Species distribution models (SDMs) link species observations to environmental conditions to estimate their spatial distribution. However, accuracy varies with the underlying data and models, making it essential to know for which species models can be trusted. Deep-learning-based SDMs ("DeepSDMs") now jointly model thousands of species, drawing on hundreds of millions of community-science records. At this scale, averaging performance hides substantial species-level variability, particularly for rare species, often of greatest conservation concern. Records are also strongly biased, making occurrence counts misleading. Accounting for these factors is essential for a reliable and informative evaluation of multi-species SDMs. Here, we introduce a Sampling-Aware Global Evaluation (SAGE) benchmark, combining GBIF records for training with sPlotOpen vegetation plots for presence-absence evaluation across 5771 plant species. We propose an evaluation framework that groups species based on two properties, sampling effort and relative prevalence, which describe how densely a species' range is sampled and how frequently the species is recorded. Evaluating single-species SDMs and multi-species DeepSDMs, we find that Random Forests and DeepSDMs perform best overall, but neither dominates: DeepSDMs outperform single-species SDMs for infrequently recorded species while offering no consistent advantage for well-sampled ones. Crucially, this advantage emerges only when established bias-correction practices, such as spatial thinning and reweighting, are carried over to the deep-learning setting. SAGE helps identify the species and data conditions for which a given approach is beneficial, thereby supporting the development of more transparent and ecologically credible SDMs. Data and code: https://earens.github.io/sage/

</details>


### [257] [Block Sparse Attention with Log-Linear Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31093)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PISA，一种金字塔Top-K块稀疏注意力机制，通过粗到细层级选择将注意力复杂度降至O(N log N)，并配以硬件友好的Triton内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型受限于自注意力的二次成本；块稀疏注意力虽高效，但传统块选择需对所有query-block对打分，仍是序列长度的二次复杂度。

Method: 构建粗到细的键层级结构（通过池化得到O(log N)层），从最粗层开始用LogSumExp打分在有限候选集上做Top-K选择，逐层细化至最细层；开发训练与推理的硬件感知Triton内核，融合层级路由与LogSumExp打分，避免显式实例化query-key打分矩阵。

Result: 总体复杂度为O(N log N)。在语言建模任务上，相比基线在常识推理等基准上性能相当，并在检索任务上取得更好结果。

Conclusion: PISA通过金字塔Top-K选择有效解决了块稀疏注意力的选择瓶颈，兼顾效率与性能，在检索任务上尤为突出。

Abstract: Scaling language models to long contexts is limited by the quadratic cost of self-attention. Block sparse attention offers an efficient alternative, but selecting the retained blocks remains a bottleneck. Conventional block selection requires scoring all query-block pairs and therefore remains quadratic in sequence length. To address this issue, we propose PISA, a block-sparse attention mechanism that employs a pyramid Top-$K$ selection strategy. The main idea is to gradually narrow down the candidates across different levels, making it more efficient to find the most relevant keys. Specifically, we construct a coarse-to-fine hierarchy of keys and perform selection from the coarsest level. At each level, LogSumExp scoring is applied to a bounded candidate set to select candidates for the next finer level, continuing until the finest level is reached. Through pooling, we construct $O(\log N)$ levels of keys, yielding an overall complexity of $O(N\log N)$, where $N$ denotes the sequence length. We develop hardware-aware Triton kernels for both training and inference, fusing hierarchical routing and LogSumExp scoring without materializing the query-key score matrix. We further evaluate our method on language modeling tasks. Compared with the baseline, our method achieves comparable performance on benchmarks such as commonsense reasoning while delivering better results on retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [258] [The Residual Stream's Effective Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31098)
*Barak Gahtan,Ido Galil,Alex M. Bronstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效深度 (Deff)，将 transformer 的逐层残差流视为离散时间过程，通过表征相似度随层距的衰减聚合成单一标量诊断。16 个仅解码器模型中 15 个测量值低于正交更新参考上限 F_L=2L/(L+1)，该差距主要反映残差更新相关性，而非深度未被利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分残差累积的结构性后果与经验性后果，诊断 transformer 深度是否被有效利用。

Method: 定义有效深度 Deff：将逐层残差流建模为离散时间过程，度量表征相似度随层距的衰减，并将衰减轮廓聚合成一个标量。使用正交更新闭式参考 F_L=2L/(L+1) 作为基准，并设计位置0、token归一化、top-PC 对照以及残差携带干预实验。

Result: 16 个模型中 15 个默认测量值低于 F_L（Qwen3.5: 32–44%，OLMo-2: 40–41%，Pythia: 23–28%）。对照显示差距并非源于持续初始状态或更新规模失衡，而主要是残差更新相关性的标定特征。token归一化或去除 top-1-PC 后 16 个模型全部低于参考。OLMo-2 早期即建立该 regime 并在 5T token 内稳定，Pythia-1.4B 呈递减轨迹。

Conclusion: Deff 应被理解为全局累积状态诊断指标，而非能力评分或剪枝方法。残差携带干预支持其机理解释。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{effective depth} ($\Deff$), a scalar diagnostic that treats the layer-wise residual stream of a transformer as a discrete-time process, measures how representation similarity decays with layer distance, and aggregates that profile into one number. Across sixteen decoder-only language models, $\Deff$ separates a structural consequence of residual accumulation from an empirical one: even maximally diverse orthogonal updates have the closed-form reference $F_L = 2L/(L+1)<2$, yet fifteen of sixteen default measurements lie below $F_L$ (Qwen3.5: 32--44\%, OLMo-2: 40--41\%, Pythia: 23--28\%). Matched references show that the gap is not caused by the persistent initial state or update-size imbalance, but is largely a calibrated signature of correlated residual updates rather than evidence that depth is unused. Symmetric position-0, token-normalisation, and top-PC controls show the regime is not reducible to BOS or top-PC artefacts: the lone above-reference default outlier joins the same regime, and all sixteen models are sub-reference after token-normalisation or top-1-PC removal. Intermediate checkpoints show that the regime is established early in OLMo-2 and stable through 5T tokens, while Pythia-1.4B follows a distinct decreasing trajectory. A controlled residual-carry intervention supports the mechanism, and $\Deff$ is best read as a \emph{global} accumulated-state diagnostic, not as a capability score or pruning method.

</details>


### [259] [Bayesian Optimization with Fisher Information Geometry: Gradient Bounds and Trust-Region Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31107)
*Saksham Kiroriwal,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用信息几何视角分析贝叶斯优化，通过Fisher信息度量导出输入空间的敏感度张量，解释高维BO梯度消失，并据此提出FITR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维贝叶斯优化中常出现梯度消失等问题，现有启发式方法（如RAASP、长度尺度缩放）缺乏统一解释。作者希望通过信息几何提供一个统一的理论分析框架。

Method: 将Fisher信息度量通过代理后验映射拉回到输入空间，得到局部敏感度张量，并据此推导可重参数化采集函数梯度的上界。基于该分析提出FITR：用局部pullback-Fisher权重替代基于长度尺度的缩放，构建基于信赖域的BO方法。

Result: 理论分析解释了高维BO的梯度消失并统一了若干启发式方法；在SE核GP基准上，FITR表现有竞争力；方法可推广到非各向同性代理，但收益更依赖具体任务。

Conclusion: 信息几何为BO提供了有效的分析工具，FITR作为通用方法不依赖显式长度尺度的GP核，具备一定竞争力与可推广性。

Abstract: We study Bayesian optimization (BO) through the lens of information geometry. Pulling back the Fisher information metric through the surrogate posterior map yields a local sensitivity tensor on the input space, which leads to an upper bound on the gradient of reparameterizable acquisition functions. This view explains vanishing-gradient behavior in high-dimensional BO and provides a common interpretation of heuristics such as RAASP and dimension-scaled lengthscales. Building on this analysis, we propose FITR, a trust-region-based BO method that replaces lengthscale-based scaling by local pullback-Fisher weights. FITR is not restricted to GP kernels with explicit lengthscales. On GP benchmarks with an SE kernel, experiments show competitive performance using FITR. The proposed method also easily generalizes to non-isotropic surrogates, although the gains are more task-dependent in that setting.

</details>


### [260] [From Shortcut Learning to Discrete Neural Insertion Sort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31114)
*Konstantinos Mylonas,Thrasyvoulos Spyropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以插入排序为案例研究神经算法推理是否真正遵循算法执行，发现CLRS30基线模型依赖捷径，并提出离散神经插入排序模型实现强长度泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确的最终输出和中间监督并不能证明模型遵循了预期的算法执行过程，需要研究模型是否真正学会了算法的忠实执行。

Method: 分析CLRS30基线NAR，发现hint目标优化较弱且准确率低，且许多中间表示在参考执行结束前就已被解码为有序序列，表明模型学到了捷径。据此提出离散神经插入排序（Discrete Neural Insertion Sort）：将序列表示为链，分离标量交换与控制状态转移，并在每次处理器步骤后将节点表示投影回离散状态。

Result: 仅在长度为16的序列上训练，模型在长度64和128上达到100%有序序列准确率。消融实验表明，仅靠离散化和图结构不足够：若没有对全局内循环状态的额外监督，模型甚至在训练长度上也会失败。

Conclusion: 离散执行可以支持强长度泛化，但学习忠实的算法执行需要针对问题特定的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Neural algorithmic reasoning aims to train neural networks to follow known algorithms and generalize beyond the input sizes seen during training. However, correct final outputs and intermediate supervision do not necessarily show that a model follows the intended execution. We study this problem using insertion sort. Our analysis of the CLRS30 baseline NAR shows that the hint objective is weakly optimized and that hint accuracy remains low. Moreover, many intermediate representations can already be decoded into sorted sequences before the reference insertion-sort execution terminates, suggesting that the model learns a shortcut to the final output. Motivated by these findings, we introduce Discrete Neural Insertion Sort. Our model represents the sequence as a chain, separates scalar exchanges from control-state transitions, and projects node representations back to discrete states after every processor step. When trained only on sequences of length 16, the model achieves $100\%$ sorted-sequence accuracy on sequences of length 64 and 128. However, an ablation shows that discretization and graph structure alone are insufficient: without additional supervision of the global inner-loop state, the model fails even at the training length. Our results show that discrete execution can support strong length generalization, while also highlighting the problem-specific inductive bias required to learn a faithful algorithmic execution.

</details>


### [261] [Frame the adversary: a structure-aware attack methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31128)
*Vicky Kouni,Stelios Perrakis,Francis Bach,Pascal Frossard,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于优化的频率域对抗攻击方法，将攻击建模为结构化非正交变换约束集上的加权ℓ2投影，实现了有几何解释、可控且可迁移的攻击生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率域攻击能利用跨架构共享的频谱敏感性，揭示比空间扰动更深的脆弱性，适合安全关键场景的鲁棒性评估；但现有方法通常并非源自显式刻画变换域结构的优化问题，缺乏理论支撑。

Method: 引入与高度结构化非正交变换相关的扰动约束集（具灵活、非预定义频率处理能力），通过专用优化框架构造攻击；证明攻击解为该约束集上的加权ℓ2投影，从而给出清晰的几何刻画，并保证优化目标与扰动约束一致。

Result: 在标准数据集上针对预训练模型和对抗鲁棒模型进行评估，攻击作为结构化扰动集上的优化问题解，效果显著，且在不同、未见过的架构间仍具强迁移性。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为设计与分析变换域攻击的理论基线，针对模型的基本脆弱性而非鲁棒性文献中常研究的特定架构伪影。

Abstract: Frequency-based adversarial attacks have recently grown popular by exploiting spectral sensitivities shared across neural architectures. Unlike spatial perturbations, frequency-based attacks expose deeper vulnerabilities, making them especially valuable for robust evaluation of safety-critical and security-sensitive applications. Yet, existing approaches are typically not derived as solutions to an optimization problem that explicitly captures transform-domain structure. In this paper, we propose a methodology for crafting principled frequency-based adversarial attacks, via a dedicated optimization framework. A cornerstone of our method hinges on the introduction of a perturbation constraint set, tied to highly structured non-orthogonal transforms, well-known for their flexible, non-predefined frequency handling. We prove that the attacks emerge as weighted $\ell_2$-projections onto this set, yielding a general and controlled attack generation mechanism. By this, we provide a clear geometric attack characterization, ensuring alignment between the optimization objective and the perturbation constraint. We assess our framework on standardized datasets, for pretrained and adversarially robust models. Results highlight that our attacks, being solutions to an optimization problem, over a structured perturbation set, are highly effective, even across different, unseen architectures. Our methodology could serve as a theoretical baseline for designing and analyzing transformed-based attacks, targeting fundamental model vulnerabilities, instead of mere architecture-specific artifacts typically studied in the robustness literature.

</details>


### [262] [CRNDiff: Count-Native Diffusion Framework via Chemical Reaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31149)
*Yuxuan Qiu,Praful Gagrani,Tetsuya J Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRNDiff将计数值空间的扩散模型与推理时条件生成结合，通过出生-死亡过程的闭式转移核实现高效反向采样，并用倾斜Feynman-Kac调控从冻结生成器中采样稀有细胞亚群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA等科学测量是非负整数计数，而连续状态扩散模型用连续坐标近似离散结构，存在不匹配问题；同时从冻结生成器中采样稀有亚群（条件生成）也很困难，重要性权重易集中。

Method: 基于随机化学反应网络（CRN）计数原生马尔可夫跳跃过程，提出CRNDiff，将计数空间扩散与推理时条件生成结合。独立出生-死亡实例化给出前向加噪的闭式转移核，支持FFBS反向采样，并支持数据驱动的终端加噪时间选择（无需验证扫描）。引入倾斜Feynman-Kac（FK）调控，在FK粒子校正前倾斜后验边缘，缓解稀有目标群的重要性权重集中，无需重训练即可从冻结生成器采样目标亚群。

Result: 在人类心脏细胞图谱scRNA数据上，CRNDiff在三个评估目标群体中条件保真度最高，且目标越稀有平均纯度边际越大；生成细胞保留标记级差异表达结构；用生成细胞替代真实训练细胞后下游分类性能接近真实数据参考。

Conclusion: CRNDiff通过计数空间扩散与FK调控，实现了对稀有细胞亚群的高保真条件生成，在scRNA-seq任务上优于对比生成模型，并具下游应用价值。

Abstract: Scientific measurements such as single-cell RNA (scRNA) sequencing often take the form of nonnegative integer counts, whereas continuous-state diffusion models approximate this discrete structure using continuous coordinates. Building on stochastic chemical reaction networks (CRNs), a class of count-native Markov jump processes, we introduce CRNDiff, a structured framework that combines count-space diffusion with inference-time conditioning on rare subpopulations. An independent birth--death instantiation yields a closed-form transition kernel for forward noising. This kernel enables reverse sampling via forward-filtering backward-sampling (FFBS) and supports data-driven selection of the terminal noising time, eliminating the need for a validation sweep. This tractability also lets us introduce tilted Feynman--Kac (FK) steering, a method for sampling target subpopulations from a frozen generator without retraining. By tilting posterior marginals before FK particle correction, steering mitigates importance-weight concentration when the target population is rare. Using scRNA-seq data from the human heart cell atlas, we test the ability of CRNDiff to generate cell-type-specific distributions. Across the three evaluated target populations, CRNDiff achieves the highest conditional fidelity among the evaluated generative models, with larger mean purity margins for rarer target populations. Generated cells preserve marker-level differential-expression structure. Replacing real training cells for the target classes with generated cells yields downstream classification performance approaching that of the real-data reference.

</details>


### [263] [Teacher-Anchored Selection of Post-Training Quantized Models under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31155)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez,Muhammad Shahzad,Xia Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无目标标签或标签稀缺、存在域偏移的情况下，如何从压缩模型族中选择合适的部署候选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩模型会形成一族部署候选，域偏移下最优模型未必是压缩最狠的那个；而目标标签缺失或稀缺使选择困难，因此需要研究无标签/少标签的模型选择机制。

Method: 以固定候选族和教师模型为设定，比较最小教师失真规则与各类估计器；分析置信度估计器与输出分布估计器；对二次族给出精确二次恒等式；并证明对称损坏下若系数和与候选无关，则线性准则中依赖标签的部分会被共同因子缩放。

Result: 最小教师失真几乎像常数规则，总选同一8位、逐通道、未裁剪配置，但并不最小化目标交叉熵；CNN过度自信崩溃时置信度估计器排序近乎反向；输出分布估计器与教师相对锚点一致，并在一个架构上超过它。锚定在最小标签预算下降低平均遗憾，但超过25个标签后优势消退。

Conclusion: 无标签模型选择中，失真稳定但未必最优；输出分布类估计器和锚定策略在少标签时更有效，但优势有限，且未给出选择遗憾的界。

Abstract: Compressing a trained model yields a family of deployment candidates, and under domain shift the most compressed one need not be the one to deploy. We study selection over such a family, with candidates and teacher fixed and target labels absent or scarce. Two findings organize the label-free case. Minimum teacher distortion behaves almost as a constant rule, selecting the same eight-bit, per-channel, unclipped configuration in every run, which does not minimize empirical target cross-entropy. Established estimators divide sharply: in the overconfident-collapse regime of the CNN families, confidence-based estimators order the family close to backwards, and the diagnostics that identify it need the labels the setting denies, while output-distribution estimators match the teacher-relative anchor and on one architecture beat it. Distortion is nonetheless stable, so a supervised term can move selection away from it. Combining the two, we give exact quadratic identities for a canonical quadratic analogue of the family. We also show that under symmetric corruption the label-dependent part of a criterion linear in the label indicator is multiplied by one common factor whenever its coefficient sums are candidate-invariant, a class holding teacher contrasts and accuracy but not cross-entropy. These characterize the score's components without bounding selection regret. Across one hundred and thirty-four candidate families, one per independently trained convolutional or Vision Transformer teacher, anchoring reduces mean regret at the smallest label budget in every setting, an advantage that fades beyond twenty-five labels.

</details>


### [264] [Bayesian Tensor Autoencoder with Physics-informed Predictive Prior for Multi-dimensional Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31157)
*Jianan Liu,Chunguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PPPTAE框架，将预测先验引入基于重建的张量自编码器，以实现多维（张量）时间序列异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序异常检测方法多限于单/多变量时间序列，处理多维时间序列时需reshape，破坏内在相关性；且重建式与预测式AE各用不同信息，未能充分利用。

Method: 以张量AE为骨干避免破坏相关性；通过贝叶斯融合将预测先验引入重建式AE；并在预测先验的神经网络中引入物理规律（张量低秩分解规则）以缓解过泛化。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合重建与预测信息并通过物理先验约束AE，可有效提升多维时间序列异常检测性能。

Abstract: Multi-dimensional time series, inherently tensorial, are common in practice. Despite great progress in time series anomaly detection, most existing methods are confined to uni-/multi-variate time series. When handling multi-dimensional time series using these methods, reshaping operations are required, which inevitably break the intrinsic correlations and thus lead to performance degradation. In uni-/multi-variate time series anomaly detection, AutoEncoders (AEs) are widely adopted and generally categorized into reconstruction-based and prediction-based AEs. The reconstruction-based AE utilizes the current observation for reconstruction, while the prediction-based AE utilizes the historical information to predict the current observation. Thus, the two AEs utilize different information. To bridge the gap between reconstruction-based and prediction-based AEs, so as to fully leverage the available information and thus further enhance performance, we propose a predictive prior and incorporate it into the reconstruction-based AE. It may not be very difficult to conceive this idea, but designing the predictive prior so that it can work for tensor anomaly detection is non-trivial. Specifically, to avoid breaking the intrinsic correlations within the multi-dimensional time series, we use the tensor AE as the backbone. To incorporate the predictive prior into the reconstruction-based AE, we propose a Bayesian fusion approach and our analysis reveals that this approach can enhance the modeling capability of the model for normal data. To mitigate the over-generalization problem of AE, we incorporate physical laws, i.e. tensor low-rank decomposition rules, into the neural networks in the predictive prior, leading to the Physics-informed Predictive Prior Tensor AE (PPPTAE) framework. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [265] [I Act Therefore I Am: When Is JEPA's Action-Conditioning Enough to Learn Causal Mechanisms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31161)
*Yuhang Liu,Zhuo Huang,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究JEPA何时能恢复潜在的因果状态，提出信息论目标与可识别性条件，并实例化为A-JEPA，实验验证状态恢复与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA可学习动作条件下的未来预测表示，但准确预测不一定意味着恢复产生观测动态的潜在因果状态，需明确其可识别性条件。

Method: 建立由潜在因果状态生成观测的潜变量模型，动作条件转移机制驱动动态；提出结合条件似然最大化与熵最大化的信息论目标；给出可识别性条件（关键是动作诱导的转移变化充分）；实例化为动作调制高斯加噪模型A-JEPA。

Result: 合成环境验证了可识别性条件及对适度违反的鲁棒性；视觉基准显示状态恢复改进并能迁移到未见转移机制。

Conclusion: JEPA在满足动作诱导变化等条件下可恢复潜在因果状态（至分量可逆变换与置换），A-JEPA为有效实现。

Abstract: Recent empirical and theoretical advances suggest that joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) may learn meaningful representations for action-conditioned prediction of future outcomes, thus becoming one of the foundational structures for world models. However, accurate prediction does not, in general, necessarily imply recovery of underlying causal states that give rise to the observed dynamics. This work investigates when and how JEPAs can recover the underlying causal states from observations. We first introduce a latent variable model, in which high-dimensional observations are generated from latent causal states whose dynamics are governed by action-conditioned transition mechanisms. Based on this formulation, we develop a general information-theoretic objective that combines conditional likelihood maximization for learning transition dynamics with entropy maximization for preserving latent state information. We then establish identifiability conditions under which representations learned by this general objective recover the underlying latent causal states up to component-wise invertible transformations and permutation. One key condition for such identifiability is sufficient action-induced variation in the transition mechanisms. Guided by this finding, we instantiate the general objective with an action-modulated Gaussian additive-noise model, yielding action-modulated JEPA (A-JEPA). Experiments on synthetic environments verify the theoretical findings under the identifiability conditions and robustness to moderate violations, while visual benchmarks demonstrate improved state recovery and transfer to unseen transition mechanisms.

</details>


### [266] [Different Corruptions, Different Signals: Uncertainty and Loss in Federated Data Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31454)
*Bradley Scott,Zeqi Luo,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了联邦学习中的两种数据损坏检测信号：输入条件不确定性与预测-标签损失，发现两者各自在不同损坏类型下更有效——损失对持续标签翻转更强，期望熵不确定性对图像噪声更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的数据损坏可影响输入或标签，但尚不清楚输入条件不确定性与预测-标签损失是否对这两种损坏模式具有同等的检测能力，因此需要系统比较。

Method: 在Dirichlet非IID划分下，用ResNet-20在CIFAR-10和SVHN上测试两类信号：不确定性信号由学习的aleatoric方差估计、MC dropout方差和熵度量表征；损失信号基于所提供的标签计算。针对加性图像噪声与持续随机标签翻转两种损坏进行对比。

Result: 对持续随机标签翻转，预测-标签损失在CIFAR-10上AUC为0.85、SVHN上为0.95，而所有不确定性估计器均接近随机（0.49–0.50）；对图像噪声，期望熵不确定性高于随机（CIFAR-10为0.67，SVHN为0.66），损失表现相当（两者均为0.64）。

Conclusion: 不同信号在不同损坏下更有效：预测-标签损失适合持续标签翻转，期望熵不确定性适合图像噪声，且其优势随联邦范围内损坏比例上升而更明显。稳健的联邦学习数据质量评估应将信号与损坏类型匹配，而非仅依赖不确定性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) data corruption can affect either inputs or labels, but it remains unclear whether input-conditional uncertainty and prediction-label loss expose these corruption modes equally. This paper compares two corruption-detection signals in FL: input-conditional uncertainty and prediction-label loss. The uncertainty signal is characterised using a learned aleatoric variance estimate together with Monte Carlo (MC) dropout variance and entropy measures, while the loss is computed against the supplied label. We test these signals against additive image noise and persistent random label flips. On ResNet-20 with CIFAR-10 and SVHN under Dirichlet partitions with data that are not independent and identically distributed (non-IID), the two corruption types behave differently. For persistent random label flips, the within-client per-sample area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is 0.85 on CIFAR-10 and 0.95 on SVHN for prediction-label loss, while every uncertainty estimator stays at chance (0.49--0.50). This pattern is consistent with the model remaining confident in the underlying image despite the supplied label being wrong. For image noise, expected-entropy uncertainty rises above chance (0.67 on CIFAR-10 and 0.66 on SVHN), while loss responds comparably (0.64 on both). Each signal is therefore the stronger detector for a different corruption: the prediction-label loss for persistent label flips, and expected-entropy uncertainty for image noise, with its advantage becoming apparent as federation-wide corruption prevalence increases. Robust FL data-quality assessment should match the signal to the corruption rather than rely on uncertainty alone across corruption types.

</details>


### [267] [WorldTS: World Modeling for Multimodal Covariate-aware Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31162)
*Yuhan Zhu,Xiangfei Qiu,Hanyin Cheng,Wangmeng Shen,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldTS 提出基于世界模型的预测框架，将多模态协变量直接融入潜在状态的建模与演化，通过两阶段训练显著提升时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测多从观测空间直接学习映射，但观测仅提供系统动力学的部分信息；近期潜在空间方法虽有所改进，却未充分探索如何将外部多模态信息融入潜在状态的形成与演化。

Method: WorldTS 采用两阶段训练：首先以多模态协变量为条件，学习与预测相关的潜在状态动力学，得到编码的未来状态；随后冻结已学到的状态动力学，训练观测解码器将预测的未来状态映射回未来观测。

Result: 在 21 个真实世界数据集上进行的大量实验验证了 WorldTS 的有效性。

Conclusion: 将多模态协变量直接整合进世界模型式的潜在状态预测，是提升时间序列预测性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is typically framed as learning a direct mapping from historical to future observations in the observation space. However, sequences of observations generally provide only a partial view of the dynamics of the underlying system, with future observations being shaped by latent dynamics. Recent latent-space forecasting methods thus achieve improved performance by predicting future observations from latent-space representations of historical observations rather than directly forecasting future observations in the observation space. Next, while future observations are also shaped by external factors, how to incorporate external, often multimodal, information into forecasting, so that it can shape latent-state formation and evolution directly, remains underexplored. We propose WorldTS, a world-modeling based forecasting framework that integrates multimodal covariates directly into the forecasting to further improve forecasting performance. Specifically, WorldTS employs a two-stage training strategy. First, it learns forecasting-relevant latent state dynamics conditioned on multimodal covariates, yielding encoded future states. Next, the learned state dynamics are frozen, and an observation decoder is trained to map the predicted future states back to future observations. Extensive experiments on 21 real-world datasets offer insight into WorldTS and its effectiveness.

</details>


### [268] [Uncertainty-Aware Federated Learning for Infant Movement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31463)
*Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个用于婴儿运动分析与全身运动评估(GMA)的联邦学习框架，基于骨骼运动数据，并引入不确定性感知的联邦平均策略(UA-FedAvg)以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿运动分析可为神经发育障碍的早期识别提供生物标志物，但现有深度学习方法多依赖集中式训练，需要将多机构数据集中存储，受隐私、治理和数据共享限制，在临床场景中往往不可行。

Method: 提出联邦学习框架处理骨骼运动数据，以烦躁运动(fidgety movement)分类为用例。采用Monte Carlo Dropout估计推理时的预测不确定性，并据此提出不确定性感知联邦平均(UA-FedAvg)，将MC-Dropout导出的预测熵纳入聚合过程，按客户端预测不确定性调整其贡献。实验采用跨被试评估协议和三客户端联邦设置。

Result: 联邦学习相比独立训练的本地模型大幅提升分类性能，并接近集中式训练水平；UA-FedAvg及其结合验证损失的变体在各数据划分配置下总体优于传统FedAvg。

Conclusion: 联邦学习可在保护数据隐私的前提下实现接近集中式训练的婴儿运动分析性能，且不确定性感知聚合策略能进一步提升效果，为临床多机构协作提供可行方案。

Abstract: Infant movement analysis provides valuable biomarkers for the early identification of neurodevelopmental disorders. Recent advances in deep learning have enabled automated analysis of infant movements from video-derived skeletal representations, achieving performance comparable to expert assessment for tasks such as General Movement Assessment (GMA). However, most existing approaches rely on centralized training, requiring data from multiple institutions to be collected and stored at a single site. Such assumptions are often impractical in clinical settings due to privacy, governance, and data-sharing constraints. To address these challenges, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first federated learning framework for automated infant movement analysis and General Movement Assessment using skeletal motion data. As a clinically relevant use case, the proposed framework is evaluated on fidgety movement classification. To quantify model confidence, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout is employed to estimate predictive uncertainty during inference. Building upon this, we propose an Uncertainty-Aware Federated Averaging (UA-FedAvg) strategy that incorporates predictive entropy derived from MC-Dropout into the federated aggregation process, enabling client contributions to be adjusted according to their predictive uncertainty. Experiments were conducted using a cross-subject evaluation protocol under a three-client federated learning setting. Results demonstrate that federated learning substantially improves classification performance compared with independently trained local models while achieving performance approaching that of centralized training. Furthermore, UA-FedAvg and its variant incorporating validation loss generally outperform conventional FedAvg across the evaluated data-split configurations.

</details>


### [269] [Audio emotion recognition for atypical hearing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31168)
*Ulysse Roussel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 博士研究探索非典型聆听情境下的音频情感识别，聚焦自闭症患者的听觉过敏。核心思路是利用声学特征理解情感，并通过小样本标注数据泛化情感反应。第一步是用LoRA微调CLAP基础模型，在神经典型聆听者的效价-唤醒度数据集上训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症患者的听觉过敏现象难以评估且因人而异，需要探索非典型聆听情境下的音频情感识别方法。

Method: 采用CLAP（对比语言-音频预训练）大基础模型，结合低秩适配（LoRA）进行微调，训练数据为神经典型聆听者的效价（valence）和唤醒度（arousal）数据集。

Result: 摘要聚焦于研究目标与方法设计（第一步微调），未报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 研究提出借助声学特征情感理解及小样本标注数据泛化能力，为自闭症听觉过敏评估提供新的AER研究方向，初步通过对CLAP的LoRA微调奠定基础。

Abstract: My doctoral work aims to explore Audio Emotion Recognition (AER) in the context of atypical listening. This research focuses on auditory hypersensitivity in people with autism, a phenomenon that is often difficult to evaluate and unique to each individual. Our core idea is to leverage our understanding of affect from acoustic traits, relying on the possibility of generalizing affective responses from a small amount of annotated data. As a first step, we fine-tune a large foundation model, Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) using low-rank adaptation (LoRA), trained on a valence and arousal dataset of neurotypical listeners.

</details>


### [270] [ALF: An Active Learning Framework for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31197)
*Shikha Surana,Alex Hawkins-Hooker,Olivia Gallup,Christoph Brunken,Jules Tilly,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍 ALF，一个模块化主动学习框架，支持离线和在线两种完整数据获取闭环，旨在推动科学发现中的数据高效获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习用于科学发现常受数据限制；在预算约束下获取高质量数据是关键。主动学习适用于实验、测量或仿真成本高昂的场景，但现有工具多只覆盖数据获取流程的一部分，且通常只侧重离线基准测试或在线部署之一。

Method: 提出 ALF，一个模块化主动学习框架，通过五个模块化组件运行完整数据获取闭环，并为离线（基于已有数据集，可控可复现实验）和在线（通过 oracle 在真实部署中获取新候选）两种场景提供统一 API。

Result: ALF 支持离线和在线两种完整数据获取流程，并以开源形式发布，地址为 https://github.com/instadeepai/alf。

Conclusion: ALF 旨在填补现有主动学习工具在离线与在线支持上的空白，为科学发现中的数据获取提供统一、模块化、开源的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning for scientific discovery is almost systematically data bound. Producing relevant high quality data, under budget constraints, is amongst the most promising ways to advance the field. Active learning (AL) offers promise wherever labelling requires expensive experiment, measurement, or simulation. Most existing tools cover only part of the data acquisition loop, and typically focus on either offline benchmarking or online deployment, but not both. We present ALF, a modular AL Framework that runs the full data acquisition loop via five modular components. One clear API for both settings: offline, against an existing dataset for controlled and reproducible experimentation; and online, against an oracle for acquiring new candidates in real-world deployments. ALF is open-source and available at https://github.com/instadeepai/alf.

</details>


### [271] [Self-Supervised Representation Learning: From Spectral Foundation Models to Auroral Emission Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31206)
*Matthieu Le Lain,Gaël Cessateur,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用223,000条无标注极光光谱对1D Vision Transformer做掩码自编码器预训练，学到的表征可恢复诊断所需的发射线强度比并超越专家特征与既往监督分类器；迁移实验表明光谱窗口匹配的预训练模型（光学）优于红外模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极光光谱仪可记录数十万条光谱，但仅几百条能由专家标注，大量无标注数据未被利用。作者希望借助自监督预训练充分挖掘这些无标注光谱。

Method: 对223,000条无标注光谱，用掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练一个1D Vision Transformer；随后进行线性探针与微调评估，并与专家特征、既往监督分类器对比；同时测试两个天文光谱基础模型和一个时间序列模型的迁移效果。

Result: 无监督表征可恢复发射线强度比（R² 0.91 vs 未训练对照0.77）；单线性探针即媲美13个专家特征。微调后宏观AP 88.5 vs 既往77.8，mAP 0.870；仅用10%标签即比从零训练同架构提升+0.159。归因显示模型利用了N2+两条谱带。迁移方面：红外训练的SpectraFM低于未训练对照，光学的SpecFormer接近但未达域内预训练水平。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练可有效利用无标注极光光谱，学到物理可解释且性能优越的表征；现有预训练模型不能直接替代，迁移效果取决于光谱窗口是否匹配。

Abstract: Auroral spectrographs such as the Auroral Spectrograph In Skibotn (ASIS) record hundreds of thousands of emission spectra, but only a few hundred can be labelled by an expert. To exploit the rest, we pretrain a 1D Vision Transformer with a masked autoencoder on 223,000 unlabelled spectra. Without labels, its representation recovers the emission-line intensity ratios that physicists use to diagnose the precipitating particles (R^2 0.91 vs. 0.77 for an untrained control) and, under one linear probe, classifies as well as 13 features designed by experts. Fine-tuned, the model outperforms the previous supervised auroral classifier on its own benchmark (macro-AP 88.5 vs. 77.8), reaches 0.870 mAP, and exceeds the same architecture trained from scratch by +0.159 with 10% of the labels; attribution shows that it uses both N2+ bands. Could an existing pretrained model replace it? Two astronomical spectral foundation models and a time-series model transfer according to their spectral window: SpectraFM, trained in the infrared, falls below the untrained control, whereas SpecFormer, trained in the optical, approaches in-domain pretraining without reaching it.

</details>


### [272] [Budgeted Quotient-Residual Guidance for Frozen Pocket-Conditioned Molecular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31222)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预算商残差引导(QRG)，一种推理时校正方法，使冻结的分子扩散采样器无需重训练即可响应距离、接触等商特征目标，并给出理论推导与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口袋条件分子扩散虽更新环境原子坐标，但许多先导优化目标表达在距离、接触、锚定子结构等商特征上，原始局部商梯度往往无法激活这些信号。

Method: QRG将商余向量提升为度量水平的环境方向，并用冻结采样器自身的步长范数设定信任预算：商几何决定方向，采样器运动约束尺度。推导了水平提升、闭式采样器预算更新、冻结反向步附近的KL/动力学解释、等变条件及带预算上限的分段与残差控制乘积预算拆分。

Result: 受控商任务证实采样器相对投递能激活原始局部商梯度休眠的信号。在冻结TargetDiff骨干上，CBGBench官方seed-0实验：Local-QRG将fragment growing有效性从0.815提至0.864，scaffold hopping从0.664提至0.707，linker design从0.681提至0.712；PredNext-QRG改善fragment/scaffold，linker近中性。新颖性保持1.000，多样性维持。

Conclusion: QRG为冻结分子采样器提供轻量级商感知推理路径，具有显式运行时核算，无需重训练或修改骨干，可按任务获得改进。

Abstract: Pocket-conditioned molecular diffusion updates ambient atom coordinates, but many lead-optimization objectives are expressed on quotient features such as distances, contacts, and anchored substructures. We introduce budgeted quotient-residual guidance (QRG), an inference-time correction that makes these quotient objectives active without retraining the molecular generator. QRG lifts quotient covectors to metric-horizontal ambient directions and delivers them through a trust budget set by the frozen sampler's own step norm: quotient geometry chooses the direction, while sampler motion bounds the scale. We derive the horizontal lift, closed-form sampler-budget update, KL/kinetic interpretation around a frozen reverse step, equivariance conditions, and a product-budget split for budget-capped section and residual controls. Controlled quotient tasks confirm that sampler-relative delivery activates signals that raw local quotient gradients leave dormant. On frozen TargetDiff backbones, official seed-0 CBGBench ligand-generation/editing sweeps show practical quality-runtime gains: Local-QRG improves validity from 0.815 to 0.864 on fragment growing, 0.664 to 0.707 on scaffold hopping, and 0.681 to 0.712 on linker design, while PredNext-QRG improves fragment/scaffold and remains near-neutral on linker. Novelty remains 1.000 and diversity is preserved in the matched multi-seed molecular slice, giving task-dependent improvements without sampler retraining or backbone modification. Overall, QRG provides a lightweight route to quotient-aware inference for frozen molecular samplers with explicit runtime accounting.

</details>


### [273] [Deterministic Regime Switching and Feasibility Inversion in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31250)
*Mahesh Reddy Pagadala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了DTR（动态张量重物化）在线驱逐策略在参考模拟器simrd上的细粒度确定性不稳定现象：LSTM trace中0.10%预算差异导致7.3倍开销差异；ResNet-32 trace中存在预算可行性反转（0.102-0.106区间OOM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTR是内存受限DNN训练的在线驱逐策略，但其对内存预算的细粒度敏感性尚未被系统研究。作者希望揭示预算微小变化引发的确定性不稳定病理。

Method: 使用DTR作者的公开执行trace在参考模拟器simrd上进行细粒度预算扫描，测量LSTM和ResNet-32上的不稳定现象，并通过DTR作者自带的变体做消融实验。

Result: LSTM上0.10%预算差异导致快慢执行机制开销差达7.3倍，慢机制由同一storage的广泛重复驱逐驱动（每storage驱逐数1.33→8.27，而不同被驱逐storage集合几乎不变，Jaccard 0.999）。ResNet-32上出现确定性可行性反转：ratio 0.101可行，0.102-0.106不可行(OOM)，0.107起又可行，直接原因是完全pinned的递归重物化前沿在驱逐完所有可驱逐张量后仍超预算。消融实验表明LSTM不稳定性与size-staleness联合评分项有关。

Conclusion: 这些是至少两种不同的预算敏感病理而非单一机制，作者区分了已验证结论与待验证假设。所有结果基于参考模拟器，生产运行时复现为未来工作，代码、插桩和原始结果随预印本公开。

Abstract: We report fine-grained, deterministic instability in Dynamic Tensor Rematerialization (DTR), an online eviction policy for memory-constrained DNN training, measured on the reference DTR simulator (simrd) using public execution traces. On an LSTM trace, memory budgets differing by 0.10% of unconstrained peak memory select fast and slow execution regimes whose overheads differ by as much as 7.3x; the slow regime is driven by broadly repeated re-eviction of the same storages (evictions per storage rise from 1.33 to 8.27 while the set of distinct evicted storages is essentially unchanged: 5,233 vs 5,236, with the two sets overlapping at Jaccard 0.999). On a ResNet-32 trace, a fine budget sweep reveals a deterministic feasibility inversion: the run is feasible at ratio 0.101, infeasible (OOM) across 0.102-0.106, and feasible again from 0.107. We trace the immediate cause of the OOM to a fully pinned recursive rematerialization frontier that exceeds the budget after every evictable tensor has been evicted. Ablations using the DTR authors' own variants implicate the joint size-staleness scoring term in the observed LSTM instability. We argue these are at least two distinct budget-sensitive pathologies rather than one mechanism, and we separate what is demonstrated from what remains hypothesised. All results concern the reference simulator; reproduction in a production runtime is future work. Code, instrumentation, and raw results accompany this preprint.

</details>


### [274] [Softmax Reparameterization for Output-Head Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31291)
*Asim Kadav,Christian Flores,Chirag Arora,Varun Kotte,Hongbo Zheng,Lan Yan,Priya Shanmugasundaram,Tracy Holloway King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 softmax 重参数化（post-training 预处理）：在量化前对输出头做功能等价变换，减去词表行均值的标量倍数，通过验证 KL 一维搜索选系数，降低小模型输出头量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型的输出头因大词表成为显著推理开销，需要降低其量化成本，同时保持全精度 softmax 分布不变。

Method: 对每个输出行减去词表行均值的标量倍数，按验证 KL 分别为 RTN、AW-MSE、GPTQ 选择系数；搜索包含原始头与固定均值中心化，并用秩一修正处理 soft-capping 等非线性 logit 路径。

Result: 七个头上 W4 增益集中在基线量化扭曲严重处：Phi-4-mini 上 AW-MSE KL 从 0.936 降至 0.256；增益在更强 GPTQ 校准下保持，且与精确逐通道缩放、仿射量化互补。冻结系数可迁移到 C4、OpenWebMath，18 次比较全胜、其余 6 次持平；W2 下收益更广。匹配残差分析显示保真度提升可伴随更大 logit 重建误差但 Fisher 加权代价更低。Phi 输出头 W4 使 batch-one 生成延迟相对 BF16 头降低 10.8%。

Conclusion: softmax 重参数化是一种简单、免训练的量化前处理，显著改善输出头量化保真度并带来实际推理延迟收益，且与现有量化方法互补。

Abstract: Large vocabularies make output heads a substantial inference cost in small language models. We propose softmax reparameterization, a post-training method that selects a functionally equivalent output head before quantization. The method subtracts a scalar multiple of the vocabulary-row mean from every output row and selects the coefficient by validation KL separately for RTN, activation-weighted MSE, and full-Hessian GPTQ. This one-dimensional search includes the original head and fixed mean-centering, preserves the full-precision softmax distribution, and leaves the trained decoder unchanged; a rank-one correction handles nonlinear logit paths such as soft-capping. Across seven heads, W4 gains concentrate where baseline quantization substantially distorts predictions: on Phi-4-mini, AW-MSE KL falls from 0.936 to 0.256. The gains survive stronger GPTQ calibration and remain complementary to exact per-channel scaling and affine quantization. Across four heads and three W4 quantizers, frozen WikiText-selected coefficients also transfer to C4 and OpenWebMath, outperforming mean-centering in all 18 comparisons where the frozen coefficient differs from $1$ and matching it in the remaining six. At W2, used as a compression stress test, benefits broaden across nearly the full model--quantizer matrix. Matched residual analysis shows that improved fidelity can accompany greater logit reconstruction error while reducing the residual's Fisher-weighted cost. For shift-compatible heads, reparameterization adds no inference operation and preserves packed W4 execution: with the decoder held in BF16, quantizing the Phi output head reduces batch-one generation latency by 10.8% relative to the BF16-head baseline.

</details>


### [275] [Benchmarking Attention for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31306)
*Maximilian Schambach,Clemens Biehl,Sam Thelin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为表格型上下文学习模型（TabPFN、Mitra、ConTextTab等）建立了可复现的二维注意力基准测试，在三种GPU（A100、H100、B200）上对比多种注意力后端（Torch SDPA、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM、SageAttention）的前向与反向吞吐量，揭示行/列注意力最优后端选择因硬件和模型细节而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格型in-context学习依赖行注意力与列注意力的交替计算，其序列长度、内存布局和隐藏维度与一维语言模型显著不同，但现有高效注意力研究多针对一维序列，二维表格场景尚属空白。

Method: 构建可复现基准测试，在A100/H100/B200三代GPU上，针对真实表格形状，测量Torch SDPA（efficient与cuDNN）、FlashAttention-2/3/4、vLLM和SageAttention等后端的前向与反向吞吐量。

Result: 各GPU代际定制的FlashAttention整体表现最佳；但在列注意力、较长序列且头维度处于交叉点时，cuDNN有时更优；推理专用后端中，SageAttention在行注意力及超过16k行的大序列上表现良好。最优后端取决于列/行注意力类型、硬件和模型细节。

Conclusion: 该可复现基准为表格原生注意力的未来改进奠定基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Tabular in-context learners such as TabPFN, Mitra, or ConTextTab rely on alternating row and column attention over 2D sequences of latent embeddings. These attention patterns differ markedly from the one-dimensional case in language models: row attention involves longer sequences while column attention operates on much shorter ones, and the strided memory layout of tabular data makes producing contiguous tensors costly. Moreover, the hidden dimensions used in current models are small compared to recent language models. Yet efficient attention has been studied mostly for one-dimensional sequences, leaving the two-dimensional tabular setting unexplored. To this end, we create a reproducible benchmarking setup and study the unique characteristics of tabular attention across several backends -- Torch SDPA (efficient and cuDNN), FlashAttention-2/3/4, and the inference-only backends vLLM and SageAttention -- measuring forward and backward throughput across realistic tabular shapes on three GPU generations (A100, H100, B200). We find that the optimal backend choice differs between column and row attention and varies across hardware as well as model specifics: While the FlashAttention implementations tailored for each GPU generation perform overall best, they are at times outperformed by CuDNN in the case of column attention at longer sequences with cross-over points depending on the head dimension. Among inference-only backends, SageAttention performs well for row attention and large sequences beyond 16\,k rows. Our reproducible benchmark lays the foundation for future improvements to table-native attention. The self-contained benchmarking and evaluation code is openly available at: https://github.com/SAP-samples/tabular-attention-benchmark

</details>


### [276] [LUCID: Learning Under Confounding for Inference and Discovery in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31315)
*Mohammad Fesanghary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID是一种“体制自适应去混杂层”，先用Marčenko–Pastur谱路由器从数据估计混杂体制，再据此选择去混杂策略，可包裹现有因果发现算法，在多种合成分布外基准上取得最优的时序因果图发现F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列中普遍存在未观测的共同原因（混杂因子），会制造伪相关，使因果发现方法误判为直接边。现有方法缺乏对不同混杂体制的自适应处理能力。

Method: 提出LUCID：（1）用Marčenko–Pastur谱路由器从数据中估计混杂体制；（2）按体制匹配去混杂策略——当谱显示普遍因子混杂时，衰减因子主导的变异，从残差创新中恢复同期(lag-0)结构，并用数据驱动的无边缘零假设校准边选择；（3）可包裹现有发现引擎，非绑定特定算法。

Result: 在涵盖混杂强度与稀疏性、载荷密度、滞后结构、波动动态、边异质性、持续性、间歇性与尾部行为变化的分布外合成基准上，LUCID取得最佳族加权有向、滞后分辨图F1=0.60，较最强基线提升0.19绝对（约46%相对）；相比更宽松的滞后折叠评分优势更明显，且在间歇性与重尾混杂下保持稳健。

Conclusion: LUCID通过体制自适应的去混杂有效缓解未观测共同原因导致的伪关联，并在三种现有发现方法上均带来一致提升；代码、基准生成器与全部实验已开源。

Abstract: Unobserved common causes are pervasive in real-world time series and can induce spurious associations that causal discovery methods mistake for direct edges. We propose LUCID (Learning Under Confounding for Inference and Discovery, a regime-adaptive deconfounding layer that first estimates the confounding regime from data using a Marčenko--Pastur spectral router, then applies a deconfounding strategy matched to that regime. When the spectrum indicates pervasive factor confounding, LUCID attenuates factor-dominated variation and recovers contemporaneous (lag-$0$) structure from the resulting innovations, with edge selection calibrated against a data-driven edge-free null. Rather than being tied to a particular discovery algorithm, it can wrap existing discovery engines; we demonstrate consistent improvements across three such methods. On a diverse synthetic out-of-distribution benchmark spanning changes in confounder strength and sparsity, loading density, lag structure, volatility dynamics, edge heterogeneity, persistence, intermittency, and tail behavior, LUCID achieves the best family-weighted directed, lag-resolved graph $F_1$ ($0.60$), improving over the strongest baseline by $0.19$ absolute ($\approx\!46\%$ relative). Its advantage widens relative to looser lag-collapsed scoring, and remains robust under intermittent and heavy-tailed confounding. Code reproducing the method, the benchmark generators, and every reported experiment is available at https://github.com/bloomberg/causal-ts.

</details>


### [277] [More Sensors Only One Field: Rethinking Continual Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31325)
*Lewei Xie,Haoyu Zhang,Jiajun Zhou,Yulong Chen,Guanxing Chen,Yu-An Huang,Hau-San Wong,Yifan Zhang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STFO，一种将预测知识参数化为共享"场演化算子"的持续时空预测方法，通过观测和查询接口应对传感器布局变化与过程漂移，在多个流式数据集上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续时空预测需在动态演化和传感器网络扩张下支持交通管理与环境监测。传统图式持续学习方法将预测表示绑定到当前传感器布局，传感器扩张会改变已学到的空间关系表示。作者的关键洞察是：传感器扩张改变的是关于过程的可用证据，而非待学习的动力学本身。

Method: STFO将预测知识参数化为共享的场演化算子，通过观测与查询接口处理布局变化：1) 基于归一化坐标的聚合将不规则传感器历史映射到固定潜在网格，无需传感器特定参数即可复用已学空间图；2) 谱描述子总结跨空间尺度的变化，并调节傅里叶传播与注意力，使算子响应适应当前空间态势；3) 基于坐标的解码在传感器位置查询演化后的场，并将空间修正与局部历史预测结合。

Result: 在PEMS-Stream、CA-Stream、AIR-Stream上平均预测性能达到SOTA。STFO-Large相对DOL在PEMS-Stream上平均MAE降低8.4%，在CA-Stream上降低4.7%。

Conclusion: 通过将空间表示与具体传感器布局解耦并建模为场演化算子，STFO有效支持传感器扩张与过程漂移下的持续时空预测，并在多个流式基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Continual spatio-temporal forecasting supports traffic management and environmental monitoring under evolving dynamics and expanding sensor networks. However, conventional graph-based continual learning methods tie forecasting representations to the current sensor layout, so sensor expansion can alter the representation of learned spatial relationships. Our key insight is that sensor expansion changes the evidence available about a process without necessarily changing the dynamics to be learned. We propose STFO (Spatio-Temporal Field Operator), which parameterizes forecasting knowledge as a shared field-evolution operator and handles changing sensor layouts through observation and query interfaces. Normalized coordinate-based aggregation lifts irregular sensor histories onto a fixed latent grid, enabling reuse of learned spatial maps across observation sets without sensor-specific parameters. To accommodate process drift, a spectral descriptor summarizes variation across spatial scales and conditions Fourier propagation and attention to adapt operator responses to the current spatial regime. Coordinate-based decoding queries the evolved field at sensor locations and combines spatial corrections with local-history predictions. Experiments on PEMS-Stream, CA-Stream, and AIR-Stream demonstrate state-of-the-art average forecasting performance. STFO-Large reduces average MAE over DOL by 8.4% on PEMS-Stream and 4.7% on CA-Stream. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xielewei/Spatio-Temporal-Field-Operator.

</details>


### [278] [Bridging Body and Brain: Gene-Driven Morphology--Control Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31329)
*Fu Feng,Ruixiao Shi,Yucheng Xie,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Morphogene紧凑潜变量蓝图，将形态与控制的生成在肢体级别联合条件化，从而实现显式协调；基于此提出GeCode，将形态-控制协同设计转化为在Morphogene空间中的探索，在多种2D/3D任务上超越SOTA并更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形态-控制协同设计方法通常用独立网络分别建模形态设计与控制，仅通过共享任务目标间接耦合，缺乏显式的高层协调。受生物基因协调发育的启发，作者希望引入一个能同时连接"身体"与"大脑"的紧凑表示。

Method: 1) Morphogene：紧凑潜变量蓝图，通过AdaConcat在肢体级别联合条件化形态与控制生成，使其变化同时引发两者的协调改变。2) GeCode：将协同设计表述为Morphogene空间的探索；每个Morphogene锚定一个局部设计区域，探索附近的身体-大脑设计，并通过性能引导更新将锚点移向有前景区域。

Result: 在多种2D和3D协同设计任务上，GeCode一致优于现有SOTA方法，实现显著更快的收敛速度和更高的最终性能。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑的Morphogene表示显式耦合形态与控制，GeCode将协同设计转化为兼顾局部细化与全局探索的搜索过程，同时保持身体-大脑兼容性，验证了显式协调表示的有效性。

Abstract: Morphology--control co-design jointly optimizes an agent's body structure and control policy as an integrated embodied system. However, existing methods typically model morphology design and control with separate networks coupled only indirectly through a shared task objective, limiting explicit high-level coordination. Inspired by natural genes that coordinate biological development, we introduce \textbf{Morphogene}, a compact latent blueprint that bridges an agent's body and brain. Through AdaConcat, Morphogene jointly conditions morphology and control generation at the limb level, allowing its variations to induce coordinated changes in both components. Building on this representation, we propose \textbf{GeCode}, which formulates co-design as exploration in the compact Morphogene space. Each Morphogene anchors a local design region in which nearby body--brain designs are explored, while performance-guided updates move these anchors toward promising regions for more efficient exploration of the broader design space. This process combines local refinement with global exploration while preserving body--brain compatibility. Extensive experiments across diverse 2D and 3D co-design tasks demonstrate that GeCode consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, achieving substantially faster convergence and higher final performance.

</details>


### [279] [Progressive Memory Transformer: Memory-Aware Attention for Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31351)
*Tord Sture Stangeland,Andreas Köhler,Steffen Mæland,Adín Ramíres Rivera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Progressive Memory Transformer (PMT)，通过可写窗口对齐记忆暴露多尺度表示，并设计局部/中程/全局三个独立自监督目标显式强化结构层级，在分类、探测与预测任务上均获优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列同时具有细粒度变化、中程motif和全局属性等多尺度结构，下游任务也对应不同尺度；但现有多数自监督方法仅用实例级对比损失和有限时序邻域监督，未显式利用结构层级。

Method: 提出显式跨三尺度独立强制的学习框架：局部token连续性目标、中程窗口级motif目标、全局序列级一致性目标。为使骨干网络在每个尺度都暴露表示，引入PMT，在Transformer上增加可写、窗口对齐的记忆模块，从而在常规token与序列表示之外提供中程尺度表示。

Result: 在7个UCR/UEA/UCI分类基准、cue-retention探测及预测基准上，PMT在全局、中程、局部尺度均有良好可探测性：低标签（1–5%）分类表现强，多horizon预测具竞争力，并定性与定量证明记忆状态能捕获中程motif。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间序列的多尺度结构层级，并让骨干网络提供对应尺度表示，可有效提升自监督表示质量，兼顾低标签分类与预测任务。

Abstract: Time-series carry structure simultaneously at multiple scales (fine-grained variation, mid-range motifs, and global properties) and downstream tasks operate at correspondingly different scales. Most existing self-supervised learning approaches supervise representations globally via instance-level contrastive losses and limited temporal neighborhood supervision, but do not explicitly exploit the structural hierarchy. We propose a learning framework that explicitly enforces a structural hierarchy across three scales independently: a local objective for token continuity, a mid-range objective for window-level motifs, and a global objective for sequence-level agreement. Realizing this framework requires the backbone to expose a representation at each scale; we introduce \textbf{Progressive Memory Transformer} (PMT), which augments a transformer with writable, window-aligned memory that exposes the mid-range scale alongside the token and sequence-level representations conventional transformers already provide. Across seven UCR/UEA/UCI classification benchmarks, a cue-retention probe, and forecasting benchmarks, PMT learns representations that probe well at the global, mid-range, and local scales---strong low-label classification (1--5\% labels), competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, and quantitative and qualitative evidence that memory states capture mid-range motifs.

</details>


### [280] [Brenier Meets Adversarial Training: Optimal Transport Geometry for Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31363)
*Alireza Abdollahpoorrostam,Ehsan Sharifian,Buse Şen,Marco Cuturi,Daniel Kuhn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带Wasserstein惩罚的DRO，将对手问题重构为传输映射优化，证明最优映射满足循环单调性，并提出多起点粒子上升与输入凸神经网络参数化两种方法保证该性质，实验优于标准对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRO虽为非凸损失下分布偏移提供理论框架，但评估最坏风险困难，且标准对抗训练因逐样本局部优化违反循环单调性、浪费传输成本。

Method: 研究惩罚式DRO，将对手问题重构为将经验样本推向对抗样本的传输映射优化，证明最优映射具循环单调性；提出多起点粒子上升（并行梯度上升+重分配）和以输入凸神经网络梯度参数化对抗映射两种方法。

Result: 在鲁棒回归、图像分类和鲁棒控制实验中，方法持续优于标准对抗训练及最先进基线，在分布偏移下取得更好鲁棒性与泛化。

Conclusion: 循环单调性是设计高效DRO对抗策略的关键，所提方法可有效提升非凸DRO的实用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) provides a principled framework for learning under distribution shift, but its practical use is hindered by the difficulty of evaluating worst-case risks for nonconvex loss functions. We study a penalized DRO formulation in which the adversary may choose any distribution but incurs a Wasserstein penalty for deviating from the empirical distribution. We show that the adversary's problem can be reformulated as an optimization problem over transport maps that push empirical samples to adversarial ones, and we prove that optimal maps are cyclically monotone. We also show that standard adversarial training---based on per-sample local optimization---violates cyclical monotonicity and wastes transport costs unless the adversary is severely restricted. We propose two remedies. First, we introduce multi-start particle ascent, which alternates parallel gradient ascent with reassignment to enforce cyclical monotonicity across samples. Second, we parameterize adversarial maps as gradients of input-convex neural networks, which guarantees cyclical monotonicity by construction. Experiments on robust regression, image classification, and robust control show that our methods consistently outperform standard adversarial training and state-of-the-art baselines, achieving improved robustness and better generalization under distribution shift.

</details>


### [281] [Towards Understanding LLM-Based Log Anomaly Detection: An Empirical Study of Performance, Efficiency, and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31371)
*Bin Li,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统实证分析LLM做日志异常检测时，适应策略、架构、参数规模和量化配置如何影响检测效果、成本与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在日志异常检测上表现良好，但其适应策略、架构和部署配置如何影响检测效果尚不清楚，缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 在三个公开日志数据集上开展系统实证分析，考察不同适应策略、模型架构、参数规模和量化设置，并分析不同扰动水平下的结构、语义与标签噪声鲁棒性。

Result: 不同适应策略性能差异显著；模型规模扩展带来的检测增益因数据集而异；检测精度相近的模型计算成本差异明显；低比特量化在测试配置下基本保持检测性能。

Conclusion: 为LLM日志异常检测的性能、效率与鲁棒性提供实证见解，强调超越传统以准确率为导向的实用考量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising performance in log anomaly detection, yet how their adaptation strategies, architectures, and deployment configurations affect detection effectiveness remains insufficiently understood. To investigate these factors, we conduct a systematic empirical analysis across three public log datasets, examining different adaptation strategies, model architectures, parameter scales, and quantization settings. Our results reveal substantial performance differences across adaptation strategies, while model scaling yields varying detection gains across datasets. We further observe that models with comparable detection accuracy can exhibit markedly different computational costs, and that low-bit quantization largely preserves detection performance in the evaluated configurations. Finally, we examine detection robustness under structural, semantic, and label noise at different perturbation levels. These findings provide empirical insights into the performance, efficiency, and robustness of LLM-based log anomaly detection, highlighting practical considerations beyond conventional accuracy-oriented evaluation.

</details>


### [282] [Differential Attention Unlocks Complementary EEG and Speech Fusion for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31399)
*Philip H. Lee,Shreeram Suresh Chandra,John H. L. Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EmoSpeechBrain多模态情感识别框架，通过差分注意力抑制EEG噪声，实现有效融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朴素融合在EEG-语音多模态情感识别中常不如较强的单模态，原因是EEG伪迹噪声污染了共享表征，因此噪声抑制是有效融合的前提。

Method: EEG编码器采用差分注意力，用两个注意力图之差抵消共享噪声、分离判别性神经活动；基于注意力的门控适配器将两模态对齐到共享空间并加权各自贡献。

Result: 在PME4和EAV数据集上，相比其他SOTA EEG编码器MER准确率最高提升12.9%，超过单模态语音和EEG基线最高13.1%和23.1%。

Conclusion: 一旦抑制EEG噪声，融合可获得朴素组合无法实现的增益。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition (MER) increasingly pairs EEG with speech, treating internal neural signals and external vocal expression as informative views of affect. In practice, naive fusion underperforms the stronger single modality, because EEG artifacts inject noise that corrupts the shared representation. We introduce EmoSpeechBrain, a multimodal framework built on the insight that noise suppression is a precondition for effective fusion. Its EEG encoder uses differential attention, taking the difference between two attention maps to cancel shared noise and isolate discriminative neural activity. An attention-based gating adapter aligns both modalities in a shared space and weights each one's contribution to the prediction. On two datasets - PME4 and EAV, EmoSpeechBrain improves MER accuracy by up to 12.9% over other state-of-the-art (SOTA) EEG encoders, and surpasses unimodal speech and EEG baselines by up to 13.1% and 23.1%. These results show that once EEG noise is suppressed, fusion delivers gains that naive combination cannot.

</details>


### [283] [Decodable In-Context State and Model Output Across Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31401)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文追踪探针准确率、模型输出与引导响应在预训练及后训练checkpoint中的变化，发现探针准确率随Pythia预训练上升，探针引导转向效果随规模增大而增强，但仅凭错误上的可解码性无法证明输出信息被丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明探针能解码模型错误中的上下文绑定，并能通过探针引导修复部分错误。本文希望进一步理解这些现象在训练过程中的演变，并检验相关方法论上的局限。

Method: 追踪公开预训练与后训练checkpoint上的探针准确率、模型输出与引导响应；比较保存分数以区分探针正确但与均匀分布概率不同的错误；用oracle-target steering与保存聚合量对比；在最终状态与候选logits上训练解码器做留出比较；给出信息论反例。

Result: 探针准确率在Pythia预训练中上升；探针引导转向从几乎无全试验收益转变为在两个模型规模上收益更大。保存分数能区分概率低与高于均匀分布的正确候选。Oracle-target引导已能修复许多早期错误，但保存聚合量无法分离目标质量与干预敏感性。在晚期checkpoint错误上，未发现最终状态解码器相对候选logits解码器有优势。

Conclusion: 探针可解码性随预训练提升，引导效果随规模增强；但仅凭错误上的可解码性无法建立输出信息被丢弃的结论，其与下游遗漏的联系仍待研究。

Abstract: Prior work established that a probe can decode an in-context binding on model errors and that probe-guided steering can repair some of them. We follow probe accuracy, model output, and steering response across public pretraining and post-training checkpoints. Probe accuracy rises during Pythia pretraining, while probe-guided steering moves from negligible all-trial benefit to a larger benefit at two model sizes. Saved scores distinguish probe-correct errors with low and above-uniform model probability for the correct candidate. Oracle-target steering already repairs many early errors, but saved aggregates cannot separate target quality from intervention sensitivity. A held-out comparison of decoders trained on the final state or candidate logits finds no detected final-state advantage on late-checkpoint model errors. An information-theoretic counterexample explains why decodability on errors alone cannot establish discarded output information. The connection to downstream omissions remains open.

</details>


### [284] [Evaluating the accuracy of KV cache reuse techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31415)
*Samuel Cestola,Tianxiang Xia,Pengfei Zheng,Weiyan Zheng,Bo Wang,Yi Zhao,Diego Didona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前位置无关KV缓存复用技术的评估方法未能真实反映因复用导致的精度损失，导致效果被高估，且现有数据集缺乏必要的复用动态。为此，作者提出一种无歧义的评估方法，并开发了Boxoffice工具来自动生成具有挑战性复用模式的评估数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置无关KV缓存复用旨在通过跨提示复用chunk级KV缓存来降低检索增强生成的延迟。然而现有评估依赖的测量方式无法忠实捕捉复用带来的精度损失，常常人为夸大报告的效果；同时现有数据集不具备充分评估这类技术所需的复用动态。

Method: 提出一种评估方法论，能够无歧义地测量因KV缓存复用导致的精度损失；并开发了Boxoffice工具，可编程地生成能够触发具有挑战性KV缓存复用模式的评估数据集。

Result: 揭示了当前评估方法对KV缓存复用技术效果的高估问题，以及现有数据集在复用动态方面的不足；所提方法与工具能够更准确地测量精度损失并生成更具挑战性的评估数据。

Conclusion: 为位置无关KV缓存复用技术提供了更严谨的评估基础，有助于避免因评估偏差而高估其有效性，并推动该领域朝着更可靠的评测方向发展。

Abstract: Position-independent KV cache reuse aims to reduce latency in retrieval-augmented generation by reusing chunk-level KV caches across prompts. We show that current evaluations of KV cache reuse techniques rely on measurements that fail to faithfully capture the loss of accuracy attributable to reuse, often artificially inflating the reported effectiveness. We also show that existing datasets do not exhibit the reuse dynamics needed to thoroughly evaluate such techniques. To address these issues, we propose an evaluation methodology that measures this accuracy loss without ambiguity and we introduce Boxoffice, a tool that programmatically generates evaluation datasets that exercise challenging KV cache reuse patterns.

</details>


### [285] [Scaffold: Support Graph Theory Based Sparsification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31466)
*Siddhartha Shankar Das,Sai Karthik Navuluru,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Baris Coskunuzer,Lakshman Tamil,Edoardo Serra,Alex Pothen,Robert Rallo,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scaffold：一种基于支撑图理论的无监督图稀疏化框架，通过联合控制膨胀与拥塞保留通信结构，在19个基准上以10%-50%边达到接近全图GNN性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN依赖图边消息传递，计算和内存开销随图密度急剧增加。现有图稀疏化方法盲目删边会破坏重要通信结构，导致预测性能下降。

Method: 提出Scaffold，基于支撑图理论预条件子的拓扑式无监督稀疏化框架，显式联合控制两个互补结构量：膨胀（被删边绕行路径长度）与拥塞（重路由路径在保留支撑上的集中程度），在保留短通信路径的同时避免结构瓶颈。

Result: 在19个同质与异质基准（小图到大图）上取得最佳综合排名；仅用原图10%-50%的边即恢复或接近全图GNN性能，内存不足全图训练一半，并降低含稀疏化开销的端到端训练时间。

Conclusion: Scaffold是首个采用联合支撑路径膨胀-拥塞准则的可扩展GNN稀疏化框架，有效平衡效率与性能，并已开源。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) rely on message passing over graph edges, making their computational and memory costs strongly dependent on graph density. Graph sparsification offers a natural way to reduce these costs, but removing edges indiscriminately can distort important communication structure and degrade predictive performance. We introduce Scaffold, a topology-based, unsupervised graph sparsification framework derived from support graph theory preconditioners. Scaffold explicitly controls two complementary structural quantities: dilation, which measures the length of rerouting paths induced by removed edges, and congestion, which measures how strongly these rerouted paths concentrate on the retained support. By jointly controlling dilation and congestion, Scaffold preserves short communication paths while avoiding structural bottlenecks. To our knowledge, Scaffold is the first scalable GNN sparsification framework to use a joint supporting-path dilation-congestion criterion. Across 19 homophilic and heterophilic benchmarks spanning small to large graphs, Scaffold achieves the best aggregate rank among the evaluated sparsification and related methods. Using only 10%-50% of the original edges per sparse support, Scaffold recovers or closely approaches full-graph GNN performance while using less than half the memory of full-graph training and reducing end-to-end training time, including sparsification overhead. We provide an open-source software package at https://github.com/siddhartha047/Scaffold.

</details>


### [286] [HySTAR: Anchored Hypergraphs for Stable Credit Assignment in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31531)
*Xinglong Luo,Yuding Zhang,Yuheng Kuang,Shuxuan Yuan,Zhenni Zeng,Weiqiang Zhu,Zhenhai Ji,Zhengning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HySTAR是一个基于MAPPO的框架，通过分离自适应表示学习与时间一致的高阶价值分解基，解决部分可观测与共享奖励下协作多智能体强化学习中的"结构目标漂移"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测和共享奖励的协作多智能体强化学习中，需要将团队结果分配给个体智能体及高阶联盟。MAPPO式critic将联合行为压缩为单一全局价值，而动态重构分组拓扑的critic会随交互或活跃智能体变化改变智能体与联盟到价值组件的映射，这种不一致性被称为结构目标漂移。

Method: HySTAR分离自适应表示学习与时间一致的高阶价值分解基：以重叠稀疏超图作为均匀覆盖的分解支架，用时空编码器表示物理和任务相关交互，并结合时间与结构相关性构造智能体特定优势。

Result: 在SMAC、GRF、Traffic Junction和MPE上一致优于MAPPO式、价值分解和动态分组基线。最难SMAC设置下相对MAPPO提升16.7%、相对HYGMA提升15.6%；GRF六个场景全部第一；Traffic Junction收敛轮次较MAGIC最多减少40.2%；MPE获得最高回合奖励。

Conclusion: 控制拓扑、智能体死亡、邻域和参数分析支持在保持分解支架锚定的同时自适应传播表示的优势。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning under partial observability and shared rewards requires assigning team outcomes to individual agents and high-order coalitions. A MAPPO-style critic compresses joint behavior into one global value, while critics that dynamically reconstruct the grouping topology change the mapping from agents and coalitions to value components as interactions or active agents evolve. We refer to this inconsistency as structural target drift. We introduce HySTAR, a MAPPO-based framework that separates adaptive representation learning from a temporally consistent high-order value-decomposition basis. HySTAR anchors an overlapping sparse hypergraph as a uniformly covered decomposition scaffold, uses a spatiotemporal encoder to represent physical and task-dependent interactions, and combines temporal and structural relevance to construct agent-specific advantages. Experiments on SMAC, GRF, Traffic Junction, and MPE demonstrate consistent improvements over MAPPO-style, value-factorization, and dynamic-grouping baselines. On the hardest SMAC settings, HySTAR achieves relative gains of 16.7\% over MAPPO and 15.6\% over HYGMA, ranks first on all six GRF scenarios, reduces Traffic Junction convergence epochs by up to 40.2\% relative to MAGIC, and obtains the highest MPE episode rewards. Controlled topology, agent-death, neighborhood, and parameter analyses support the benefit of anchoring the decomposition scaffold while adapting the propagated representations.

</details>


### [287] [NEXT: Physics-Informed Neuro-Spectral Exponential Time Differencing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31539)
*Márcio Marques,Leonardo Mendonça,Leonardo M. Moreira,Christian Júnior de Oliveira,Vitor Balestro,Tiago Novello,Daniel Yukimura,Pavel Petrov,Lucas Nissenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NEXT架构，将NeuSA的谱表示与高阶指数积分器结合，通过对刚性线性部分精确积分、非线性部分用神经网络建模，稳定求解刚性PDE的正问题与反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN存在谱偏差和缺乏因果性的问题；NeuSA虽缓解了这两点，但其数值积分在刚性微分方程上不稳定，而这类方程在物理问题中很常见。

Method: NEXT结合NeuSA对PDE解的谱表示与高阶指数时间差分积分器：通过矩阵指数对PDE诱导向量场的线性刚性部分进行精确积分，非线性剩余部分由神经网络建模。

Result: 在一组刚性PDE基准实验中，NEXT保持稳定且精确，而NeuSA数值发散；同时NEXT可应用于反问题，从稀疏数据中学习未知参数或边界条件。

Conclusion: NEXT是一种稳定、精确且适用于刚性问题正/反求解的神经谱架构，代码已开源。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build neural representations of time-dependent PDE solutions, naturally incorporating physics knowledge and observational data, which makes them well suited to both forward and inverse PDE problems. PINNs, however, are known to suffer from spectral bias and lack of causality. Neuro-Spectral Architectures (NeuSA), a recently proposed alternative to PINNs, mitigate both issues, but their numerical integration becomes unstable for stiff differential equations arising in many relevant physical problems. This study proposes Neuro-Spectral Exponential Time Differencing Architectures (NEXT), which combines the spectral representation of the PDE solution in NeuSA with high-order exponential integrators. Within this approach, the linear stiff part of the vector field induced by the PDE is integrated exactly through matrix exponentials, while the possibly nonlinear remainder is modeled by a neural network. The effectiveness of NEXT is verified through benchmark experiments on a set of stiff PDEs, in which NEXT is stable and accurate while NeuSA diverges numerically. It is also shown that NEXT can be applied to inverse problems, where the model has to learn unknown parameters or boundary conditions from sparse data. All code used in this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/marcioh2m/next.git .

</details>


### [288] [BeatGraph: Self-Supervised Heartbeat Graphs for Infant ECG Representations from the Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31546)
*Mohammad Nur Hossain Khan,M. S. Krafczyk,Beverly G. Bolster,Nancy McElwain,Mark A. Hasegawa-Johnson,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BeatGraph，以心跳为表示单元将30秒心电窗口建模为心跳图，在婴儿ECG多任务与跨年龄段迁移上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG基础模型将信号切成定长patch，忽略心脏结构：patch可能切断单个心跳，且每patch内的心跳数随心率变化。这对心率更高、且与成人12导联临床数据差异大的婴儿尤为不利，因此婴儿ECG模型应直接以心跳为推理单元。

Method: BeatGraph以心跳为表示单元，将每个30秒窗口建模为心跳图：共享心跳编码器从波形与心搏间期嵌入每个心跳，带位置编码的Transformer按时间排序心跳，残差图注意力层建模心跳间关系，最终经注意力池化得到窗口嵌入。在新建的未标注婴儿录音语料上以掩码心跳嵌入预测预训练，再针对各任务微调。

Result: 单一骨干支持睡眠-清醒检测、婴儿状态分类、活动来源识别（婴儿或照护者发起）与情感识别，在各任务上宏F1比最强基线提升0.076至0.158。跨年龄段迁移：ZZU-pECG儿科基准（0–14岁）AUROC达0.892，与最佳已发表自监督ECG模型相差0.001；在成人PTB-XL线性评估下与该模型持平，尽管仅用婴儿数据预训练。同时发布首个公开婴儿ECG语料：143名3–11月龄婴儿、3,408小时单通道ECG，含未标注预训练数据、基准任务与受试者级划分。

Conclusion: 以心跳为单位的图表示可有效适配婴儿ECG并泛化至儿科与成人数据，同时公开首个家庭、教室与实验室场景采集的婴儿ECG语料及基准。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) foundation models typically tokenize the signal into fixed-length patches that ignore cardiac structure, so a patch may split a heartbeat and the number of beats in each patch shifts with heart rate. This matters most for infants, whose heart rates are higher and whose ECG differs from the adult, clinic-recorded 12-lead data these models are built on. A model for infant ECG should therefore reason about heartbeats directly rather than recover them from arbitrary patches. We propose BeatGraph, which makes the heartbeat its unit of representation, modeling each 30-second window as a graph of beats. A shared beat encoder embeds each heartbeat from its waveform and inter-beat intervals, a Transformer with positional encoding orders the beats in time, and residual graph attention layers relate every beat to every other before attention pooling yields a window embedding. We pretrain BeatGraph on our new corpus of unlabeled infant recordings by predicting masked-beat embeddings, then fine-tune it for each task. One backbone supports sleep-wake detection, infant-state classification, activity-source identification (infant- or caregiver-initiated movement), and affect recognition, improving macro-F1 over the strongest baseline on each task by 0.076 to 0.158. It also transfers across age groups, reaching 0.892 AUROC on the ZZU-pECG pediatric benchmark (ages 0 to 14), within 0.001 of the best published self-supervised ECG model, and matching that model under linear evaluation on the adult PTB-XL benchmark despite infant-only pretraining. Finally, to our knowledge, we release the first public infant ECG corpus collected in homes, classrooms, and laboratory settings with state and affect labels. It contains 3,408 hours of single-channel ECG from 143 infants aged 3 to 11 months, with unlabeled pretraining data, benchmark tasks, and subject-level splits.

</details>


### [289] [Online Learning via Learned Latent Bayesian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31559)
*Guy Gerson,Tomer Raviv,Nir Shlezinger,Tirza Routtenberg,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AURA将在线学习建模为贝叶斯状态跟踪，通过离线元学习低维潜状态空间模型并在潜空间用扩展卡尔曼滤波实现高效单步在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳环境下在线学习需在严格计算约束下快速适应流式数据。贝叶斯滤波是原则性方法，但直接用于现代深度模型因参数空间高维而计算不可行，现有方法只能依赖受限近似或手工设计的低维子空间。

Method: 提出AURA元学习框架：离线学习一个低维潜状态空间模型，刻画分布偏移下最优模型参数的演化；在线适应时在该潜空间用扩展卡尔曼滤波进行单步更新，并通过学习到的提升映射重建完整模型参数，兼顾效率与表达能力。

Result: 在时变信道下神经无线接收器的在线适应以及非平稳图像分类任务上，AURA在适应速度、准确率和计算效率上均显著优于现有的在线学习和贝叶斯滤波基线。

Conclusion: 适应感知的潜几何结构对高维模型中有效的贝叶斯在线学习有益，验证了学习合适低维动力学表示是解决该瓶颈的关键。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary environments requires models to adapt rapidly from streaming data under strict computational constraints. A principled approach casts online learning as Bayesian state tracking, where model parameters are updated sequentially via Bayesian filtering. However, applying Bayesian filters directly to modern deep models is computationally prohibitive due to the high dimensionality of parameter space, forcing existing methods to rely on restrictive approximations or manually designed low-dimensional subspaces. In this work, we identify the absence of a suitable low-dimensional dynamical representation as the core bottleneck in Bayesian filtering-based online learning. Accordingly, we propose Adaptive Update through Representation Adaptation (AURA), a meta-learning framework that learns offline a low-dimensional latent state-space model governing the evolution of optimal model parameters under distribution shift. Online adaptation is then performed via extended Kalman filtering in this learned latent space followed by reconstruction of the full model parameters through a learned lifting map, enabling efficient single-step online adaptation while preserving model expressiveness. Evaluated on online adaptation of neural wireless receivers under time-varying channels and on non-stationary image classification, AURA shows substantial improvements in adaptation speed, accuracy, and computational efficiency over existing online learning and Bayesian filtering baselines, demonstrating that an adaptation-aware latent geometry is beneficial for effective Bayesian online learning in high-dimensional models.

</details>


### [290] [Generalization behavior of OPTQ and the role of regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31560)
*Erin George,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从泛化角度分析OPTQ及其随机变体stochastic OPTQ的量化误差，推导期望平方误差界，并提出正则化参数λ的新选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型神经网络需通过量化压缩权重，OPTQ在标定集上最小化平方量化误差，但缺乏泛化性能的理论保证与λ选择的指导。

Method: 在泛化框架下研究OPTQ与stochastic OPTQ，推导测试点从固定分布抽取时的期望平方误差界，得到两个结果：一个将泛化误差与独立同分布标定集上的误差关联；另一个对足够良好的分布给出与标定集无关的stochastic OPTQ泛化误差界，二者均突出λ的作用。

Result: 两个泛化误差界均依赖正则化项λ，据此提出λ的新选择建议，实验表明该选择优于文献中的先前建议。

Conclusion: 正则化参数λ在泛化误差界中起关键作用，基于理论的λ选择在实践中表现更优。

Abstract: Large neural networks can be compressed by rounding or "quantizing" their weights to numbers that admit representations with fewer bits. One algorithm for quantization, OPTQ, progressively quantizes the weights of a neural network so that the squared quantization error on a specified calibration dataset is as small as possible. We study the performance of OPTQ and a variant algorithm, stochastic OPTQ, in a generalization setting and derive bounds for the expected squared error accrued by the algorithm when a test point is drawn from a fixed distribution. We prove two results. One result relates the generalization error to the error on a calibration dataset comprising independent samples from the same distribution as the test distribution. The other result bounds the generalization error of stochastic OPTQ for all sufficiently nice distributions, regardless of the calibration dataset. In both of these results, the regularization term $λ$ plays an important role. We use insights from these results to make a new recommendation for the choice of $λ$ and see that this choice of $λ$ preforms favorably in experiments when compared to prior recommendations in the literature.

</details>


### [291] [Weight Pair Encoding: Inducing a Smaller Grammar in Neural Network Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31564)
*Irene Tallini,Daniele Solombrino,Alberto Cazzaniga,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeightPE通过在直通估计器内嵌入有损Re-Pair压缩器，微调神经网络权重以适配更小的语法（grammar），在ViT上显著压缩权重而精度损失较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法压缩比固定大小条目的扁平码本更具优势（可变长模式、层次化复用），且此前语法大小从未被用作网络权重的显式训练目标。论文旨在探索能否让权重显式微调以适配更小的语法。

Method: 将网络int8权值展平为单一字符串，在全局L2预算内使近似匹配的Re-Pair模式精确相等；把有损Re-Pair压缩器放入直通估计器中，网络用重写后的权重计算并反向传播训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上微调的ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16的MLP权重上，WeightPE生成的Re-Pair语法大小分别是等效int8 QAT的0.43x和0.38x，精度分别损失1.9和1.1个百分点；该趋势在LZ78、SEQUITUR等未参与微调的压缩器上同样成立。

Conclusion: 这是首次将语法大小用作网络权重的显式训练目标，证明权重可被微调以适配更小的语法并泛化到其他压缩器。

Abstract: We show that neural network weights can be explicilty fintuned to admit a smaller grammar. Weight Pair Encoding (WeightPE) does so by placing a lossy Re-Pair compressor inside a straight-through estimator. The int8 weights of the network are flattened into one string, and near-matching Re-Pair patterns are made exactly equal within a global L2 budget. The network computes with the rewritten weights and trains through them with a straight-through estimator. Unlike a flat codebook of fixed-size entries, a grammar offers variable-length patterns and reuses them hierarchically inside larger ones. On the MLP weights of ViT-B/16 and ViT-L/16 finetuned on CIFAR-10, WeightPE produces a Re-Pair grammar 0.43x and 0.38x the size of the one produced by an equivalent int8 QAT run, at a cost of 1.9 and 1.1 accuracy points. The trend extends to different grammar compressors (LZ78, SEQUITUR), over which the networks has not be finetuned against. To our knowledge, this is the first time grammar size has been used as an explicit training objective for network weights.

</details>


### [292] [Trust Guided Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31586)
*Chainesh Gautam,Raghuram Bharadwaj Diddigi,Chandramouli Kamanchi,Pankaj Dayama,Sumanta Mukherjee,Kameshwaran Sampath*

Main category: cs.LG
